"""Optional shadow diagnostics over existing summaries and Phase 2A arithmetic.
No detector execution, production card mutation, routing or shadow confidence.
"""
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import asdict
from math import fsum, isclose
from hashlib import sha256
import re
# local imports
from evidence_lineage import Addition, Family, Kind, LineageError, REGISTRY, build_lineage, lineage_to_json
from marginal_corroboration import Contribution, allocate_marginal
from scorecard_integrity import COMPONENT_FIELDS, reconstruct_scorecard_calculation

# Exact aliases from derive_signals/compute_scorecard, not guessed detector edges.
_SIGNAL_GROUPS = {
    'metadata':('metadata_synthetic','metadata_enhancement','metadata_missing','metadata_clean','metadata_camera_like','provenance_state'),
    'osint':('osint_ai_context','osint_clean'),
    'sensor_origin':('camera_like_support','sensor_trace_inconsistency'),
    'cfa_demosaic':('cfa_camera_like_support','cfa_inconsistency'),
    'compression_history':('compression_consistent','compression_inconsistency','double_jpeg_suspect','block_history_anomaly'),
    'subject_boundary':('subject_boundary_strong','subject_boundary_medium'),
    'subject_texture_consistency':('subject_texture_strong','subject_texture_moderate'),
    'subject_process_asymmetry':('subject_process_asymmetry_strong','subject_process_asymmetry_moderate','region_geometry_unreliable'),
    'subject_material_realism':('material_realism_strong','material_realism_medium','material_realism_weak','material_realism_low'),
    'region_masks':('segmentation_fragmented','segmentation_clean','segmentation_natural','person_mask_reliable','subject_area_ratio','boundary_support_ratio','boundary_support_low','boundary_reliability_score'),
    'segmentation_alignment':('segmentation_boundary_score','segmentation_boundary_strong','segmentation_boundary_medium'),
    'fft':('fft_structured','fft_score','peak_periodicity','peak_symmetry','peak_count','axis_bias'),
    'patch_similarity':('patch_structured','patch_score','repetition_score','coverage_ratio','pair_count','separated_pair_count','mean_similarity','max_similarity'),
    'boundary':('strong_local_edit','fragmented_local_edit'),
    'cross_signal':('strong_cross',),
}
SIGNAL_SOURCES = {name:(section,) for section,names in _SIGNAL_GROUPS.items() for name in names}
SIGNAL_SOURCES.update(provenance_clean=('metadata','osint'),local_signals_weak=('ela','noise','cross_signal','boundary'))


def _operation_sources(reason, previous, nodes, ds):
    sources=set()
    unrecognized=[]
    for name in re.findall(r'\bds\.([a-zA-Z_]+)',reason):
        if name not in SIGNAL_SOURCES:
            unrecognized.append(name)
        sources.update(SIGNAL_SOURCES.get(name,()))
    sources.update(re.findall(r'\bev\.([a-zA-Z_]+)',reason))
    if 'ctx.' in reason or 'natural_scene_risk' in reason:
        sources.add('scene_context')
    if 'asym_strong' in reason or 'asym_medium' in reason:
        sources.add('subject_process_asymmetry')
    if 'boundary_stack' in reason:
        sources.update(('boundary','cross_signal'))
        if 'boundary_stack_strong' in reason:
            sources.add('fft')
        sources.update(s for s,flag in (('subject_boundary','subject_boundary_strong'),('segmentation_alignment','segmentation_boundary_strong')) if getattr(ds,flag,False))
    if re.search(r'(?<![\w.])boundary_(strong|medium)\b',reason):
        # Actual OR members; don't invent a missing second observation.
        strength='strong' if re.search(r'(?<![\w.])boundary_strong\b',reason) else 'medium'
        sources.update(s for s,flag in (('subject_boundary','subject_boundary_'+strength),('segmentation_alignment','segmentation_boundary_'+strength)) if getattr(ds,flag,False))
    if 'patch_spatial_' in reason:
        sources.add('patch_spatial')
    if 'camera_benign' in reason:
        sources.update(('metadata','sensor_origin'))
    if 'clone_candidate' in reason:
        sources.add('patch_similarity')
    if reason in {'boundary_reliability','boundary_reliability_caution'}:
        sources.add('region_masks')
    if reason in {'region_geometry_reliability','region_geometry_caution'}:
        sources.add('subject_process_asymmetry')
    if reason.startswith('photo_context_'):
        sources.update(('metadata','scene_context'))
    component=None
    if reason=='local_support_conversion': component='local_edit_support_score'
    elif reason=='measured_camera_caution' or 'card.camera_counter_support' in reason: component='camera_counter_support'
    elif 'card.synthetic_boost' in reason: component='synthetic_boost'
    elif reason=='clone_floor': component='clone_support_score'
    if component:
        for row in previous:
            if row['field']==component:
                sources.update(s.removeprefix('evidence:') for s in row['evidence_ids'])
    # These can be absence conditions or negative OR alternatives rather than
    # support. Keep the condition separately; never manufacture their ancestry.
    ids=tuple(sorted('evidence:'+s for s in sources if 'evidence:'+s in nodes and nodes['evidence:'+s].available))
    return ids,tuple(unrecognized)


def _operation_records(calc, nodes, ds):
    rows=[]
    for index,op in enumerate(calc['component_operations']):
        ids,unknown=_operation_sources(op['reason'],rows,nodes,ds)
        # Contribution after later attenuation of this same component. This is
        # not an additive attribution of nonlinear floors or Q to a detector.
        factor=1.0
        later_floor=False
        for later in calc['component_operations'][index+1:]:
            if later['field']==op['field']:
                if later['operation']=='multiply': factor*=later['operand']
                elif later['operation']=='maximum': later_floor=True
        stable_rule_id=sha256(op['reason'].encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()[:12]
        rows.append({'contribution_id':f'component:{op["field"]}:{op["operation"]}:{stable_rule_id}',
            'ledger_index':index,
            'field':op['field'],'operation':op['operation'],'raw_contribution':op.get('raw_operand', op['operand']),
            'reliability_adjusted_contribution':op['operand']*factor if op['operation']=='add' and not later_floor else None,
            'later_component_multiplier':factor,'nonlinear_floor_follows':later_floor,
            'evidence_ids':ids,'measurement_ancestry':tuple(sorted({a for key in ids for a in nodes[key].measurement_ancestry})),
            'condition':op['reason'],'unknown_signal_aliases':unknown,
            'control_dependencies':('evidence:region_masks',) if op['reason'] in {'boundary_reliability','boundary_reliability_caution'} else (),
            'diagnostic_contribution':op['operand'],'difference':op['operand']-op.get('raw_operand', op['operand']),
            'reason':('Production numerical restatement suppression already applied; no shadow reallocation.' if 'numerical_allocation' in op
                      else 'Existing explicit arithmetic/control policy retained; not another independent evidence credit.')})
    if len({r['contribution_id'] for r in rows})!=len(rows):
        raise LineageError('Duplicate component-rule identifier; review operation lineage.')
    return rows


def _corroboration(ids, nodes):
    selected=[nodes[key] for key in sorted(set(ids)) if key in nodes and nodes[key].available]
    measurements={a for r in selected for a in r.measurement_ancestry if a!='measurement:selected_mask_geometry'}
    shared=defaultdict(list)
    overlaps=defaultdict(list)
    for r in selected:
        for a in r.measurement_ancestry:
            if a!='measurement:selected_mask_geometry': shared[a].append(r.evidence_id)
        for group in r.overlap_groups: overlaps[group].append(r.evidence_id)
    families=sorted({r.family.value for r in selected if r.family not in {Family.GEOMETRY,Family.CONTEXT}})
    return {'supporting_indicator_count':len(selected),'distinct_measurement_ancestries':sorted(measurements),
        'measurement_ancestry_count':len(measurements),'distinct_measurement_families':families,
        'shared_ancestry':{k:v for k,v in sorted(shared.items()) if len(v)>1},
        'overlapping_feature_groups':{k:v for k,v in sorted(overlaps.items()) if len(v)>1},
        'derived_or_composite_indicators':[r.evidence_id for r in selected if r.kind!=Kind.PRIMARY],
        'missing_required_ancestry':{r.evidence_id:list(r.missing_dependencies) for r in selected if r.missing_dependencies},
        'only_one_measurement_family':len(families)==1,
        'independent_observation_count':None,
        'interpretation':'Distinct extractions/families are descriptive, not a statistical independence count.'}


def _gate_diagnostics(sections, ds, nodes, calc):
    strong=[]
    mat=sections.get('subject_material_realism')
    if mat and mat.score is not None and mat.score>=.58: strong.append('evidence:subject_material_realism')
    for key,pattern in (('subject_process_asymmetry','subject_process_asymmetry_strong'),('boundary','possible_foreground_background_processing_mismatch'),('cross_signal','strong_hotspot_convergence')):
        if (section:=sections.get(key)) and section.pattern==pattern: strong.append('evidence:'+key)
    boundary=['evidence:'+key for key in ('boundary','boundary_focus','subject_boundary','segmentation_alignment')
        if (s:=sections.get(key)) and s.status in {'medium','strong'} and s.polarity=='suspicious']
    groups={
        'determine_confidence.strong_signals':strong,
        '_boundary_corroborated':boundary,
        'suspicious_domain_bonus':['evidence:'+r['section'] for r in calc['base_evidence'] if r['polarity']=='suspicious' and r.get('credited_weight', r['weight'])>0],
        'synthetic_material_asymmetry_conjunction':['evidence:'+k for k,f in (('subject_material_realism','material_realism_strong'),('subject_process_asymmetry','subject_process_asymmetry_strong')) if getattr(ds,f,False)],
        'synthetic_fft_patch_conjunction':['evidence:'+k for k,f in (('fft','fft_structured'),('patch_similarity','patch_structured')) if getattr(ds,f,False)],
        'camera_convergence_bonus':['evidence:'+k for k,f in (('metadata','metadata_camera_like'),('sensor_origin','camera_like_support'),('cfa_demosaic','cfa_camera_like_support')) if getattr(ds,f,False)],
        'synthetic_supported_by_other_domains':['evidence:'+k for k,f in (('metadata','metadata_synthetic'),('osint','osint_ai_context'),('subject_material_realism','material_realism_strong')) if getattr(ds,f,False)],
    }
    return {name:{**_corroboration(ids,nodes),
        'production_indicator_ids':ids,'production_indicator_count':len(ids),
        'production_gate_changed':False,
        'ancestry_review_recommended':bool(_corroboration(ids,nodes)['shared_ancestry'] or _corroboration(ids,nodes)['overlapping_feature_groups']),
        'counterfactual_gate_result':None,
        'limitation':'Gate can reuse ancestors or related feature definitions. No replacement threshold or independence test is defined.'}
        for name,ids in sorted(groups.items())}


def _aggregates(calc, allocated):
    bases={}
    for polarity in ('suspicious','benign'):
        selected=[r for r in allocated if r['polarity']==polarity and r['diagnostic_contribution']>0]
        domains={r['domain'] for r in selected if r['domain']}
        bases[polarity]=fsum(r['diagnostic_contribution'] for r in selected)+max(0,len(domains)-1)*calc['domain_bonus']
    values=calc['final_contributions']; t=calc['aggregate_transforms']
    q=fsum([bases['suspicious'],*(values[n] for n in ('synthetic_boost','edit_boost','provenance_boost','sensor_boost','compression_boost'))])
    return {'suspicious_score':max(0,q*t['camera_multiplier']-values['caution_penalty'])*t['geometry_multiplier']*t['photo_context_multiplier'],
        'benign_score':bases['benign'],'base_suspicious':bases['suspicious'],'base_benign':bases['benign']}


def build_dependency_analysis(sections, card, ds, *, context=None, hypotheses=(), experimental=None):
    """Pure optional sidecar. Existing multi-role component policy stays fixed.

    Default: structural restatement rules only. No current audited detector is a
    declared pure restatement: all real children add relationships, localization
    or interpretation. Thus valid baseline totals ordinarily remain identical.
    """
    if not card.is_valid:
        return {'mode':'shadow','lineage_version':'2b2','status':'unavailable',
            'production_score_unchanged':True,'limitations':['Production scorecard is invalid; shadow aggregation withheld.']}
    calc=card.calculation
    replay=reconstruct_scorecard_calculation(calc)
    if any(not isclose(getattr(card,k),replay[k],rel_tol=1e-12,abs_tol=1e-12) for k in (*COMPONENT_FIELDS,'suspicious_score','benign_score')):
        raise LineageError('Production card differs from its calculation; shadow aggregation withheld.')
    records=build_lineage(sections,context=context)
    nodes={r.evidence_id:r for r in records}
    contributions=[Contribution('base:'+r['section'],('evidence:'+r['section'],),'base_'+r['polarity'],
        r.get('credited_weight', r['weight']),r['polarity'],nodes['evidence:'+r['section']].hypotheses,r['domain']) for r in calc['base_evidence']]
    allocated=allocate_marginal(contributions,records,experimental=experimental)
    operations=_operation_records(calc,nodes,ds)
    unknown_operations=[r['contribution_id'] for r in operations if r['unknown_signal_aliases'] or
        (r['operation']=='add' and r['raw_contribution']>0 and not r['evidence_ids'] and not r['control_dependencies'])]
    unknown_base=[c.contribution_id for c in contributions if any(not nodes[e].known for e in c.evidence_ids)]
    aggregates=_aggregates(calc,allocated)
    families={}
    for family in Family:
        rows=[r for r in allocated if any(nodes[e].family==family for e in r['evidence_ids'])]
        if rows:
            families[family.value]={
                'evidence_ids':sorted({e for r in rows for e in r['evidence_ids']}),
                'existing_suspicious_base':fsum(r['existing_contribution'] for r in rows if r['polarity']=='suspicious'),
                'shadow_suspicious_base':fsum(r['diagnostic_contribution'] for r in rows if r['polarity']=='suspicious'),
                'existing_benign_base':fsum(r['existing_contribution'] for r in rows if r['polarity']=='benign'),
                'shadow_benign_base':fsum(r['diagnostic_contribution'] for r in rows if r['polarity']=='benign'),
                'pending_incremental_credit_ids':[r['contribution_id'] for r in rows if r['requires_empirical_validation']],
                'component_operation_ids':[r['contribution_id'] for r in operations if any(nodes[e].family==family for e in r['evidence_ids'])],
                'component_attribution_note':'Operation references can span families; do not sum mixed-family references as disjoint credits.',
            }
    role_map={}
    for h in hypotheses:
        role_map[h.hypothesis_id]={role:list(getattr(h,role)) for role in ('supporting_evidence','contradicting_evidence','moderating_evidence','contextual_evidence')}
    scored_ids=['evidence:'+r['section'] for r in calc['base_evidence'] if r.get('credited_weight', r['weight'])>0]
    return {'mode':'shadow','lineage_version':'2b2','status':'partial' if unknown_operations or unknown_base else 'complete','production_score_unchanged':True,
        'policy':'structural_restatement_only' if experimental is None else 'experimental:'+experimental.name,
        'lineage':lineage_to_json(records),'families':families,'contributions':allocated,
        'component_lineage':operations,'production_calculation_version':calc['calculation_version'],
        'lineage_coverage':{'scoring_indicator_count':len(contributions),
            'known_scoring_indicator_count':sum(all(nodes[e].known for e in c.evidence_ids) for c in contributions),
            'unknown_base_credit_ids':unknown_base,
            'unknown_component_rule_ids':unknown_operations},
        'domain_accounting':{'production_suspicious_bonus':calc['base_suspicious']-fsum(r.get('credited_weight', r['weight']) for r in calc['base_evidence'] if r['polarity']=='suspicious'),
            'shadow_suspicious_bonus':aggregates['base_suspicious']-fsum(r['diagnostic_contribution'] for r in allocated if r['polarity']=='suspicious'),
            'policy':'Existing 0.25 per additional credited domain retained; families are not substituted for domains.'},
        'shared_ancestry':_corroboration(scored_ids,nodes),
        'corroboration_gates':_gate_diagnostics(sections,ds,nodes,calc),
        'hypothesis_evidence_roles':role_map,'shadow_aggregates':aggregates,
        'comparison':{key:{'production':getattr(card,key),'shadow':aggregates[key],
            'difference':aggregates[key]-getattr(card,key)} for key in ('suspicious_score','benign_score')},
        'limitations':[
            'Shadow totals have no confidence tier, hypothesis selection or probability interpretation.',
            'Measurement ancestry does not establish statistical independence or forensic redundancy.',
            'Added localization/interpretation has no calibrated marginal weight; existing credit is provisional.',
            'Existing component synergies, camera moderation, penalties and domain bonus policy remain fixed. This is not a full causal ablation.',
            'Measurement IDs describe feature extractions; overlapping helper code and masks are recorded separately.',
            'The morphology segmentation stage is not scored in normal summary synthesis; its snapshot alias is region_masks.',
        ]}


def safe_dependency_analysis(*args, **kwargs):
    """An optional diagnostic failure cannot change the authoritative decision."""
    try:
        return build_dependency_analysis(*args,**kwargs)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {'mode':'shadow','lineage_version':'2b2','status':'error','production_score_unchanged':True,
            'errors':[f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'],'shadow_aggregates':{},
            'limitations':['Dependency diagnostics failed; no shadow conclusion or score is available.']}

--- End of evidence_dependency_analysis.py ---

--- Start of evidence_lineage.py ---
# evidence_lineage.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images

"""Versioned, immutable measurement ancestry. A family is not independence.
Registry follows the Phase 2B.1 code audit. Measurement IDs denote distinguishable
feature extractions, not statistically independent samples or sensor attribution.
"""
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from enum import Enum
from types import MappingProxyType

class LineageError(ValueError):
    pass

class Family(str, Enum):
    PROVENANCE = 'provenance'
    PROCESSING = 'processing_residual'
    SPECTRAL = 'spectral'
    RECURRENCE = 'patch_recurrence'
    SURFACE = 'subject_surface'
    ACQUISITION = 'camera_pipeline'
    COMPRESSION = 'compression_history'
    EDGE = 'intrinsic_edge_transition'
    GEOMETRY = 'geometry'
    CONTEXT = 'analyst_context'
    UNKNOWN = 'unknown'

class Kind(str, Enum):
    PRIMARY = 'primary'
    DERIVED = 'derived'
    COMPOSITE = 'composite'

class Addition(str, Enum):
    OBSERVATION = 'new_measurement'
    LOCALIZATION = 'localization'
    RELATIONSHIP = 'cross_measurement_relationship'
    INTERPRETATION = 'interpretation'
    RESTATEMENT = 'restatement'
    CONTROL = 'reliability_or_context'

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvidenceSpec:
    key: str
    detector: str
    family: Family
    measurements: tuple[str, ...]
    parents: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    optional_parents: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    hypotheses: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    kind: Kind = Kind.PRIMARY
    addition: Addition = Addition.OBSERVATION
    overlap_groups: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not isinstance(self.key,str) or not self.key or not isinstance(self.detector,str) or not self.detector:
            raise LineageError('Evidence key and source detector must be nonempty text.')
        for name in ('measurements','parents','optional_parents','hypotheses','overlap_groups'):
            values=getattr(self,name)
            if type(values) is not tuple or any(not isinstance(v,str) or not v for v in values) or len(set(values))!=len(values):
                raise LineageError('Evidence collections must be immutable unique text tuples: '+name)
        if self.addition==Addition.RESTATEMENT and self.measurements:
            raise LineageError('A pure restatement cannot declare a new measurement.')

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LineageRecord:
    evidence_id: str
    source_detector: str
    family: Family
    measurement_ancestry: tuple[str, ...]
    direct_dependencies: tuple[str, ...]
    optional_dependencies: tuple[str, ...]
    missing_dependencies: tuple[str, ...]
    hypotheses: tuple[str, ...]
    status: str
    available: bool
    scoring_available: bool
    kind: Kind
    addition: Addition
    overlap_groups: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    known: bool = True

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not isinstance(self.evidence_id,str) or not self.evidence_id.startswith('evidence:') or not isinstance(self.source_detector,str) or not isinstance(self.status,str):
            raise LineageError('Lineage identity, source and status must be text.')
        if not isinstance(self.family,Family) or not isinstance(self.kind,Kind) or not isinstance(self.addition,Addition):
            raise LineageError('Record categories must use lineage enums.')
        for name in ('measurement_ancestry','direct_dependencies','optional_dependencies','missing_dependencies','hypotheses','overlap_groups'):
            values=getattr(self,name)
            if type(values) is not tuple or any(not isinstance(v,str) for v in values):
                raise LineageError('Lineage records cannot retain mutable payload collections.')
        if any(type(getattr(self,name)) is not bool for name in ('available','scoring_available','known')):
            raise LineageError('Availability flags must be booleans.')


SYN = ('synthetic_origin',)
EDIT = ('localized_editing',)
CAM = ('camera_acquisition',)
SYN_EDIT = SYN + EDIT
UNAVAILABLE = frozenset({'not_run', 'unavailable', 'skipped', 'failed', 'error'})
ACTIVE = frozenset({'weak', 'medium', 'strong'})

# Masks are required geometric inputs of regional consumers. They are controls,
# not independent evidence of generation. Optional parents record actual payload
# reuse without requiring parents the detector can run without.
_SPECS = (
    EvidenceSpec('metadata','exiftool_backend.analyze_file_with_exiftool',Family.PROVENANCE,('embedded_assertions',),hypotheses=SYN+CAM,overlap_groups=('provenance_assertion',)),
    EvidenceSpec('osint','summary_sections.build_osint_section',Family.PROVENANCE,('reviewed_source_context',),hypotheses=SYN,overlap_groups=('provenance_assertion',)),
    EvidenceSpec('ela','vf_ela.run_ela',Family.PROCESSING,('ela_recompression_difference',),hypotheses=EDIT,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies',)),
    EvidenceSpec('noise','vf_noise.run_noise_analysis',Family.PROCESSING,('luminance_residual',),hypotheses=EDIT,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies','highpass_features')),
    EvidenceSpec('cross_signal','vf_cross_signal.compute_cross_signal_payload',Family.PROCESSING,(),('ela','noise'),hypotheses=EDIT,kind=Kind.COMPOSITE,addition=Addition.RELATIONSHIP,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies',)),
    EvidenceSpec('boundary','vf_cross_signal.classify_boundary_convergence',Family.PROCESSING,(),('ela','noise'),hypotheses=EDIT,kind=Kind.COMPOSITE,addition=Addition.INTERPRETATION,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies','contour_interpretation')),
    EvidenceSpec('boundary_focus','vf_boundary_focus.run_boundary_focus_analysis',Family.PROCESSING,(),('ela','noise','region_masks'),hypotheses=EDIT,kind=Kind.COMPOSITE,addition=Addition.LOCALIZATION,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('segmentation_alignment','vf_segmentation_alignment.compute_segmentation_boundary_alignment',Family.PROCESSING,(),('cross_signal','region_masks'),hypotheses=EDIT,kind=Kind.COMPOSITE,addition=Addition.LOCALIZATION,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('fft','vf_fft.run_fft_analysis',Family.SPECTRAL,('global_spectral_features',),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,overlap_groups=('spectral_feature_code',)),
    EvidenceSpec('region_fft','vf_region_fft.run_region_fft_analysis',Family.SPECTRAL,('regional_spectral_features',),('region_masks',),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,kind=Kind.DERIVED,addition=Addition.LOCALIZATION,overlap_groups=('spectral_feature_code','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('patch_similarity','vf_patch_similarity.run_patch_similarity_analysis',Family.RECURRENCE,('retained_patch_matches',),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,overlap_groups=('texture_recurrence',)),
    EvidenceSpec('patch_spatial','vf_patch_spatial.run_patch_spatial_analysis',Family.RECURRENCE,(),('patch_similarity','region_masks'),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,kind=Kind.DERIVED,addition=Addition.LOCALIZATION,overlap_groups=('texture_recurrence','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('subject_texture_consistency','vf_subject_texture_consistency.run_subject_texture_consistency_analysis',Family.SURFACE,('masked_hybrid_texture_descriptors',),('region_masks',),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,overlap_groups=('texture_recurrence','surface_feature_definitions','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('subject_process_asymmetry','vf_subject_process_asymmetry.run_subject_process_asymmetry_analysis',Family.SURFACE,('regional_patch_statistics',),('region_masks',),('subject_texture_consistency','region_fft'),SYN_EDIT,Kind.COMPOSITE,Addition.INTERPRETATION,('surface_feature_definitions','highpass_features','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('subject_material_realism','vf_subject_material_realism.run_subject_material_realism_analysis',Family.SURFACE,('subject_material_patch_statistics',),('region_masks',),hypotheses=SYN_EDIT,kind=Kind.DERIVED,addition=Addition.INTERPRETATION,overlap_groups=('surface_feature_definitions','highpass_features','selected_geometry')),
    EvidenceSpec('sensor_origin','vf_sensor_origin.run_sensor_origin_analysis',Family.ACQUISITION,('luminance_phase_residual',),hypotheses=CAM+EDIT,overlap_groups=('highpass_features','acquisition_phase_features')),
    EvidenceSpec('cfa_demosaic','vf_cfa_demosaic.run_cfa_demosaic_analysis',Family.ACQUISITION,('chroma_phase_directionality',),hypotheses=CAM+EDIT,overlap_groups=('highpass_features','acquisition_phase_features')),
    EvidenceSpec('compression_history','vf_compression_history.run_compression_history_analysis',Family.COMPRESSION,('aligned_offgrid_boundary_energy',),hypotheses=EDIT+CAM,overlap_groups=('processing_anomalies',)),
    EvidenceSpec('subject_boundary','vf_subject_boundary.run_subject_boundary_analysis',Family.EDGE,('intrinsic_edge_ring_features',),hypotheses=EDIT,overlap_groups=('highpass_features','image_gradients')),
    EvidenceSpec('region_masks','vf_person_subject_mask.derive_region_masks_from_person_mask',Family.GEOMETRY,('selected_mask_geometry',),kind=Kind.DERIVED,addition=Addition.CONTROL,overlap_groups=('selected_geometry',)),
    EvidenceSpec('scene_context','hypothesis_engine.build_context',Family.CONTEXT,(),addition=Addition.CONTROL),
)

def validate_registry(specs):
    records = tuple(specs)
    mapping = {s.key: s for s in records}
    if len(mapping) != len(records):
        raise LineageError('Duplicate evidence registration.')
    for s in records:
        if not isinstance(s.family,Family) or not isinstance(s.kind,Kind) or not isinstance(s.addition,Addition):
            raise LineageError('Lineage category must be a declared enum.')
        if not s.key or any(p not in mapping for p in s.parents+s.optional_parents):
            raise LineageError('Empty identifier or unregistered parent.')
        if len(set(s.parents+s.optional_parents)) != len(s.parents+s.optional_parents):
            raise LineageError('Duplicate parent.')
    visited, pending = set(), set()
    def visit(key):
        if key in pending:
            raise LineageError('Cyclic evidence ancestry: '+key)
        if key in visited:
            return
        pending.add(key)
        for parent in mapping[key].parents+mapping[key].optional_parents:
            visit(parent)
        pending.remove(key); visited.add(key)
    for key in sorted(mapping):
        visit(key)
    return MappingProxyType(mapping)

REGISTRY = validate_registry(_SPECS)

def numerical_restatement_sources(key, pattern):
    """Pattern-specific equivalence, not a family-wide suppression rule.
    The fragment/partial fallback in classify_boundary_convergence only tests
    existing overlap thresholds. The contour-positive branch adds measured
    topology and deliberately is NOT in this contract. Cross and boundary are
    sibling consumers of the same overlap, not independent extractions.
    """
    if key == 'boundary' and pattern in {
        'possible_boundary_fragment_convergence', 'partial_boundary_convergence',
    }:
        return ('cross_signal',)
    return ()

def build_lineage(sections, *, context=None, registry=REGISTRY):
    """Snapshot status/ancestry only; never retain summaries, details or arrays.
    Inconclusive detector interpretation can still provide a measured upstream
    feature (e.g. ELA/noise maps). It has no status-weighted scoring credit.
    Missing optional payloads do not invalidate a detector's own measurements.
    """
    registry = validate_registry(tuple(registry.values()))
    results = {}
    def build(key):
        if key in results:
            return results[key]
        spec = registry.get(key)
        section = sections.get(key)
        status = getattr(section,'status','not_run')
        available = status in ACTIVE or status == 'inconclusive'
        if key == 'scene_context':
            status = 'available' if context is not None else 'not_run'
            available = context is not None
        if spec is None:
            record = LineageRecord('evidence:'+key,'unregistered',Family.UNKNOWN,(),(),(),(),(),status,
                available,False,Kind.DERIVED,Addition.INTERPRETATION,known=False)
        else:
            parents = {p:build(p) for p in spec.parents+spec.optional_parents}
            missing = tuple('evidence:'+p for p in spec.parents if not parents[p].available or parents[p].missing_dependencies)
            ancestry = {'measurement:'+m for m in spec.measurements} if available else set()
            for p,record in parents.items():
                if record.available:
                    ancestry.update(record.measurement_ancestry)
            record = LineageRecord('evidence:'+key,spec.detector,spec.family,tuple(sorted(ancestry)),
                tuple('evidence:'+p for p in spec.parents),tuple('evidence:'+p for p in spec.optional_parents),
                missing,spec.hypotheses,status,available,status in ACTIVE and not missing,
                spec.kind,spec.addition,spec.overlap_groups)
        results[key]=record
        return record
    for key in sorted(set(registry)|set(sections)):
        build(key)
    return tuple(sorted(results.values(),key=lambda r:r.evidence_id))


def lineage_to_json(records):
    return [asdict(record) for record in sorted(records,key=lambda r:r.evidence_id)]


def lineage_from_json(items):
    """Restore validated immutable records, without accepting payload fields."""
    result=[]
    fields=set(LineageRecord.__dataclass_fields__)
    tuple_fields=('measurement_ancestry','direct_dependencies','optional_dependencies','missing_dependencies','hypotheses','overlap_groups')
    for item in items:
        if set(item)!=fields:
            raise LineageError('Serialized lineage fields do not match version 2b2.')
        values=dict(item)
        for name in tuple_fields:
            if not isinstance(values[name],(list,tuple)):
                raise LineageError('Serialized references must be lists.')
            values[name]=tuple(values[name])
        try:
            for name,enum in (('family',Family),('kind',Kind),('addition',Addition)):
                values[name]=enum(values[name])
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise LineageError('Unknown serialized lineage category.') from exc
        result.append(LineageRecord(**values))
    nodes={r.evidence_id:r for r in result}
    if len(nodes)!=len(result):
        raise LineageError('Duplicate serialized evidence identifier.')
    visited=set()
    def visit(key, pending=()):
        if key not in nodes or key in pending:
            raise LineageError('Missing or cyclic serialized lineage parent.')
        if key in visited: return
        for parent in nodes[key].direct_dependencies+nodes[key].optional_dependencies:
            visit(parent,pending+(key,))
        visited.add(key)
    for key in nodes: visit(key)
    return tuple(sorted(result,key=lambda r:r.evidence_id))

#exiftool_backend.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme

"""
exiftool_backend.py
Phase 1 metadata/provenance extractor for Signal Class 1:
static metadata checks for declarative or intentional signals.
Design goals:
- Use ExifTool as canonical metadata backend
- Preserve raw watchlist hits
- Interpret signals conservatively
- Serialize findings to exif_signals.json
Tested conceptually for:
- JPEG / TIFF / HEIC / PNG / WebP
- EXIF / XMP / PNG text / possible C2PA presence
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import json
import hashlib
import shutil
import re
import sys, subprocess
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

# local imports
from exif_config import (
    WATCH_TAGS,
    TOOL_RULES,
    DECLARATIVE_GENERATION_TERMS,
    DECLARATIVE_ENHANCEMENT_TERMS,
    TEXT_LIKE_TAG_HINTS,
    GENERATION_PROMPT_TAGS
)
from metadata_evidence import classify_metadata_signals, DIGITAL_SOURCE_TYPE_DECLARATIONS

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

EXIFTOOL_COMMAND = "exiftool"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dataclasses
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class Signal:
    id: str
    severity: str
    confidence: float
    status: str
    summary: str
    evidence: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None

@dataclass
class ExtractorInfo:
    tool: str
    version: str
    command: List[str]
    extracted_at: str

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Utility helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def max_rule_confidence(items: List[Dict[str, Any]], default: float = 0.5) -> float:
    if not items:
        return default
    return max(safe_float(item.get("rule_confidence", default), default) for item in items)

def max_rule_severity(items: List[Dict[str, Any]], default: str = "medium") -> str:
    if not items:
        return default
    rank = {"low": 1, "medium": 2, "high": 3}
    best = default
    best_rank = rank.get(default, 2)
    for item in items:
        sev = item.get("rule_severity", default)
        sev_rank = rank.get(sev, 2)
        if sev_rank > best_rank:
            best = sev
            best_rank = sev_rank
    return best

def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat()

def sha256_file(path: Path, chunk_size: int = 1024 * 1024) -> str:
    hasher = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        while True:
            chunk = f.read(chunk_size)
            if not chunk:
                break
            hasher.update(chunk)
    return hasher.hexdigest()

def normalize_text(value: Any) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
        try:
            return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False).lower()
        except Exception:
            return str(value).lower()
    return str(value).lower()

def safe_float(value: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
    try:
        return float(value)
    except Exception:
        return default

def ensure_exiftool_available(exiftool_cmd: str = EXIFTOOL_COMMAND) -> None:
    if shutil.which(exiftool_cmd) is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"ExifTool not found in PATH: '{exiftool_cmd}'. "
            f"Install ExifTool and ensure it is available on the command line."
        )

def split_group_and_tag(raw_key: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Example:
        'XMP-xmp:CreatorTool' -> ('XMP-xmp', 'CreatorTool')
        'Software' -> ('', 'Software')
    """
    if ":" in raw_key:
        group, tag = raw_key.split(":", 1)
        return group, tag
    return "", raw_key

def is_text_like_tag(tag_name: str) -> bool:
    tag_l = tag_name.lower()
    return any(hint in tag_l for hint in TEXT_LIKE_TAG_HINTS)


def _is_embedded_metadata_entry(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Exclude host paths and extractor diagnostics from declarative evidence."""
    raw_group, raw_tag = split_group_and_tag(str(entry.get("raw_key", "")))
    group = str(entry.get("group") or raw_group).lower()
    tag = str(entry.get("tag") or raw_tag).lower()
    return (group not in {"system", "exiftool", "composite"}
            and tag not in {"sourcefile", "filename", "basename", "directory", "filepath"})

def contains_any(text: str, terms: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    hits = []
    for term in terms:
        if term in text:
            hits.append(term)
    return hits

def deduplicate_match_items(items: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for item in items:
        key = (
            item.get("group"),
            item.get("tag"),
            json.dumps(item.get("value"), sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
            if isinstance(item.get("value"), (dict, list))
            else str(item.get("value")),
            tuple(item.get("matched_terms", [])),
            item.get("category"),
        )
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            result.append(item)
    return result

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# ExifTool execution
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def run_hidden_subprocess(command, **kwargs) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    startupinfo = None
    creationflags = 0
    if sys.platform.startswith("win"):
        startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
        startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
        startupinfo.wShowWindow = 0
        creationflags = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW
    try:
        return subprocess.run(
            command,
            startupinfo=startupinfo,
            creationflags=creationflags,
            **kwargs,
        )
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
        stderr = exc.stderr if exc.stderr else ""
        stdout = exc.stdout if exc.stdout else ""
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"External command failed:\n"
            f"Command: {command}\n"
            f"Return code: {exc.returncode}\n"
            f"STDOUT: {stdout}\n"
            f"STDERR: {stderr}"
        ) from exc

def get_exiftool_version(exiftool_cmd: str = EXIFTOOL_COMMAND) -> str:
    result = run_hidden_subprocess(
        [exiftool_cmd, "-ver"],
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return result.stdout.strip()

def run_exiftool_json(file_path: Path, exiftool_cmd: str = EXIFTOOL_COMMAND) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[str]]:
    """
    Returns:
        records: parsed JSON list from exiftool
        command: command used
    """
    command = [
        exiftool_cmd,
        "-j",
        "-G1",
        "-a",
        "-u",
        "-struct",
        "-api",
        "RequestAll=2",
        str(file_path),
    ]

    result = run_hidden_subprocess(
        command,
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=20,
    )

    try:
        data = json.loads(result.stdout)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to parse ExifTool JSON output: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(data, list) or not data:
        raise RuntimeError("ExifTool returned empty or unexpected JSON structure.")
    return data, command

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Metadata parsing and watchlist extraction
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def extract_watchlist_hits(record: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """
    Extracts metadata hits grouped into evidence families.
    Purpose:
    - Route high-value metadata tags into semantically meaningful buckets
    - Preserve watchlist hits for later interpretation
    - Preserve broad text-like fields for secondary scanning
    """
    hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {
        "software": [],
        "creator_tool": [],
        "history": [],
        "derived_from": [],
        "container_text": [],
        "c2pa": [],
        "other_watchlist": [],
        "broad_text_scan": [],
        "generation_prompt": [],
        "digital_source_type": [],
    }
    for raw_key, value in record.items():
        group, tag = split_group_and_tag(raw_key)
        tag_l = tag.lower().strip()
        group_l = group.lower().strip()
        entry = {
            "raw_key": raw_key,
            "group": group,
            "tag": tag,
            "value": value,
        }
        if not _is_embedded_metadata_entry(entry):
            continue
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # High-priority semantic routing
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if tag_l == "digitalsourcetype":
            hits["digital_source_type"].append(entry)
            continue
        # Software attribution / processing identity
        if tag_l in {"software", "processingsoftware", "hostcomputer"}:
            hits["software"].append(entry)
            continue
        # XMP creator tool
        if tag_l in {"creator", "artist", "author", "copyright"}:
            hits.setdefault("creator_identity", []).append(entry)
            continue
        # XMP prompt info
        if tag_l in GENERATION_PROMPT_TAGS:
            hits["generation_prompt"].append(entry)
            continue        
        # XMP history / resource-event style tags
        if tag_l.startswith("history"):
            hits["history"].append(entry)
            continue
        # XMP media-management lineage / derivation tags
        if tag_l.startswith("derivedfrom"):
            hits["derived_from"].append(entry)
            continue
        # C2PA / JUMBF / manifest-related metadata
        if (
            tag_l in {"jumbf", "c2pa", "manifest", "claimgenerator", "actions"}
            or "c2pa" in group_l
            or "jumbf" in group_l
        ):
            hits["c2pa"].append(entry)
            continue
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Container text / free-text style metadata
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if tag_l in {"comment", "source", "warning", "xmlcomadobexmp", "prompt", "parameters", "description", "imagedescription", "caption", "xpcomment", "usercomment", }:
            hits["container_text"].append(entry)
            continue
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Configured watchlist fallback
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if tag_l in WATCH_TAGS:
            hits["other_watchlist"].append(entry)
            continue
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Broad secondary scan for text-like tags
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if is_text_like_tag(tag):
            hits["broad_text_scan"].append(entry)
    return hits

def assess_camera_metadata_richness(record: dict[str, object], hits: dict[str, list[dict[str, object]]] | None = None,) -> Optional[Signal]:
    def has_any_suffix(*suffixes: str) -> bool:
        suffixes_l = tuple(s.lower() for s in suffixes)
        for key in record.keys():
            k = str(key).lower()
            if k.endswith(suffixes_l):
                return True
        return False
    found: list[str] = []
    tag_groups = [
        ("make", ("make",)),
        ("model", ("model", "camera model name")),
        ("lens", ("lens model", "lens id")),
        ("exposure", ("exposure time",)),
        ("aperture", ("f number", "aperture", "aperture value")),
        ("iso", ("iso",)),
        ("capture_time", ("date/time original", "createdate", "create date")),
        ("scene_type", ("scene type",)),
        ("gps", ("gps latitude", "gps longitude", "gps date/time")),
    ]
    for label, suffixes in tag_groups:
        if has_any_suffix(*suffixes):
            found.append(label)
    # Optional vendor / smartphone enrichers
    vendor_markers = 0
    if any(str(k).startswith("OnePlus:") for k in record.keys()):
        vendor_markers += 1
    if any(str(k).startswith("Google:") for k in record.keys()):
        vendor_markers += 1
    if len(found) >= 4:
        confidence = 0.78 if vendor_markers == 0 else 0.84
        return Signal(
            id="CAMERA_METADATA_RICH",
            severity="low",
            confidence=confidence,
            status="supporting",
            summary=(
                "Metadata contains multiple camera-acquisition fields consistent "
                "with a real photographic pipeline."
            ),
            evidence=[{
                "fields_detected": found,
                "vendor_markers": vendor_markers,
            }],
        )
    return None

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Evidence interpretation
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def collect_textual_evidence(hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Flatten selected evidence buckets into a single list that can be scanned.
    """
    evidence = []
    for bucket_name in (
        "software",
        "creator_tool",
        "history",
        "derived_from",
        "container_text",
        "c2pa",
        "other_watchlist",
        "broad_text_scan",
        "generation_prompt"
    ):
        evidence.extend(item for item in hits.get(bucket_name, []) if _is_embedded_metadata_entry(item))
    return evidence

def find_term_matches(hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """
    Scan evidence text for tool names and declarative phrases.
    Uses unified TOOL_RULES loaded from exif_rules.json.
    """
    evidence = collect_textual_evidence(hits)
    result = {
        "generation_tools": [],
        "enhancement_tools": [],
        "generic_editing_tools": [],
        "declarative_generation_terms": [],
        "declarative_enhancement_terms": [],
    }
    for item in evidence:
        text = f"{item.get('tag', '')} {normalize_text(item.get('value'))}"
        # Tool rules
        for rule in TOOL_RULES:
            term = rule.get("term", "").lower().strip()
            aliases = [a.lower().strip() for a in rule.get("aliases", [])]
            category = rule.get("category", "").strip()
            confidence = safe_float(rule.get("confidence", 0.5))
            severity = rule.get("severity", "medium")
            candidate_terms = [term] + aliases
            matched_terms = [t for t in candidate_terms if t and t in text]
            if not matched_terms:
                continue
            enriched = {
                **item,
                "matched_terms": matched_terms,
                "rule_term": term,
                "category": category,
                "rule_confidence": confidence,
                "rule_severity": severity,
            }
            if category == "generation":
                result["generation_tools"].append(enriched)
            elif category == "enhancement":
                result["enhancement_tools"].append(enriched)
            elif category == "generic_editing":
                result["generic_editing_tools"].append(enriched)

        # Declarative phrases
        dec_gen_hits = contains_any(text, DECLARATIVE_GENERATION_TERMS)
        dec_enh_hits = contains_any(text, DECLARATIVE_ENHANCEMENT_TERMS)
        if dec_gen_hits:
            result["declarative_generation_terms"].append(
                {**item, "matched_terms": dec_gen_hits}
            )
        if dec_enh_hits:
            result["declarative_enhancement_terms"].append(
                {**item, "matched_terms": dec_enh_hits}
            )
        for key in result:
            result[key] = deduplicate_match_items(result[key])       
    return result

def _explicit_generation_workflow(items: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:
    """Require generation-specific structure, not merely a populated tag.

    Recognize ComfyUI sampler nodes, diffusion parameters or a Midjourney job
    signature. A Job ID alone, a folder name or generic prompt prose is not enough.
    Tool names alone remain ambiguous; authenticity is never verified here.
    """
    text = "\n".join(normalize_text(item.get("value")) for item in items)
    sampler_node = re.search(r'["\'](?:class_type|type)["\']\s*:\s*["\'](?:ksampler|samplercustom)', text)
    parameters = all(re.search(pattern, text) for pattern in (
        r"\bsteps\s*:", r"\bsampler\s*:", r"\bseed\s*:", r"\bcfg scale\s*:",
    ))
    job_id = re.search(
        r"\bjob id\s*:\s*[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}\b", text,
    )
    generation_parameter = re.search(r"(?:^|\s)--(?:chaos|c|stylize|s)\s+\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b", text)
    version_or_style = re.search(r"(?:^|\s)--(?:(?:version|v)\s+\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|style\s+raw\b)", text)
    return bool(sampler_node or parameters or (job_id and generation_parameter and version_or_style))


def _source_type_declarations(
    hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]], signal_id: str,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    evidence = []
    for item in hits.get("digital_source_type", []):
        if not _is_embedded_metadata_entry(item):
            continue
        value = item.get("value")
        values = value if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) else [value]
        for candidate in values:
            if isinstance(candidate, str) and DIGITAL_SOURCE_TYPE_DECLARATIONS.get(candidate.strip()) == signal_id:
                evidence.append({**item, "declaration_kind": "iptc_digital_source_type", "matched_value": candidate.strip()})
    return evidence


def build_signals(
    file_path: Path,
    record: Dict[str, Any],
    hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
    matches: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
) -> List[Signal]:
    signals: List[Signal] = []

    # 0. Generator prompt
    prompt_fields = hits.get("generation_prompt", [])
    generation_prompt_evidence = [
        item for item in prompt_fields + hits.get("container_text", [])
        if _is_embedded_metadata_entry(item) and _explicit_generation_workflow([item])
    ]
    if generation_prompt_evidence:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="EMBEDDED_GENERATION_PROMPT",
                severity="high",
                confidence=0.98,
                status="hit",
                summary=(
                    "Image metadata contains an embedded generation prompt or workflow, "
                    "which declares a generation workflow; metadata authenticity is unverified."
                ),
                evidence=generation_prompt_evidence,
            )
        )
    elif prompt_fields:
        signals.append(Signal(
            id="AMBIGUOUS_WORKFLOW_METADATA", severity="low", confidence=0.40,
            status="supporting",
            summary="Workflow or prompt fields are populated without a specific generation structure.",
            evidence=prompt_fields,
        ))
    # 1. Creator
    creator_identity = hits.get("creator_identity", [])
    if creator_identity:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="CREATOR_IDENTITY_PRESENT",
                severity="low",
                confidence=0.65,
                status="supporting",
                summary="Metadata contains creator or authorship information.",
                evidence=creator_identity,
            )
        )

    # 2. Software trace
    software_evidence = hits["software"] + hits["creator_tool"]
    if software_evidence:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="SOFTWARE_TRACE_PRESENT",
                severity="medium",
                confidence=0.70,
                status="hit",
                summary="Image metadata contains explicit software attribution.",
                evidence=software_evidence,
            )
        )
    # 3. Edit history
    if hits["history"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="EDIT_HISTORY_PRESENT",
                severity="medium",
                confidence=0.80,
                status="hit",
                summary="XMP edit-history/resource-event style fields are present.",
                evidence=hits["history"],
            )
        )
    # 4. Derivation lineage
    if hits["derived_from"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="DERIVATION_LINEAGE_PRESENT",
                severity="medium",
                confidence=0.75,
                status="hit",
                summary="Metadata indicates that this file may derive from another managed asset.",
                evidence=hits["derived_from"],
            )
        )
    # 5. PNG/WebP or container textual metadata
    if hits["container_text"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="CONTAINER_TEXTUAL_METADATA_PRESENT",
                severity="low",
                confidence=0.60,
                status="hit",
                summary="Textual/container metadata fields are present and may contain workflow clues.",
                evidence=hits["container_text"],
            )
        )
    # 6. C2PA presence
    if hits["c2pa"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="C2PA_PRESENT",
                severity="medium",
                confidence=0.85,
                status="hit",
                summary="Potential C2PA/JUMBF provenance-related metadata is present; separate verification is required.",
                evidence=hits["c2pa"],
            )
        )
    # 7. Explicit AI generation tool name match
    if matches["generation_tools"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="AI_TOOL_NAME_MATCH",
                severity=max_rule_severity(matches["generation_tools"], default="high"),
                confidence=max_rule_confidence(matches["generation_tools"], default=0.88),
                status="hit",
                summary="Metadata contains one or more tool names associated with AI image generation workflows.",
                evidence=matches["generation_tools"],
            )
        )
    # 8. Explicit AI enhancement tool name match
    if matches["enhancement_tools"]:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="ENHANCEMENT_TOOL_NAME_MATCH",
                severity="high",
                confidence=0.86,
                status="hit",
                summary="Metadata contains one or more tool names associated with AI enhancement or upscaling workflows.",
                evidence=matches["enhancement_tools"],
            )
        )
    # 9. Explicit declarative generation wording
    generation_declarations = (matches["declarative_generation_terms"]
                               + _source_type_declarations(hits, "DECLARED_SYNTHETIC"))
    if generation_declarations:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="DECLARED_SYNTHETIC",
                severity="high",
                confidence=0.92,
                status="hit",
                summary="Metadata declares synthetic or AI-generated origin through wording or IPTC Digital Source Type; authenticity is unverified.",
                evidence=generation_declarations,
            )
        )
    # 10. Explicit declarative enhancement wording
    enhancement_declarations = (matches["declarative_enhancement_terms"]
                                + _source_type_declarations(hits, "DECLARED_ENHANCEMENT"))
    if enhancement_declarations:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="DECLARED_ENHANCEMENT",
                severity="high",
                confidence=0.90,
                status="hit",
                summary="Metadata declares enhancement or generative editing through wording or IPTC Digital Source Type; this does not establish whole-image generation.",
                evidence=enhancement_declarations,
            )
        )
    # 11. Generic editing software only
    if (
        matches["generic_editing_tools"]
        and not matches["generation_tools"]
        and not matches["enhancement_tools"]
        and not matches["declarative_generation_terms"]
        and not matches["declarative_enhancement_terms"]
    ):
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="GENERIC_EDITING_SOFTWARE_PRESENT",
                severity=max_rule_severity(matches["generic_editing_tools"], default="low"),
                confidence=max_rule_confidence(matches["generic_editing_tools"], default=0.55),
                status="hit",
                summary="Generic image-editing software is present in metadata, but no explicit AI-specific declaration was found.",
                evidence=matches["generic_editing_tools"],
            )
        )
    # 11. Camera info present - strong counter signal!
    camera_signal = assess_camera_metadata_richness(record, hits)
    if camera_signal:
        signals.append(camera_signal)
    # 12. Sparse metadata heuristic
    sparse_indicator = assess_metadata_sparsity(record, hits)
    if sparse_indicator is not None:
        signals.append(sparse_indicator)
    # 13. Inconclusive fallback
    if not signals:
        signals.append(
            Signal(
                id="METADATA_INCONCLUSIVE",
                severity="low",
                confidence=0.40,
                status="inconclusive",
                summary="No meaningful declarative or provenance-related metadata signals were found.",
                evidence=None,
            )
        )
    return signals

def _has_meaningful_value(value: Any) -> bool:
    if value is None:
        return False
    if isinstance(value, str):
        v = value.strip()
        if not v:
            return False
        if v.lower() in {"srgb", "rgb", "xyz", "acsp", "unknown ()"}:
            return False
        return True
    if isinstance(value, (list, dict)):
        return len(value) > 0
    return True

def _count_semantic_hits(hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> int:
    semantic_buckets = (
        "software",
        "creator_tool",
        "history",
        "derived_from",
        "container_text",
        "c2pa",
        "other_watchlist",
        "generation_prompt",
        "digital_source_type",
        # intentionally exclude broad_text_scan
    )
    count = 0
    for bucket in semantic_buckets:
        for item in hits.get(bucket, []):
            tag = str(item.get("tag", "")).strip()
            value = item.get("value")
            # ignore known non-provenance boilerplate even if they leaked in
            if tag in {"SourceFile", "ExifToolVersion", "ProfileDescription"}:
                continue
            if _has_meaningful_value(value):
                count += 1
                # print(f"meanigful value: {value}")
    return count


def assess_metadata_sparsity(record: Dict[str, Any], hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> Optional[Signal]:
    """
    Conservative sparse-metadata heuristic.
    Distinguish provenance-bearing metadata from technical/container metadata.
    """

    ignored_prefixes = {
        "SourceFile",
        "ExifTool:ExifToolVersion",
        "ExifTool:Now",
        "ExifTool:NewGUID",
        "ExifTool:FileSequence",
        "ExifTool:ProcessingTime",
        "System:FileName",
        "System:BaseName",
        "System:Directory",
        "System:FilePath",
        "System:FileSize",
        "System:ZoneIdentifier",
        "System:FileModifyDate",
        "System:FileAccessDate",
        "System:FileCreateDate",
        "System:FilePermissions",
        "System:FileAttributes",
        "System:FileDeviceNumber",
        "System:FileInodeNumber",
        "System:FileHardLinks",
        "System:FileUserID",
        "System:FileGroupID",
        "System:FileDeviceID",
        "System:FileBlockSize",
        "System:FileBlockCount",
        "File:FileType",
        "File:FileTypeExtension",
        "File:MIMEType",
    }

    technical_prefixes = (
        "JFIF:",
        "ICC-header:",
        "ICC_Profile:",
        "Composite:",
    )

    technical_exact = {
        "File:ExifByteOrder",
        "File:ImageWidth",
        "File:ImageHeight",
        "File:EncodingProcess",
        "File:BitsPerSample",
        "File:ColorComponents",
        "File:YCbCrSubSampling",
        "IFD0:XResolution",
        "IFD0:YResolution",
        "IFD0:ResolutionUnit",
        "IFD0:YCbCrPositioning",
        "IFD0:ProfileCopyright",
    }

    provenance_suffixes = (
        "Make",
        "Model",
        "LensModel",
        "LensID",
        "ExposureTime",
        "FNumber",
        "ISO",
        "DateTimeOriginal",
        "CreateDate",
        "Software",
        "CreatorTool",
        "History",
        "DerivedFrom",
        "Comment",
        "Description",
        "ImageDescription",
        "UserComment",
        "Prompt",
        "Parameters",
        "Manifest",
        "JUMBF",
        "C2PA",
        "ClaimGenerator",
        "Actions",
    )

    provenance_keys = []
    technical_keys = []

    for key in record.keys():
        if key in ignored_prefixes:
            continue
        if key in technical_exact or key.startswith(technical_prefixes):
            technical_keys.append(key)
            continue
        if any(key.endswith(suffix) for suffix in provenance_suffixes):
            provenance_keys.append(key)
            continue

        # unknown remainder: keep but do not count strongly
        # you can decide later whether to track these separately

    semantic_hit_count = _count_semantic_hits(hits)   
    if semantic_hit_count == 0:
        return Signal(
            id="METADATA_STRIPPED_OR_SPARSE",
            severity="low",
            confidence=0.55,
            status="weak_hit",
            summary=(
                "Metadata appears sparse or stripped of provenance-bearing fields; "
                "technical/container metadata may still be present."
            ),
            evidence=[{
                "provenance_keys_detected": provenance_keys,
                "technical_keys_detected_count": len(technical_keys),
            }],
        )            
    return None


def derive_classification(signals: List[Signal]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    result = classify_metadata_signals(s.id for s in signals)
    # Keep the extractor's legacy status; the summary exposes the prompt pattern.
    if result["metadata_status"] == "embedded_generation_prompt_detected":
        result["metadata_status"] = "declared_synthetic_candidate"
    return result

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main orchestration
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_output_payload(
    file_path: Path,
    record: Dict[str, Any],
    extractor_info: ExtractorInfo,
    hits: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
    matches: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
    signals: List[Signal],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "file": {
            "path": str(file_path),
            "name": file_path.name,
            "format": record.get("File:FileType"),
            "mime_type": record.get("File:MIMEType"),
            "filesize_bytes": file_path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": sha256_file(file_path),
        },
        "extractor": asdict(extractor_info),
        "raw_watchlist_hits": hits,
        "term_matches": matches,
        "signals": [asdict(s) for s in signals],
        "classification": derive_classification(signals),
        "notes": [
            "Metadata alone does not prove AI generation or enhancement.",
            "C2PA presence should be verified separately in a dedicated provenance step.",
            "Absence of metadata is weak context only and may result from export, stripping, or recompression.",
        ],
    }

def analyze_file_with_exiftool(
    file_path: Path,
    output_json_path: Optional[Path] = None,
    exiftool_cmd: str = EXIFTOOL_COMMAND,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not file_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"File does not exist: {file_path}")
    ensure_exiftool_available(exiftool_cmd=exiftool_cmd)
    version = get_exiftool_version(exiftool_cmd=exiftool_cmd)
    records, command = run_exiftool_json(file_path=file_path, exiftool_cmd=exiftool_cmd)

    # ExifTool returns a list with one record for one file
    record = records[0]
    hits = extract_watchlist_hits(record)
    matches = find_term_matches(hits)
    signals = build_signals(file_path, record, hits, matches)
    extractor_info = ExtractorInfo(
        tool="exiftool",
        version=version,
        command=command,
        extracted_at=utc_now_iso(),
    )
    payload = build_output_payload(
        file_path=file_path,
        record=record,
        extractor_info=extractor_info,
        hits=hits,
        matches=matches,
        signals=signals,
    )
    if output_json_path is not None:
        output_json_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output_json_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    return payload


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract declarative/provenance-oriented metadata signals using ExifTool.")
    parser.add_argument("image_path", type=str, help="Path to the image file")
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=str,
        default="exif_signals.json",
        help="Path to output JSON file",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--exiftool",
        type=str,
        default=EXIFTOOL_COMMAND,
        help="ExifTool command or full path",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()
    image_path = Path(args.image_path).expanduser().resolve()
    output_path = Path(args.output).expanduser().resolve()
    payload = analyze_file_with_exiftool(file_path=image_path, output_json_path=output_path, exiftool_cmd=args.exiftool,)
    print(f"[OK] Wrote metadata signal report to: {output_path}")
    print(f"[INFO] metadata_status = {payload['classification']['metadata_status']}")
    print(f"[INFO] declared_generation = {payload['classification']['declared_generation']}")
    print(f"[INFO] declared_enhancement = {payload['classification']['declared_enhancement']}")
    print(f"[INFO] provenance_present = {payload['classification']['provenance_present']}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

--- End of exiftool_backend.py ---

--- Start of gui_semantics.py ---
#sgui_semantics.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages
#
# Signal kinds / strengths how this translates into colours

from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional, Any

class LogLevel(str, Enum):
    INFO = "info"
    SUCCESS = "success"
    WARN = "warn"
    ERROR = "error"
    FINDING = "finding"

class SignalPolarity(str, Enum):
    NEUTRAL = "neutral"
    SUSPICIOUS = "suspicious"
    BENIGN = "benign"

class SignalStrength(str, Enum):
    NOT_RUN = "not_run"
    INCONCLUSIVE = "inconclusive"
    WEAK = "weak"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    STRONG = "strong"
    
SEMANTIC_COLORS = {
    "text_primary": "#d1d5db",
    "text_muted": "#9ca3af",
    "border_subtle": "#374151",
    "bg_subtle": "#1f2937",
    # log / event semantics
    "info": "#6b7280",
    "success": "#4b7f52",
    "warn": "#b08900",
    "error": "#b91c1c",
    "finding": "#6d28d9",
    # suspicious signal strengths
    "suspicious_weak": "#a16207",
    "suspicious_medium": "#b45309",
    "suspicious_strong": "#b91c1c",
    # benign signal strengths
    "benign_weak": "#bbf7d0",
    "benign_medium": "#4ade80",
    "benign_strong": "#15803d",
    # neutral / inconclusive
    "neutral_not_run": "#9ca3af",
    "neutral_inconclusive": "#6b7280",
}    

# helper functions
def color_for_log_level(level: str) -> str:
    level = (level or "info").lower()
    return {
        "info": SEMANTIC_COLORS["info"],
        "success": SEMANTIC_COLORS["success"],
        "warn": SEMANTIC_COLORS["warn"],
        "error": SEMANTIC_COLORS["error"],
        "finding": SEMANTIC_COLORS["finding"],
    }.get(level, SEMANTIC_COLORS["info"])
    
def color_for_signal(polarity: str, strength: str) -> str:
    polarity = (polarity or "neutral").lower()
    strength = (strength or "inconclusive").lower()
    if polarity == "suspicious":
        return {
            "weak": SEMANTIC_COLORS["suspicious_weak"],
            "medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["suspicious_medium"],
            "strong": SEMANTIC_COLORS["suspicious_strong"],
        }.get(strength, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    if polarity == "benign":
        return {
            "weak": SEMANTIC_COLORS["benign_weak"],
            "medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["benign_medium"],
            "strong": SEMANTIC_COLORS["benign_strong"],
        }.get(strength, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    return {
        "not_run": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_not_run"],
        "inconclusive": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
        "weak": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
        "medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
        "strong": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
    }.get(strength, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    
def color_for_confidence(confidence: str, polarity: str = "neutral") -> str:
    confidence = (confidence or "Low").capitalize()
    polarity = (polarity or "neutral").lower()
    if polarity == "suspicious":
        return {
            "High": SEMANTIC_COLORS["suspicious_strong"],
            "Medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["suspicious_medium"],
            "Low": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
        }.get(confidence, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    if polarity == "benign":
        return {
            "High": SEMANTIC_COLORS["benign_strong"],
            "Medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["benign_medium"],
            "Low": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
        }.get(confidence, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    return {
        "High": SEMANTIC_COLORS["warn"],
        "Medium": SEMANTIC_COLORS["warn"],
        "Low": SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"],
    }.get(confidence, SEMANTIC_COLORS["neutral_inconclusive"])
    
@dataclass
class ModuleSummary:
    name: str
    status: str = "not_run"
    polarity: str = "neutral"
    domain: str = "unknown"
    pattern: Optional[str] = None
    score: Optional[float] = None
    confidence: Optional[str] = None
    note: str = ""
    artifacts: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
--- End of gui_semantics.py ---

#hypothesis_engine.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
from math import fsum
from typing import Optional
# local imports to avoid circular dependencies
from gui_semantics import ModuleSummary
from numerical_evidence import allocate_base_evidence, add_fragment_component, finalize_component_allocations
from metadata_evidence import (
    ProvenanceState, MISSING_METADATA_PATTERNS, metadata_provenance_state,
)
from scorecard_integrity import (
    STATUS_WEIGHTS, COMPONENT_FIELDS, BOOST_FIELDS, ComponentLedger,
    available_sections, usable_observation, validate_score_inputs,
    validate_derived_signals, validate_scorecard,
    camera_attenuation,
    ScorecardIntegrityError,
    validate_score_context,
)

STATUS_WEIGHT = STATUS_WEIGHTS

@dataclass

    natural_repetition: bool = False
    decorative_geometric: bool = False
    smartphone_computational: bool = False
    screen_origin: bool = False

@dataclass

    sensor_origin: ModuleSummary | None = None
    cfa_demosaic: ModuleSummary | None = None
