# metadata_evidence.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#
"""Shared metadata semantics. Presence and declarations are not authentication."""

from enum import Enum

class ProvenanceState(str, Enum):
    VERIFIED_GENERATION_ASSERTION = "verified_generation_assertion"
    UNVERIFIED_GENERATION_DECLARATION = "unverified_generation_declaration"
    UNVERIFIED_ENHANCEMENT_DECLARATION = "unverified_enhancement_declaration"
    AMBIGUOUS_TOOL_OR_WORKFLOW_METADATA = "ambiguous_tool_or_workflow_metadata"
    PROVENANCE_PRESENT_UNVERIFIED = "provenance_present_unverified"
    NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE = "no_substantive_provenance_evidence"


GENERATION_PATTERNS = frozenset({
    "declared_synthetic_candidate", "embedded_generation_prompt_detected",
})
ENHANCEMENT_PATTERNS = frozenset({"declared_enhancement_candidate"})
MISSING_METADATA_PATTERNS = frozenset({
    "sparse_or_stripped", "metadata_stripped", "social_media_provenance",
})
GENERATION_SIGNAL_IDS = frozenset({"EMBEDDED_GENERATION_PROMPT", "DECLARED_SYNTHETIC"})
ENHANCEMENT_SIGNAL_IDS = frozenset({"DECLARED_ENHANCEMENT"})
AMBIGUOUS_SIGNAL_IDS = frozenset({
    "AI_TOOL_NAME_MATCH", "ENHANCEMENT_TOOL_NAME_MATCH", "AMBIGUOUS_WORKFLOW_METADATA",
    "GENERIC_EDITING_SOFTWARE_PRESENT",
})

# Exact controlled-vocabulary identifiers, not substring/tool-name matches.
# https://www.iptc.org/std/photometadata/documentation/userguide/
DIGITAL_SOURCE_TYPE_DECLARATIONS = {
    f"{scheme}://cv.iptc.org/newscodes/digitalsourcetype/{term}": signal_id
    for scheme in ("http", "https")
    for term, signal_id in (
        ("trainedAlgorithmicMedia", "DECLARED_SYNTHETIC"),
        ("compositeWithTrainedAlgorithmicMedia", "DECLARED_ENHANCEMENT"),
        ("compositeSynthetic", "DECLARED_ENHANCEMENT"),
    )
}


def classify_metadata_signals(signal_ids) -> dict:
    """Canonical classification for extractor payloads and summary adapters.

    No validator exists in this path. Even a payload claiming verification must
    not be promoted to verified provenance here.
    """
    ids = set(signal_ids)
    generation = bool(ids & GENERATION_SIGNAL_IDS)
    enhancement = bool(ids & ENHANCEMENT_SIGNAL_IDS)
    if generation:
        status = ("embedded_generation_prompt_detected" if "EMBEDDED_GENERATION_PROMPT" in ids
                  else "declared_synthetic_candidate")
        state = ProvenanceState.UNVERIFIED_GENERATION_DECLARATION
    elif enhancement:
        status = "declared_enhancement_candidate"
        state = ProvenanceState.UNVERIFIED_ENHANCEMENT_DECLARATION
    elif "C2PA_PRESENT" in ids:
        status = "provenance_present_unverified"
        state = ProvenanceState.PROVENANCE_PRESENT_UNVERIFIED
    elif ids & AMBIGUOUS_SIGNAL_IDS:
        status = "edited_or_processed_but_inconclusive"
        state = ProvenanceState.AMBIGUOUS_TOOL_OR_WORKFLOW_METADATA
    elif "CAMERA_METADATA_RICH" in ids:
        status = "camera_metadata_present"
        state = ProvenanceState.NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE
    elif "METADATA_STRIPPED_OR_SPARSE" in ids:
        status = "sparse_or_stripped"
        state = ProvenanceState.NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE
    else:
        status = "inconclusive"
        state = ProvenanceState.NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE
    return {
        "metadata_status": status,
        "provenance_state": state.value,
        "stripped": "METADATA_STRIPPED_OR_SPARSE" in ids,
        "declared_generation": generation,
        "declared_enhancement": enhancement,
        "provenance_present": "C2PA_PRESENT" in ids,
        "provenance_verified": False,
    }


def metadata_provenance_state(section) -> ProvenanceState:
    if section is None:
        return ProvenanceState.NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE
    details = section.details or {}
    ids = details.get("signal_ids")
    if ids:
        return ProvenanceState(classify_metadata_signals(ids)["provenance_state"])
    # Backward compatibility for older ModuleSummary objects without raw IDs.
    if section.pattern in GENERATION_PATTERNS:
        return ProvenanceState.UNVERIFIED_GENERATION_DECLARATION
    if section.pattern in ENHANCEMENT_PATTERNS:
        return ProvenanceState.UNVERIFIED_ENHANCEMENT_DECLARATION
    if section.pattern == "provenance_present_unverified":
        return ProvenanceState.PROVENANCE_PRESENT_UNVERIFIED
    if section.pattern == "edited_or_processed_but_inconclusive":
        return ProvenanceState.AMBIGUOUS_TOOL_OR_WORKFLOW_METADATA
    return ProvenanceState.NO_SUBSTANTIVE_PROVENANCE_EVIDENCE

--- End of metadata_evidence.py ---

#narrative_builder.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

from __future__ import annotations
from html import escape
# local imports to avoid circular dependencies
from gui_semantics import color_for_confidence, color_for_signal
from summary_types import SummaryResult

ORDERED_SECTION_KEYS = [
    "osint",
    "metadata",
    "sensor_origin",
    "cfa_demosaic",
    "ela",
    "noise",
    "compression_history",
    "subject_boundary",
    "region_masks",
    "segmentation_alignment",
    "cross_signal",
    "boundary",
    "boundary_focus",
    "patch_similarity",
    "patch_spatial",
    "region_fft",
    "fft",
    "subject_texture_consistency",
    "subject_process_asymmetry",
    "subject_material_realism",
]

def render_summary_text(result: SummaryResult) -> str:
    if not result.sections:
        return ""
    lines = [
        "Overall Forensic Reading (OSINT · Metadata · ELA · Residual · Sensor Origin · CFA/Demosaicing · Compression History · Patch Similarity · Cross-Signal · Boundary Signal · Boundary Focus · Subject Boundary · Region FFT · FFT)"
        f"Confidence Tier: {result.overall_confidence}",
        result.overall_text,
        "",
        "--------------------------------------------------",
        "",
    ]
    if result.context_info:
        lines.insert(3, f"Context Information: {result.context_info}")
    for key in ORDERED_SECTION_KEYS:
        section = result.sections.get(key)
        if section is None:
            continue
        lines.append(section.name)
        lines.append(section.note)
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines).rstrip()

def format_overall_text_html(text: str) -> str:
    if not text:
        return ""
    # Escape first (important for safety)
    text = escape(text)
    lines = text.split("\n")
    html_parts = []
    in_list = False
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        # --- Bullet handling ---
        if stripped.startswith("- "):
            if not in_list:
                html_parts.append("<ul style='margin:6px 0 6px 16px; padding:0;'>")
                in_list = True
            html_parts.append(f"<li>{stripped[2:]}</li>")
            continue
        # --- Close list if needed ---
        if in_list:
            html_parts.append("</ul>")
            in_list = False
        # --- Section headers ---
        if stripped in {
            "Supporting Signals:",
            "Contradicting Signals:",
            "Confidence Rationale:",
        }:
            html_parts.append(
                f"<div style='margin-top:10px; font-weight:600;'>{stripped}</div>"
            )
            continue
        # --- Empty line → spacing ---
        if not stripped:
            html_parts.append("<div style='height:6px;'></div>")
            continue
        # --- Normal text ---
        html_parts.append(f"<div>{stripped}</div>")
    if in_list:
        html_parts.append("</ul>")
    return "".join(html_parts)

def render_summary_html(result: SummaryResult) -> str:
    if not result.sections:
        return ""

    confidence_color = color_for_confidence(
        result.overall_confidence,
        result.overall_polarity,
    )

    def htmlize_multiline(text: str) -> str:
        if not text:
            return ""
        return escape(text).replace("\n", "<br>")

    theme_css = """
    <style>
      :root {
        --sl-bg: #ffffff;
        --sl-text: #111827;
        --sl-muted: #4b5563;
        --sl-context-bg: #f3f4f6;
        --sl-context-border: #9ca3af;

        --sl-benign-weak: #9ae6b4;
        --sl-benign-medium: #48bb78;
        --sl-benign-strong: #166534;
      }

      @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
        :root {
          --sl-bg: #0f172a;
          --sl-text: #d1d5db;
          --sl-muted: #9ca3af;
          --sl-context-bg: #111827;
          --sl-context-border: #4b5563;

          --sl-benign-weak: #9ae6b4;
          --sl-benign-medium: #48bb78;
          --sl-benign-strong: #166534;
        }
      }

      .sl-report {
        font-family: Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif;
        font-size: 10pt;
        line-height: 1.4;
        color: var(--sl-text);
        background-color: var(--sl-bg);
        padding: 12px;
      }

      .sl-overall-title {
        font-size: 11pt;
        font-weight: 600;
        margin-bottom: 4px;
      }

      .sl-context-box {
        margin-top: 8px;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        padding: 6px 8px;
        border-left: 3px solid var(--sl-context-border);
        background-color: var(--sl-context-bg);
        color: var(--sl-muted);
        font-style: italic;
      }

      .sl-section {
        margin-top: 10px;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
      }

      .sl-section-note {
        line-height: 1.35;
      }
    </style>
    """

    parts = [
        theme_css,
        "<div class='sl-report'>",
        "<div style='margin-bottom:12px;'>",
        "<div class='sl-overall-title'>Overall Forensic Reading</div>",
    ]

    if result.overall_confidence:
        parts.append(
            "<div style='margin-bottom:2px;'>"
            f"<b>Confidence Tier:</b> "
            f"<span style='color:{confidence_color}; font-weight:600;'>{escape(result.overall_confidence)}</span>"
            "</div>"
        )

    if result.overall_pattern:
        parts.append(
            "<div style='margin-bottom:2px;'>"
            f"<b>Dominant Pattern:</b> {escape(str(result.overall_pattern))}"
            "</div>"
        )

    if result.context_info:
        parts.append(
            "<div class='sl-context-box'>"
            f"{htmlize_multiline(str(result.context_info))}"
            "</div>"
        )

    if result.overall_text:
        parts.append(
            "<div style='margin-top:6px; line-height:1.4;'>"
            f"{format_overall_text_html(result.overall_text)}"
            "</div>"
        )

    parts.append("</div>")

    for key in ORDERED_SECTION_KEYS:
        section = result.sections.get(key)
        if section is None:
            continue

        heading_color = color_for_signal(section.polarity, section.status)
        parts.append("<div class='sl-section'>")
        parts.append(
            f"<div style='color:{heading_color}; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:2px;'>"
            f"{escape(section.name)}</div>"
        )
        if section.note:
            parts.append(
                f"<div class='sl-section-note'>{htmlize_multiline(section.note)}</div>"
            )
        parts.append("</div>")

    parts.append("</div>")
    return "".join(parts)

--- Start of numerical_evidence.py ---
# numerical_evidence.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

"""Production base-credit identity/restatement correction, without reweighting.
Reuse the existing lineage graph. Shared ancestry alone never suppresses new
localization or a related measurement. Component formulas keep their explicit
scoring roles; this allocator does not treat them as independent observations.
"""
from math import fsum

from evidence_lineage import (
    Addition, Kind, build_lineage, numerical_restatement_sources,
)
from scorecard_integrity import ScorecardIntegrityError, finite_number


def allocate_base_evidence(sections, raw_rows, *, registry=None):
    """Return copied effective base rows and a deterministic allocation ledger.

    Object identity proves duplicate representation in this call. Equal-valued
    distinct objects do not prove observation identity. No IDs derived from
    memory addresses are persisted. Registered keys win identity ties.
    """
    lineage = build_lineage(sections, **({'registry': registry} if registry is not None else {}))
    nodes = {r.evidence_id.removeprefix('evidence:'): r for r in lineage}
    raw_rows = tuple(raw_rows)
    rows = {r['section']: dict(r) for r in raw_rows}
    if len(rows) != len(raw_rows):
        raise ScorecardIntegrityError('Duplicate base contribution identifiers.')
    representatives = {}
    for key in sorted(rows, key=lambda k: (not nodes[k].known, k)):
        representatives.setdefault(id(sections[key]), key)

    def ancestors(key):
        node = nodes[key]
        result = set()
        for reference in node.direct_dependencies + node.optional_dependencies:
            parent = reference.removeprefix('evidence:')
            result.add(parent)
            result.update(ancestors(parent))
        return result

    allocations = {}
    for key in sorted(rows, key=lambda k: (len(ancestors(k)), k)):
        row, node = rows[key], nodes[key]
        raw = finite_number(row['weight'], 'base:' + key)
        final, category, credited_by = raw, 'D', []
        reason = 'Related or unregistered observation retained provisionally; no independence claim.'
        unresolved = not node.known or node.kind != Kind.PRIMARY
        if node.known and node.kind == Kind.PRIMARY:
            category, reason = 'C', 'Distinct primary measurement retained at its existing status weight.'
        elif node.addition in {Addition.LOCALIZATION, Addition.RELATIONSHIP}:
            category, reason = 'C', 'Additional measured localization/relationship retained; shared ancestry is not redundancy.'
        elif key in {'boundary', 'subject_material_realism'} and node.known:
            category, reason = 'C', 'Additional contour/material properties retained under the existing scoring rules.'

        representative = representatives[id(sections[key])]
        sources = numerical_restatement_sources(key, sections[key].pattern)
        # A registered pure restatement can be added without a second lineage
        # system. It must have an actually credited ancestor in the same scope.
        if node.addition == Addition.RESTATEMENT:
            sources = tuple(sorted(ancestors(key)))
        for source in sources:
            source_row = rows.get(source)
            source_node = nodes.get(source)
            source_final = allocations.get(source, {}).get('credited_contribution', 0.0)
            if source == 'cross_signal':
                # Sibling contract above: its active overlap summary is the
                # canonical counted representation, even if parents were omitted.
                source_final = source_row['weight'] if source_row else 0.0
                if not source_row or sections[source].pattern not in {'partial_hotspot_convergence', 'strong_hotspot_convergence'}:
                    continue
            if (source_row and source_final > 0 and source_row['polarity'] == row['polarity']
                    and source_node.hypotheses == node.hypotheses
                    and set(node.measurement_ancestry) <= set(source_node.measurement_ancestry)):
                credited_by.append('evidence:' + source)
        if representative != key:
            final, category, unresolved = 0.0, 'A', False
            credited_by = ['evidence:' + representative]
            reason = 'Identical summary instance already represented by its canonical section key.'
        elif credited_by:
            final, category, unresolved = 0.0, 'B', False
            reason = 'Existing overlap/measurement already credited; this representation adds no positive measured property.'
        elif node.addition == Addition.RESTATEMENT and node.missing_dependencies:
            final, category, unresolved = 0.0, 'B', False
            reason = 'Pure restatement has no available upstream measurement to credit.'
        elif sources:
            category, unresolved = 'B', False
            reason = 'Single overlap/measurement representation retained; no other counted representation exists in this scope.'
        if node.missing_dependencies and final:
            unresolved = True
            reason += ' Raw parent summaries are missing; the observed summary contract remains provisional.'
        row['credited_weight'] = final
        allocations[key] = {
            'evidence_id': 'evidence:' + key, 'section': key,
            'raw_contribution': raw, 'measurement_ancestry': list(node.measurement_ancestry),
            'previously_credited_evidence': sorted(credited_by),
            'additional_information': node.addition.value,
            'classification': category, 'eligible_marginal_contribution': final,
            'credited_contribution': final, 'suppressed_contribution': raw - final,
            'reliability_multiplier': 1.0, 'final_adjusted_contribution': final,
            'adjustment_stage': 'base_before_aggregate_attenuation_penalties_and_floor',
            'hypotheses': list(node.hypotheses), 'polarity': row['polarity'],
            'missing_dependencies': list(node.missing_dependencies),
            'unresolved': unresolved, 'reason': reason,
        }
    effective = [rows[key] for key in sorted(rows)]
    audit = {'version': '2b4', 'contributions': [allocations[key] for key in sorted(allocations)],
             'unresolved_relationships': [allocations[key]['evidence_id'] for key in sorted(allocations)
                                          if allocations[key]['unresolved']],
             'aggregate_reconciliation': {}}
    for polarity in ('suspicious', 'benign'):
        selected = [r for r in effective if r['polarity'] == polarity]
        original_domains = {r['domain'] for r in selected if r['domain']}
        credited_domains = {r['domain'] for r in selected if r['domain'] and r['credited_weight'] > 0}
        audit['aggregate_reconciliation'][polarity] = {
            'raw_base_weight': fsum(r['weight'] for r in selected),
            'credited_base_weight': fsum(r['credited_weight'] for r in selected),
            'suppressed_base_weight': fsum(r['weight'] - r['credited_weight'] for r in selected),
            'raw_domain_bonus': max(0, len(original_domains) - 1) * 0.25,
            'credited_domain_bonus': max(0, len(credited_domains) - 1) * 0.25,
            'credited_domains': sorted(credited_domains),
        }
    return effective, audit


def add_fragment_component(ledger, audit, field, operand, reason):
    """Withhold the same confirmed restatement in its mechanism paths too.

    Local-support conversion then consumes the corrected support naturally;
    no post-aggregate subtraction or new conversion coefficient is needed.
    """
    duplicate = any(r['section'] == 'boundary' and r['classification'] == 'B'
                    and r['suppressed_contribution'] > 0 for r in audit['contributions'])
    ledger.add(field, 0.0 if duplicate else operand, reason)
    if duplicate:
        index = len(ledger.operations) - 1
        ledger.operations[index]['raw_operand'] = operand
        ledger.operations[index]['numerical_allocation'] = 'B:overlap_restatement'
        audit.setdefault('component_allocations', []).append({
            'ledger_index': index, 'field': field, 'evidence_id': 'evidence:boundary',
            'classification': 'B', 'raw_contribution': operand,
            'credited_contribution': 0.0, 'suppressed_contribution': operand,
            'reliability_multiplier': 1.0, 'final_adjusted_contribution': 0.0,
            'reason': 'Fragment fallback adds no positive measurement beyond the credited cross-overlap; mechanism restatement withheld.',
        })


def finalize_component_allocations(audit, operations):
    for row in audit.get('component_allocations', []):
        factor = 1.0
        for later in operations[row['ledger_index'] + 1:]:
            if later['field'] == row['field'] and later['operation'] == 'multiply':
                factor *= later['operand']
        row['reliability_multiplier'] = factor
        row['final_adjusted_contribution'] = row['credited_contribution'] * factor

--- End of numerical_evidence.py ---

#osint_dialogs.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages#

from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Optional
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QComboBox, QDialog, QDialogButtonBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QFormLayout, QLineEdit, QMessageBox, QPlainTextEdit, QVBoxLayout
from osint_reverse_search import ReverseSearchMatch

class OSINTFindingDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, existing_match: Optional[ReverseSearchMatch] = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("OSINT Finding")
        self.setModal(True)
        self.resize(520, 420)

        self.provider_combo = QComboBox()
        self.provider_combo.addItems(["google", "yandex", "tineye", "bing", "other"])

        self.page_url_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.title_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.image_url_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.first_seen_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.source_type_combo = QComboBox()
        self.source_type_combo.addItems([
            "unknown",
            "stock",
            "ai_gallery",
            "social",
            "news",
            "marketplace",
            "other",
        ])

        self.similarity_spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.similarity_spin.setRange(0.0, 1.0)
        self.similarity_spin.setSingleStep(0.01)
        self.similarity_spin.setDecimals(2)
        self.similarity_spin.setSpecialValueText("n/a")
        self.similarity_spin.setValue(0.0)

        self.notes_edit = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.notes_edit.setPlaceholderText(
            "Example: Higher-resolution version found; likely original stock upload."
        )

        form = QFormLayout()
        form.addRow("Provider", self.provider_combo)
        form.addRow("Result page URL *", self.page_url_edit)
        form.addRow("Title", self.title_edit)
        form.addRow("Source image URL", self.image_url_edit)
        form.addRow("Similarity (0.00 - 1.00)", self.similarity_spin)
        form.addRow("First-seen estimate", self.first_seen_edit)
        form.addRow("Source type", self.source_type_combo)
        form.addRow("Notes", self.notes_edit)

        buttons = QDialogButtonBox(
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Save | QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel
        )
        buttons.accepted.connect(self._on_accept)
        buttons.rejected.connect(self.reject)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.addLayout(form)
        layout.addWidget(buttons)

        if existing_match is not None:
            self.set_from_match(existing_match)

    def set_from_match(self, match: ReverseSearchMatch) -> None:
        self._set_combo_value(self.provider_combo, match.provider or "other")
        self.page_url_edit.setText(match.page_url or "")
        self.title_edit.setText(match.title or "")
        self.image_url_edit.setText(match.image_url or "")
        self.first_seen_edit.setText(match.first_seen_estimate or "")
        self._set_combo_value(self.source_type_combo, match.source_type or "unknown")
        self.similarity_spin.setValue(match.similarity_score if match.similarity_score is not None else 0.0)
        self.notes_edit.setPlainText(match.notes or "")

    def _set_combo_value(self, combo: QComboBox, value: str) -> None:
        index = combo.findText(value, Qt.MatchFlag.MatchFixedString)
        if index >= 0:
            combo.setCurrentIndex(index)
        else:
            combo.setCurrentText(value)

    def _on_accept(self) -> None:
        if not self.page_url_edit.text().strip():
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Missing URL", "Result page URL is required.")
            return
        self.accept()

    def get_data(self) -> dict:
        sim = self.similarity_spin.value()
        similarity_score = None if sim == 0.0 else sim

        return {
            "provider": self.provider_combo.currentText().strip(),
            "page_url": self.page_url_edit.text().strip(),
            "title": self.title_edit.text().strip(),
            "image_url": self.image_url_edit.text().strip(),
            "similarity_score": similarity_score,
            "first_seen_estimate": self.first_seen_edit.text().strip() or None,
            "source_type": self.source_type_combo.currentText().strip(),
            "notes": self.notes_edit.toPlainText().strip(),
        }

#osint_reverse_search.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages#
#
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
import mimetypes
import os
import webbrowser
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import urlparse

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# dataclasses
# ------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class ReverseSearchMatch:
    provider: str
    title: str = ""
    page_url: str = ""
    image_url: str = ""
    thumbnail_url: str = ""
    domain: str = ""
    source_type: str = "unknown"   # unknown / stock / ai_gallery / social / news / marketplace / other
    similarity_score: Optional[float] = None
    first_seen_estimate: Optional[str] = None
    notes: str = ""


@dataclass
class ReverseSearchQueryLinks:
    google_lens_url: str = "https://lens.google.com/"
    google_images_url: str = "https://images.google.com/"
    yandex_url: str = "https://yandex.com/images/"
    tineye_url: str = "https://tineye.com/"
    bing_visual_url: str = "https://www.bing.com/visualsearch"


@dataclass
class ReverseSearchResult:
    success: bool
    image_path: str
    image_sha256: str
    file_size_bytes: int
    mime_type: str
    started_at_utc: str
    completed_at_utc: str

    mode: str = "manual"
    analyst_reviewed: bool = False
    analyst_notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    providers_attempted: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    providers_succeeded: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    providers_failed: List[Dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

    query_links: ReverseSearchQueryLinks = field(default_factory=ReverseSearchQueryLinks)
    matches: List[ReverseSearchMatch] = field(default_factory=list)

    matches_found: bool = False
    best_match_url: str = ""
    best_match_title: str = ""
    best_match_similarity: Optional[float] = None

    possible_original_found: bool = False
    derivative_detected: bool = False
    ai_source_context_detected: bool = False
    stock_source_context_detected: bool = False
    news_source_context_detected: bool = False

    dominant_source_type: str = "unknown"
    first_seen_estimate: Optional[str] = None
    manual_entry_count: int = 0

    review_status: str = "not_reviewed"  # not_reviewed / reviewed_no_match / reviewed_matches_found
    summary: str = ""
    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# utils
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat()


def sha256_of_file(path: str | Path, chunk_size: int = 1024 * 1024) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            chunk = f.read(chunk_size)
            if not chunk:
                break
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def guess_mime_type(path: str | Path) -> str:
    mime, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(str(path))
    return mime or "application/octet-stream"


def ensure_dir(path: str | Path) -> Path:
    p = Path(path)
    p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return p


def save_json(data: Dict[str, Any], out_path: str | Path) -> None:
    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)


def load_json(path: str | Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def domain_from_url(url: str) -> str:
    try:
        return urlparse(url).netloc.lower()
    except Exception:
        return ""


def classify_source_type(url: str, title: str = "") -> str:
    hay = f"{url} {title}".lower()

    ai_markers = [
        "midjourney", "lexica", "civitai", "playgroundai",
        "openart", "stable diffusion", "dall-e", "firefly",
        "prompt", "dreamina", "mage.space"
    ]
    stock_markers = [
        "shutterstock", "gettyimages", "adobestock", "adobe stock",
        "istockphoto", "alamy", "dreamstime", "depositphotos",
        "pexels", "unsplash"
    ]
    social_markers = [
        "instagram", "reddit", "pinterest", "facebook",
        "x.com", "twitter", "tiktok", "imgur"
    ]
    news_markers = [
        "reuters", "apnews", "bbc", "cnn", "nytimes", "theguardian"
    ]
    marketplace_markers = [
        "etsy", "ebay", "amazon"
    ]

    if any(m in hay for m in ai_markers):
        return "ai_gallery"
    if any(m in hay for m in stock_markers):
        return "stock"
    if any(m in hay for m in social_markers):
        return "social"
    if any(m in hay for m in news_markers):
        return "news"
    if any(m in hay for m in marketplace_markers):
        return "marketplace"
    return "unknown"


def _sort_key(match: ReverseSearchMatch) -> float:
    return match.similarity_score if match.similarity_score is not None else -1.0


def summarize_matches(matches: List[ReverseSearchMatch], analyst_reviewed: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not matches:
        if analyst_reviewed:
            return {
                "matches_found": False,
                "best_match_url": "",
                "best_match_title": "",
                "best_match_similarity": None,
                "possible_original_found": False,
                "derivative_detected": False,
                "ai_source_context_detected": False,
                "stock_source_context_detected": False,
                "news_source_context_detected": False,
                "dominant_source_type": "unknown",
                "first_seen_estimate": None,
                "manual_entry_count": 0,
                "review_status": "reviewed_no_match",
                "summary": "Manual reverse-image search was reviewed, but no meaningful online match was documented."
            }

        return {
            "matches_found": False,
            "best_match_url": "",
            "best_match_title": "",
            "best_match_similarity": None,
            "possible_original_found": False,
            "derivative_detected": False,
            "ai_source_context_detected": False,
            "stock_source_context_detected": False,
            "news_source_context_detected": False,
            "dominant_source_type": "unknown",
            "first_seen_estimate": None,
            "manual_entry_count": 0,
            "review_status": "not_reviewed",
            "summary": "No reverse-image OSINT review has yet been recorded for this image."
        }

    ranked = sorted(matches, key=_sort_key, reverse=True)
    best = ranked[0]

    source_type_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for m in matches:
        source_type_counts[m.source_type] = source_type_counts.get(m.source_type, 0) + 1

    dominant_source_type = max(source_type_counts, key=source_type_counts.get)
    ai_source_context_detected = any(m.source_type == "ai_gallery" for m in matches)
    stock_source_context_detected = any(m.source_type == "stock" for m in matches)
    news_source_context_detected = any(m.source_type == "news" for m in matches)

    date_candidates = [m.first_seen_estimate for m in matches if m.first_seen_estimate]
    first_seen_estimate = min(date_candidates) if date_candidates else None

    best_sim = best.similarity_score
    possible_original_found = best_sim is not None and best_sim >= 0.90
    derivative_detected = any(
        m.similarity_score is not None and 0.55 <= m.similarity_score < 0.90
        for m in matches
    )

    parts = [f"{len(matches)} reverse-image match(es) documented."]
    parts.append(f"Best match source type: {best.source_type}.")
    if best_sim is not None:
        parts.append(f"Best normalized similarity: {best_sim:.2f}.")
    if first_seen_estimate:
        parts.append(f"Earliest observed date estimate: {first_seen_estimate}.")
    if possible_original_found:
        parts.append("A likely original or near-original online version may have been identified.")
    elif derivative_detected:
        parts.append("Online matches suggest derivative or related image reuse.")
    if ai_source_context_detected:
        parts.append("AI-related online source context detected.")
    if stock_source_context_detected:
        parts.append("Stock-image source context detected.")

    return {
        "matches_found": True,
        "best_match_url": best.page_url,
        "best_match_title": best.title,
        "best_match_similarity": best.similarity_score,
        "possible_original_found": possible_original_found,
        "derivative_detected": derivative_detected,
        "ai_source_context_detected": ai_source_context_detected,
        "stock_source_context_detected": stock_source_context_detected,
        "news_source_context_detected": news_source_context_detected,
        "dominant_source_type": dominant_source_type,
        "first_seen_estimate": first_seen_estimate,
        "manual_entry_count": len(matches),
        "review_status": "reviewed_matches_found",
        "summary": " ".join(parts)
    }


def dataclass_to_jsonable(result: ReverseSearchResult) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return asdict(result)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# persistence
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def create_empty_osint_result(image_path: str | Path) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    image_path = str(Path(image_path).resolve())
    if not os.path.isfile(image_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Image not found: {image_path}")

    now = utc_now_iso()
    return ReverseSearchResult(
        success=True,
        image_path=image_path,
        image_sha256=sha256_of_file(image_path),
        file_size_bytes=os.path.getsize(image_path),
        mime_type=guess_mime_type(image_path),
        started_at_utc=now,
        completed_at_utc=now,
        mode="manual",
        analyst_reviewed=False,
        analyst_notes=[],
        summary="No reverse-image OSINT review has yet been recorded for this image."
    )


def save_osint_result(result: ReverseSearchResult, out_dir: str | Path) -> Path:
    out_dir = ensure_dir(out_dir)
    json_path = Path(out_dir) / "osint_results.json"
    result.completed_at_utc = utc_now_iso()
    save_json(dataclass_to_jsonable(result), json_path)
    return json_path


def load_osint_result(json_path: str | Path) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    raw = load_json(json_path)

    query_links = ReverseSearchQueryLinks(**raw.get("query_links", {}))
    matches = [ReverseSearchMatch(**m) for m in raw.get("matches", [])]

    result = ReverseSearchResult(
        success=raw.get("success", True),
        image_path=raw.get("image_path", ""),
        image_sha256=raw.get("image_sha256", ""),
        file_size_bytes=raw.get("file_size_bytes", 0),
        mime_type=raw.get("mime_type", "application/octet-stream"),
        started_at_utc=raw.get("started_at_utc", utc_now_iso()),
        completed_at_utc=raw.get("completed_at_utc", utc_now_iso()),
        mode=raw.get("mode", "manual"),
        analyst_reviewed=raw.get("analyst_reviewed", False),
        analyst_notes=raw.get("analyst_notes", []),
        providers_attempted=raw.get("providers_attempted", []),
        providers_succeeded=raw.get("providers_succeeded", []),
        providers_failed=raw.get("providers_failed", []),
        query_links=query_links,
        matches=matches,
        matches_found=raw.get("matches_found", False),
        best_match_url=raw.get("best_match_url", ""),
        best_match_title=raw.get("best_match_title", ""),
        best_match_similarity=raw.get("best_match_similarity", None),
        possible_original_found=raw.get("possible_original_found", False),
        derivative_detected=raw.get("derivative_detected", False),
        ai_source_context_detected=raw.get("ai_source_context_detected", False),
        stock_source_context_detected=raw.get("stock_source_context_detected", False),
        news_source_context_detected=raw.get("news_source_context_detected", False),
        dominant_source_type=raw.get("dominant_source_type", "unknown"),
        first_seen_estimate=raw.get("first_seen_estimate", None),
        manual_entry_count=raw.get("manual_entry_count", 0),
        review_status=raw.get("review_status", "not_reviewed"),
        summary=raw.get("summary", ""),
        notes=raw.get("notes", []),
    )
    return result


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# actions
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def initialize_osint_for_image(image_path: str | Path, out_dir: str | Path) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    result = create_empty_osint_result(image_path)
    save_osint_result(result, out_dir)
    return result


def open_reverse_search(provider: str, result: ReverseSearchResult | None = None) -> str:
    provider = provider.lower().strip()

    if result is not None:
        result.providers_attempted.append(provider)

    mapping = {
        "google": "https://lens.google.com/",
        "google_lens": "https://lens.google.com/",
        "google_images": "https://images.google.com/",
        "yandex": "https://yandex.com/images/",
        "tineye": "https://tineye.com/",
        "bing": "https://www.bing.com/visualsearch",
    }

    url = mapping.get(provider)
    if not url:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown reverse-search provider: {provider}")

    webbrowser.open_new_tab(url)
    return url


def mark_reviewed_no_match(result: ReverseSearchResult) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    result.analyst_reviewed = True
    summary_fields = summarize_matches(result.matches, result.analyst_reviewed)
    _apply_summary_fields(result, summary_fields)
    return result


def add_manual_match(
    result: ReverseSearchResult,
    provider: str,
    page_url: str,
    title: str = "",
    image_url: str = "",
    thumbnail_url: str = "",
    similarity_score: Optional[float] = None,
    first_seen_estimate: Optional[str] = None,
    source_type: str = "unknown",
    notes: str = "",
) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    inferred_source_type = source_type
    if inferred_source_type == "unknown":
        inferred_source_type = classify_source_type(page_url, title)

    match = ReverseSearchMatch(
        provider=provider,
        title=title.strip(),
        page_url=page_url.strip(),
        image_url=image_url.strip(),
        thumbnail_url=thumbnail_url.strip(),
        domain=domain_from_url(page_url),
        source_type=inferred_source_type,
        similarity_score=similarity_score,
        first_seen_estimate=first_seen_estimate.strip() if first_seen_estimate else None,
        notes=notes.strip(),
    )

    result.matches.append(match)
    result.analyst_reviewed = True

    summary_fields = summarize_matches(result.matches, result.analyst_reviewed)
    _apply_summary_fields(result, summary_fields)
    return result


def replace_match(
    result: ReverseSearchResult,
    index: int,
    provider: str,
    page_url: str,
    title: str = "",
    image_url: str = "",
    thumbnail_url: str = "",
    similarity_score: Optional[float] = None,
    first_seen_estimate: Optional[str] = None,
    source_type: str = "unknown",
    notes: str = "",
) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    if not (0 <= index < len(result.matches)):
        raise IndexError("Match index out of range.")

    inferred_source_type = source_type
    if inferred_source_type == "unknown":
        inferred_source_type = classify_source_type(page_url, title)

    result.matches[index] = ReverseSearchMatch(
        provider=provider,
        title=title.strip(),
        page_url=page_url.strip(),
        image_url=image_url.strip(),
        thumbnail_url=thumbnail_url.strip(),
        domain=domain_from_url(page_url),
        source_type=inferred_source_type,
        similarity_score=similarity_score,
        first_seen_estimate=first_seen_estimate.strip() if first_seen_estimate else None,
        notes=notes.strip(),
    )

    result.analyst_reviewed = True
    summary_fields = summarize_matches(result.matches, result.analyst_reviewed)
    _apply_summary_fields(result, summary_fields)
    return result


def delete_match(result: ReverseSearchResult, index: int) -> ReverseSearchResult:
    if not (0 <= index < len(result.matches)):
        raise IndexError("Match index out of range.")

    del result.matches[index]
    summary_fields = summarize_matches(result.matches, result.analyst_reviewed)
    _apply_summary_fields(result, summary_fields)
    return result


def _apply_summary_fields(result: ReverseSearchResult, summary_fields: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    result.matches_found = summary_fields["matches_found"]
    result.best_match_url = summary_fields["best_match_url"]
    result.best_match_title = summary_fields["best_match_title"]
    result.best_match_similarity = summary_fields["best_match_similarity"]
    result.possible_original_found = summary_fields["possible_original_found"]
    result.derivative_detected = summary_fields["derivative_detected"]
    result.ai_source_context_detected = summary_fields["ai_source_context_detected"]
    result.stock_source_context_detected = summary_fields["stock_source_context_detected"]
    result.news_source_context_detected = summary_fields["news_source_context_detected"]
    result.dominant_source_type = summary_fields["dominant_source_type"]
    result.first_seen_estimate = summary_fields["first_seen_estimate"]
    result.manual_entry_count = summary_fields["manual_entry_count"]
    result.review_status = summary_fields["review_status"]
    result.summary = summary_fields["summary"]
    result.completed_at_utc = utc_now_iso()


def format_match_label(match: ReverseSearchMatch) -> str:
    sim_txt = "n/a" if match.similarity_score is None else f"{match.similarity_score:.2f}"
    domain = match.domain or "-"
    source_type = match.source_type or "unknown"
    provider = match.provider or "unknown"
    return f"[{provider}] {source_type} | sim={sim_txt} | {domain}"


def build_osint_summary_text(result: ReverseSearchResult | None) -> str:
    if result is None:
        return "OSINT: no data"

    if result.review_status == "not_reviewed":
        return "OSINT: not reviewed yet"

    if result.review_status == "reviewed_no_match":
        return "OSINT: reviewed, no documented match"

    if result.review_status == "reviewed_matches_found":
        parts = [f"OSINT: {result.manual_entry_count} finding(s)"]
        if result.dominant_source_type and result.dominant_source_type != "unknown":
            parts.append(f"dominant={result.dominant_source_type}")
        if result.best_match_similarity is not None:
            parts.append(f"best={result.best_match_similarity:.2f}")
        return " | ".join(parts)

    return "OSINT: status unknown"
--- End of osint_reverse_search.py ---

# production_corroboration.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

"""Production corroboration semantics over Phase 2B.2 lineage.
Measurement-disjoint witnesses are a structural diversity check, never a test
of statistical independence. Compound mechanisms retain related interpretations.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from math import isfinite
from numbers import Real

from evidence_lineage import Addition, Family, Kind, LineageError, build_lineage, lineage_from_json, lineage_to_json


class RequirementKind(str, Enum):
    DISTINCT_SOURCES = 'distinct_measurement_sources'
    COMPOUND = 'compound_interpretations'
    ACCUMULATION = 'general_accumulation'


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CorroborationRequirement:
    gate_id: str
    hypothesis: str
    kind: RequirementKind
    minimum_indicators: int = 1
    minimum_sources: int = 1
    distinct_families: bool = False

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.gate_id or not self.hypothesis or not isinstance(self.kind,RequirementKind):
            raise LineageError('A gate needs an identifier, hypothesis and declared semantics.')
        if any(type(v) is not int or v<1 for v in (self.minimum_indicators,self.minimum_sources)) or type(self.distinct_families) is not bool:
            raise LineageError('Corroboration requirements must be positive integer counts and a boolean family rule.')


CONTROL_ANCESTRY = frozenset({'measurement:selected_mask_geometry'})
ACTIVE = frozenset({'weak','medium','strong'})


def _valid_observation(section):
    if section is None or section.status not in ACTIVE:
        return False
    score=section.score
    return score is None or (not isinstance(score,bool) and isinstance(score,Real) and isfinite(score))


def _disjoint_witnesses(candidates, require_families):
    """Largest disjoint subset, deterministic tie-break; never greedy clustering.

    Adding a composite AB must not collapse genuinely separate primary A and B.
    Identical ancestry/family candidates can be coalesced for the search only.
    """
    unique={}
    for key,ancestry,family in sorted(candidates):
        token=(ancestry,family if require_families else '')
        unique.setdefault(token,(key,ancestry,family))
    rows=sorted(unique.values())
    best=()
    def search(index,chosen,used_measurements,used_families):
        nonlocal best
        if len(chosen)>len(best) or (len(chosen)==len(best) and chosen<best):
            best=chosen
        if len(chosen)+len(rows)-index<len(best):
            return
        for i in range(index,len(rows)):
            key,ancestry,family=rows[i]
            if not used_measurements.intersection(ancestry) and (not require_families or family not in used_families):
                search(i+1,chosen+(key,),used_measurements.union(ancestry),used_families|{family})
    search(0,(),set(),set())
    return best


def evaluate_corroboration(sections, evidence_ids, requirement, *, lineage=None, roles=None):
    """Pure hypothesis-scoped source/compound assessment; never mutates evidence.

    An observed compound summary may remain interpretable when callers omit its
    raw parent summaries. Such provisional lineage cannot count as a distinct
    measurement source. This preserves legacy compound-summary APIs without
    inventing upstream observations.
    """
    records=lineage_from_json(lineage_to_json(tuple(lineage))) if lineage is not None else build_lineage(sections)
    nodes={record.evidence_id:record for record in records}
    if len(nodes)!=len(records):
        raise LineageError('Duplicate lineage identifiers in corroboration input.')
    considered=tuple(sorted(set(evidence_ids)))
    eligible=[]; excluded={}; provisional={}; increments=[]; candidates=[]
    limitations=[]; all_ancestry={}; role_map={}
    for key in considered:
        node=nodes.get(key)
        section=sections.get(key.removeprefix('evidence:'))
        role=(roles or {}).get(key,'supporting_evidence')
        role_map[key]=role
        if not node or not node.known:
            excluded[key]='Unknown lineage cannot establish corroboration.'
            continue
        if role not in {'supporting_evidence','contradicting_evidence','moderating_evidence','contextual_evidence'}:
            raise LineageError('Unknown hypothesis evidence role.')
        if role!='supporting_evidence':
            excluded[key]='Retained as '+role+'; not counted as supporting corroboration.'
            continue
        if requirement.hypothesis!='unresolved_anomaly' and requirement.hypothesis not in node.hypotheses:
            excluded[key]='The observation is outside this hypothesis scope.'
            continue
        expected='benign' if requirement.hypothesis=='camera_acquisition' else 'suspicious'
        if not _valid_observation(section) or not node.available or section.polarity!=expected:
            excluded[key]='No valid available supporting observation in this hypothesis polarity.'
            continue
        roots=tuple(sorted(set(node.measurement_ancestry)-CONTROL_ANCESTRY))
        all_ancestry[key]=roots
        if node.addition in {Addition.LOCALIZATION,Addition.RELATIONSHIP,Addition.INTERPRETATION}:
            increments.append({'evidence_id':key,'addition':node.addition.value,
                'new_measurement_claim':bool(node.kind==Kind.PRIMARY),
                'numeric_weight_changed':False})
        complete=not node.missing_dependencies and bool(roots)
        if not complete:
            provisional[key]=list(node.missing_dependencies) or ['No non-control measurement ancestry is established.']
        if requirement.kind==RequirementKind.DISTINCT_SOURCES and not complete:
            excluded[key]='Required measurement ancestry is missing or provisional.'
            continue
        eligible.append(key)
        if complete:
            candidates.append((key,roots,node.family.value))
    selected=_disjoint_witnesses(candidates,requirement.distinct_families)
    shared={}
    for root in sorted({r for roots in all_ancestry.values() for r in roots}):
        ids=[key for key,roots in sorted(all_ancestry.items()) if root in roots]
        if len(ids)>1:
            shared[root]=ids
    overlapping={}
    for key in eligible:
        for group in nodes[key].overlap_groups:
            if group!='selected_geometry': overlapping.setdefault(group,[]).append(key)
    overlapping={k:v for k,v in sorted(overlapping.items()) if len(v)>1}
    satisfied=len(eligible)>=requirement.minimum_indicators
    if requirement.kind==RequirementKind.DISTINCT_SOURCES:
        satisfied=satisfied and len(selected)>=requirement.minimum_sources
        explanation=(f'{len(selected)} measurement-disjoint supporting indicator(s) satisfy' if satisfied else
            f'{len(selected)} measurement-disjoint supporting indicator(s) do not satisfy')+f' the existing {requirement.minimum_sources}-source diversity requirement.'
    else:
        explanation=f'{len(eligible)} observed supporting indicator(s) '+('satisfy' if satisfied else 'do not satisfy')+f' the {requirement.minimum_indicators}-indicator {requirement.kind.value} condition; shared ancestry is allowed and is not independent confirmation.'
    if provisional:
        limitations.append('Provisional compound summaries do not establish missing upstream measurements or additional distinct sources.')
    if overlapping:
        limitations.append('Feature-definition/content overlap remains; disjoint extraction IDs do not demonstrate statistical independence.')
    limitations.append('Source groups are structural measurement ancestry, not probabilities or statistically independent samples.')
    return {'gate_id':requirement.gate_id,'hypothesis':requirement.hypothesis,
        'requirement_kind':requirement.kind.value,
        'minimum_indicators':requirement.minimum_indicators,'minimum_sources':requirement.minimum_sources if requirement.kind==RequirementKind.DISTINCT_SOURCES else None,
        'distinct_families_required':requirement.distinct_families,
        'considered_evidence':list(considered),'eligible_evidence':sorted(eligible),
        'excluded_evidence':excluded,'evidence_roles':role_map,
        'distinct_ancestry_groups':[{'witness':key,'measurements':list(all_ancestry[key]),'family':nodes[key].family.value} for key in selected],
        'corroborating_source_count':len(selected),'shared_ancestry':shared,
        'overlapping_feature_groups':overlapping,'incremental_support_findings':increments,
        'provisional_lineage':provisional,'requirement_satisfied':satisfied,
        'explanation':explanation,'limitations':limitations}


def record_gate(collector, result):
    """Replace an existing gate result, avoiding repeated-evaluation state growth."""
    if collector is not None:
        collector[:]=[entry for entry in collector if entry['gate_id']!=result['gate_id']]+[result]
        collector.sort(key=lambda entry:entry['gate_id'])
    return result

# reasoning_persistence.py
#signallens_gui.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images

from __future__ import annotations
from hypothesis_evidence import ROLE_TITLES, evidence_presentation
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

def persist_reasoning_trace(reasoning_trace: dict[str, Any] | None, output_dir: str | Path) -> str | None:
    if not reasoning_trace:
        return None
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    summary_dir = output_dir / "summary"
    summary_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    trace_path = summary_dir / "reasoning_trace.json"
    trace_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(reasoning_trace, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return str(trace_path)

def _fmt_scalar(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return f"{value:.4f}"
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "true" if value else "false"
    if value is None:
        return "null"
    return str(value)

def _fmt_scalar(value):
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return f"{value:.4f}"
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "true" if value else "false"
    if value is None:
        return "null"
    return str(value)

def _filter_supporting_for_trace(items: list[str] | None, polarity: str | None, ) -> list[str]:
    if not items:
        return []
    benign_camera_patterns = {
        "camera_metadata_present",
        "weak_camera_pipeline_supported",
        "weak_camera_cfa_supported",
    }
    if polarity in {"neutral", "suspicious"}:
        return [p for p in items if p not in benign_camera_patterns]
    return items

#========================================================================
# # formatting helpers
#========================================================================
def _add_heading(lines: list[str], title: str) -> None:
    lines.append(title)
    lines.append("-" * len(title))

def _add_kv(lines: list[str], key: str, value, indent: int = 0) -> None:
    pad = " " * indent
    lines.append(f"{pad}- {key}: {_fmt_scalar(value)}")

def _add_list(lines: list[str], title: str, items: list[str] | None) -> None:
    if not items:
        return
    lines.append(title)
    for item in items:
        lines.append(f"- {item}")
    lines.append("")

#=========================================================================
# reasoning explanation helpers
# showing how and why the main decision was made 
#=========================================================================
def _build_decision_win_explanation(reasoning_trace: dict) -> list[str]:
    decision = reasoning_trace.get("decision", {}) or {}
    if decision.get("selection_reason"):
        return [decision["selection_reason"]]
    scorecard = reasoning_trace.get("scorecard", {}) or {}
    derived = reasoning_trace.get("derived_signals", {}) or {}

    lines: list[str] = []
    branch = decision.get("decision_branch")
    local_edit = scorecard.get("local_edit_support_score")
    clone = scorecard.get("clone_support_score")
    suspicious = scorecard.get("suspicious_score")
    camera_counter = scorecard.get("camera_counter_support")
    fp_risk = scorecard.get("contextual_false_positive_risk")
    patch_pattern = reasoning_trace["section_snapshot"].get("patch_similarity", {}).get("pattern")
    patch_spatial_pattern = reasoning_trace["section_snapshot"].get("patch_spatial", {}).get("pattern")
    # meta-rule: strong anomaly without stable localized mechanism
    if (
        suspicious is not None
        and suspicious >= 3.5
        and not derived.get("strong_local_edit")
        and not derived.get("patch_structured")
    ):
        lines.append(
            "strong anomaly signals exist, but no stable structural mechanism (localized editing or structured recurrence) is identified"
        )    
    if derived.get("boundary_reliability_score") is not None:
        br = float(derived.get("boundary_reliability_score") or 0.0)
        if br < 1.0:
            lines.append(
                f"boundary reliability is reduced ({_fmt_scalar(br)}), so boundary-driven signals are not treated as fully decisive"
            )                        
    if branch:        
        if branch == "possible_foreground_background_processing_mismatch":
            if local_edit is not None:
                lines.append(f"local_edit_support_score is elevated ({_fmt_scalar(local_edit)})")
            if clone is not None:
                lines.append(f"clone_support_score is lower ({_fmt_scalar(clone)}), so a clone-reuse explanation is weaker")
            if camera_counter is not None:
                lines.append(f"camera_counter_support remains limited ({_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)})")
            if fp_risk is not None:
                lines.append(f"contextual_false_positive_risk is {_fmt_scalar(fp_risk)}")
            elif branch == "localized_editing_multi_signal_convergence":
                if suspicious is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"suspicious_score is high enough for a multi-signal suspicious reading ({_fmt_scalar(suspicious)})"
                    )

                edit_boost = scorecard.get("edit_boost")
                if edit_boost is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"edit_boost contributes meaningfully to the final score ({_fmt_scalar(edit_boost)})"
                    )

                if derived.get("strong_cross") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"strong_cross = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('strong_cross'))}, indicating localized ELA/residual convergence"
                    )

                if derived.get("strong_local_edit") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"strong_local_edit = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('strong_local_edit'))}; localized editing is supported, but not at the strongest branch threshold"
                    )

                if local_edit is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"local_edit_support_score = {_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}, below the high-confidence localized-edit threshold but sufficient as moderate support"
                    )

                if camera_counter is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"camera_counter_support = {_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)}, so camera metadata moderates but does not overturn the suspicious reading"
                    )

                if fp_risk is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"contextual_false_positive_risk = {_fmt_scalar(fp_risk)}"
                    )                
                    
                if patch_pattern == "possible_clone_reuse" and clone == 0:
                    lines.append(
                        "patch similarity flagged possible clone reuse, but clone_support_score remains low after spatial/contextual moderation"                    
                    )
            elif branch == "real_photo_context_moderates_suspicion":
                lines.append(
                    "camera metadata and analyst-provided smartphone computational-photography context form a strong real-photo prior"
                )

                if derived.get("person_mask_reliable"):
                    lines.append(
                        "person-aware segmentation is reliable, so boundary and region signals are geometrically meaningful"
                    )

                if derived.get("boundary_reliability_score") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"boundary_reliability_score = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('boundary_reliability_score'))}, "
                        "so boundary-driven signals are considered but not treated as decisive"
                    )

                if scorecard is not None:
                    cam = getattr(scorecard, "camera_counter_support", None)
                    if cam is not None:
                        lines.append(
                            f"camera_counter_support = {_fmt_scalar(cam)}, materially offsetting suspicious signals"
                        )

                if local_edit is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"local_edit_support_score = {_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}, but interpreted as consistent with smartphone computational processing rather than compositing"
                    )
            elif branch == "localized_processing_inconclusive":
                if suspicious is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"suspicious_score is elevated ({_fmt_scalar(suspicious)}), but not enough to override moderating evidence decisively"
                    )
                if scorecard.get("edit_boost") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"edit_boost is present ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('edit_boost'))}), supporting localized differential processing"
                    )
                if derived.get("strong_cross") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"strong_cross = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('strong_cross'))}, so ELA/residual convergence supports localized processing"
                    )
                if local_edit is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"local_edit_support_score is moderate ({_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}), below the threshold for a strong localized-edit conclusion"
                    )
                if scorecard.get("clone_support_score") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"clone_support_score is low ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('clone_support_score'))}), so clone-reuse is not strongly supported despite patch similarity"
                    )                                        
                if scorecard.get("camera_counter_support") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"camera_counter_support is meaningful ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('camera_counter_support'))}), moderating the suspicious interpretation"
                    )
                if derived.get("strong_local_edit") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"strong_local_edit = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('strong_local_edit'))}, so the system avoids a decisive localized-edit verdict"
                    )
                if fp_risk is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"contextual_false_positive_risk = {_fmt_scalar(fp_risk)}"
                    )                    
                if scorecard.get("caution_penalty") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"caution_penalty is applied ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('caution_penalty'))}), reflecting ambiguity in mechanism attribution"
                    )                    
            elif branch == "boundary_support_limited_localized_processing":
                if derived.get("boundary_reliability_score") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"boundary_reliability_score = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('boundary_reliability_score'))}, "
                        "so boundary-dependent evidence is treated cautiously"
                    )
                if derived.get("boundary_support_ratio") is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"boundary_support_ratio = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('boundary_support_ratio'))}, "
                        "indicating limited usable boundary support"
                    )
                if local_edit is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"local_edit_support_score = {_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}, but the score is moderated by boundary reliability"
                    )
                if camera_counter is not None:
                    lines.append(
                        f"camera_counter_support = {_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)}, further moderating the localized-processing interpretation"
                    )
        elif branch == "region_geometry_unreliable_localized_processing":
            if derived.get("region_geometry_unreliable") is not None:
                lines.append(
                    f"region_geometry_unreliable = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('region_geometry_unreliable'))}, "
                    "so subject/boundary-dependent signals are treated cautiously"
                )
            if suspicious is not None:
                lines.append(
                    f"suspicious_score remains elevated ({_fmt_scalar(suspicious)}), but the unreliable region split prevents a strong compositing verdict"
                )
            if local_edit is not None:
                lines.append(
                    f"local_edit_support_score is elevated ({_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}), but depends on unstable subject/boundary geometry"
                )
            if scorecard.get("caution_penalty") is not None:
                lines.append(
                    f"caution_penalty is increased ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('caution_penalty'))}) to reflect unreliable region geometry"
                )
            if camera_counter is not None:
                lines.append(
                    f"camera_counter_support = {_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)}, further moderating the suspicious interpretation"
                )                    
        elif branch == "possible_clone_reuse":
            if clone is not None:
                lines.append(f"clone_support_score is elevated ({_fmt_scalar(clone)})")
            if local_edit is not None:
                lines.append(f"local_edit_support_score is lower ({_fmt_scalar(local_edit)}), so localized editing is less favored")
            if camera_counter is not None:
                lines.append(f"camera_counter_support remains limited ({_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)})")
        elif branch == "synthetic_multi_signal_convergence":
            if suspicious is not None:
                lines.append(f"suspicious_score is high ({_fmt_scalar(suspicious)})")
            if derived.get("fft_structured") is not None:
                lines.append(f"fft_structured = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('fft_structured'))}")
            if derived.get("patch_structured") is not None:
                lines.append(f"patch_structured = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('patch_structured'))}")
            if derived.get("camera_like_support") is not None:
                lines.append(f"camera_like_support = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('camera_like_support'))}")
        elif branch == "photo_like_consistency_supported":
            if suspicious is not None:
                lines.append(f"suspicious_score remains low ({_fmt_scalar(suspicious)})")
            if scorecard.get("benign_score") is not None:
                lines.append(f"benign_score is supportive ({_fmt_scalar(scorecard.get('benign_score'))})")
            if derived.get("camera_like_support") is not None:
                lines.append(f"camera_like_support = {_fmt_scalar(derived.get('camera_like_support'))}")
        else:
            if suspicious is not None:
                lines.append(f"suspicious_score = {_fmt_scalar(suspicious)}")
            if camera_counter is not None:
                lines.append(f"camera_counter_support = {_fmt_scalar(camera_counter)}")
            if fp_risk is not None:
                lines.append(f"contextual_false_positive_risk = {_fmt_scalar(fp_risk)}")
    return lines

def build_reasoning_trace_text(reasoning_trace: dict | None) -> str:
    if not reasoning_trace:
        return ""
    lines: list[str] = []
    lines.append("Reasoning Trace")
    lines.append("")
    analysis_mode = reasoning_trace.get("analysis_mode")
    if analysis_mode is not None:
        lines.append(f"Analysis Mode: {analysis_mode}")
        lines.append("")
    decision = reasoning_trace.get("decision", {}) or {}
    scorecard = reasoning_trace.get("scorecard", {}) or {}
    derived = reasoning_trace.get("derived_signals", {}) or {}
    sections = reasoning_trace.get("section_snapshot", {}) or {}
    pipeline = reasoning_trace.get("pipeline_status", {})
    policy = reasoning_trace.get('confidence_policy')
    if policy:
        _add_heading(lines, 'Confidence Policy')
        _add_kv(lines, 'Version', policy.get('version'))
        _add_kv(lines, 'Primary Hypothesis', policy.get('primary_hypothesis'))
        for assessment in policy.get('assessments', []):
            _add_kv(lines, assessment['hypothesis'], assessment['tier'] + ' / ' + assessment['policy_rule'])
            _add_list(lines, 'Policy Rationale', assessment['rationale'])
            _add_list(lines, 'Policy Limitations', assessment['reliability_limitations'])
        lines.append('')
    corroboration = reasoning_trace.get('corroboration_audit')
    if corroboration:
        _add_heading(lines, 'Production Corroboration Audit')
        _add_kv(lines, 'Version', corroboration.get('version'))
        for gate in corroboration.get('evaluated_gates', []):
            lines.append(f"- {gate['gate_id']}: passed={gate.get('gate_passed', gate['requirement_satisfied'])}; {gate['explanation']}")
            _add_kv(lines, '  Eligible Evidence', gate['eligible_evidence'])
            _add_kv(lines, '  Distinct Ancestry Groups', gate['distinct_ancestry_groups'])
            _add_kv(lines, '  Shared Ancestry', gate['shared_ancestry'])
        _add_list(lines, 'Corroboration Limitations', corroboration.get('limitations'))
        lines.append('')
    dependency = reasoning_trace.get('evidence_dependency_analysis')
    if dependency:
        _add_heading(lines, 'Evidence Dependency Analysis (Shadow Only)')
        for key in ('lineage_version', 'status', 'policy', 'production_score_unchanged', 'comparison', 'families', 'shared_ancestry'):
            _add_kv(lines, key, dependency.get(key))
        _add_list(lines, 'Shadow Limitations', dependency.get('limitations'))
        _add_list(lines, 'Shadow Errors', dependency.get('errors'))
        lines.append('No shadow confidence tier or forensic decision is assigned.')
        lines.append('')
    calculation = reasoning_trace.get("scorecard_calculation")
    if calculation:
        _add_heading(lines, "Scorecard Calculation")
        _add_kv(lines, "Version", calculation.get("calculation_version"))
        _add_kv(lines, "Status", calculation.get("status"))
        for key in ("base_suspicious", "base_benign", "raw_contributions", "final_contributions", "reliability_adjustments", "aggregate_transforms", "aggregate_scores"):
            if key in calculation:
                _add_kv(lines, key, calculation[key])
        _add_list(lines, "Calculation Errors", calculation.get("errors"))
        numerical = calculation.get('numerical_deduplication')
        if numerical:
            _add_heading(lines, 'Production Numerical Evidence Allocation')
            _add_kv(lines, 'Version', numerical.get('version'))
            _add_kv(lines, 'Base/domain reconciliation', numerical.get('aggregate_reconciliation'))
            for item in numerical.get('contributions', []):
                lines.append(
                    f"- {item['evidence_id']} [{item['classification']}]: "
                    f"raw={item['raw_contribution']}; credited={item['credited_contribution']}; "
                    f"suppressed={item['suppressed_contribution']}; {item['reason']}"
                )
            _add_list(lines, 'Unresolved numerical relationships', numerical.get('unresolved_relationships'))
            for item in numerical.get('component_allocations', []):
                lines.append(f"- {item['field']} / {item['evidence_id']}: raw={item['raw_contribution']}; suppressed={item['suppressed_contribution']}; multiplier={item['reliability_multiplier']}; {item['reason']}")
            lines.append('Base allocations precede aggregate attenuation, penalties and floor; shared ancestry does not establish independence.')
        for operation in calculation.get("component_operations", []):
            lines.append(f"- {operation['field']} {operation['operation']} {operation['operand']}: {operation['reason']}")
        lines.append("")
    if pipeline:
        _add_heading(lines, "Pipeline Coverage")
        _add_kv(lines, "Coverage", pipeline.get("coverage", {}).get("status"))
        _add_kv(lines, "Stage Counts", pipeline.get("coverage", {}).get("stage_counts"))
        mask = pipeline.get("mask_diagnostics", {})
        for key in ("mask_status", "mask_source", "mask_reliability", "fallback_used", "fallback_reason"):
            _add_kv(lines, key, mask.get(key))
        _add_list(lines, "Mask Rejection Reasons", mask.get("rejection_reasons"))
        _add_list(lines, "Unavailable Regional Stages", mask.get("disabled_stages"))
    _add_heading(lines, "Final Decision")
    _add_kv(lines, "Branch", decision.get("decision_branch"))
    _add_kv(lines, "Dominant Pattern", decision.get("dominant_pattern"))
    _add_kv(lines, "Confidence Tier", decision.get("confidence_tier"))
    _add_kv(lines, "Polarity", decision.get("polarity"))
    if decision.get("primary_hypothesis"):
        _add_kv(lines, "Primary Hypothesis", decision["primary_hypothesis"])
    lines.append("")
    for assessment in reasoning_trace.get("hypothesis_assessments", []):
        _add_heading(lines, assessment["hypothesis_id"])
        for key in ("polarity", "confidence_tier", "decision_branch", "explanation"):
            _add_kv(lines, key, assessment.get(key))
        presentation = evidence_presentation(assessment)
        for role, title in ROLE_TITLES.items():
            _add_list(lines, title, presentation[role.replace("_evidence", "_explanations")])
        _add_list(lines, "Limitations", assessment.get("limitations"))
    provenance = reasoning_trace.get("provenance_evidence")
    if provenance:
        _add_heading(lines, "Provenance Evidence")
        for key, value in provenance.items():
            _add_kv(lines, key, value)
    if reasoning_trace.get("decision_route_trace"):
        _add_heading(lines, "Route Candidates")
        for candidate in reasoning_trace["decision_route_trace"]:
            lines.append(
                f"- {candidate.get('route')}: matched={candidate.get('matched')}; "
                f"{candidate.get('disposition') or candidate.get('reason')}"
            )
        lines.append("")
    overall_text = decision.get("overall_text")
    if overall_text:
        _add_heading(lines, "Decision Summary")
        for raw_line in str(overall_text).splitlines():
            if raw_line.strip():
                lines.append(raw_line)
        lines.append("")
    why_lines = _build_decision_win_explanation(reasoning_trace)
    if why_lines:
        _add_heading(lines, "Why This Decision Won")
        for item in why_lines:
            lines.append(f"- {item}")
        lines.append("")
    for role, title in ROLE_TITLES.items():
        explanations = decision.get(role.replace("_evidence", "_explanations"))
        _add_list(lines, title, explanations if explanations is not None else decision.get(role.replace("_evidence", "_signals")))
    _add_list(lines, "Evidence Limitations", decision.get("limitations"))
    _add_list(lines, "Confidence Rationale", decision.get("confidence_rationale"))
    _add_heading(lines, "Scorecard Highlights")
    highlight_keys = [
        "suspicious_score",
        "benign_score",
        "local_edit_support_score",
        "clone_support_score",
        "camera_counter_support",
        "synthetic_boost",
        "edit_boost",
        "provenance_boost",
        "caution_penalty",
        "contextual_false_positive_risk",
    ]
    for key in highlight_keys:
        if key in scorecard:
            _add_kv(lines, key, scorecard[key])
    lines.append("")
    _add_heading(lines, "Derived Signals")
    derived_keys = [
        "person_mask_reliable",
        "subject_area_ratio",
        "boundary_support_ratio",
        "boundary_support_low",
        "boundary_reliability_score",        
        "strong_local_edit",
        "strong_cross",
        "region_geometry_unreliable",
        "patch_structured",
        "fft_structured",
        "camera_like_support",
        "metadata_synthetic",
        "metadata_enhancement",
        "metadata_missing",
        "provenance_state",
        "provenance_clean",
        "local_signals_weak",
    ]
    for key in derived_keys:
        if key in derived:
            _add_kv(lines, key, derived[key])
    lines.append("")
    _add_heading(lines, "Section Snapshot")
    for key, section in sections.items():
        lines.append(f"- {key}")
        if isinstance(section, dict):
            preferred_order = ["pattern", "score", "polarity", "status", "name", "note"]
            for sub_key in preferred_order:
                if sub_key in section:
                    lines.append(f"    {sub_key}: {_fmt_scalar(section[sub_key])}")
            for sub_key, sub_value in section.items():
                if sub_key not in preferred_order:
                    lines.append(f"    {sub_key}: {_fmt_scalar(sub_value)}")
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"

def persist_reasoning_trace_text(reasoning_trace: dict[str, Any] | None, output_dir: str | Path) -> str | None:
    if not reasoning_trace:
        return None
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    summary_dir = output_dir / "summary"
    summary_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    trace_txt_path = summary_dir / "reasoning_trace.txt"
    trace_txt_path.write_text(
        build_reasoning_trace_text(reasoning_trace),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return str(trace_txt_path)

--- End of reasoning_persistence.py ---
