#vf_overlay.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages#
#

from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageColor

#------------------------------------------------
# This module provides functionality to create visual overlays on images, highlighting areas of interest such as hotspots identified in ELA or noise analysis.
#------------------------------------------------

def create_shared_boundary_overlays(output_dir: Path, selected_file: Path, cross_signal_payload: dict, ela_payload: dict | None, noise_payload: dict | None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
    if selected_file is None:
        return None
    cross_payload = cross_signal_payload
    if not cross_payload:
        return None
    boundary_signal = cross_payload.get("boundary_signal", {})
    largest_cluster = boundary_signal.get("largest_shared_cluster", {})
    boundary_blocks = largest_cluster.get("blocks", [])
    if not boundary_blocks:
        return None
    block_size = 64
    if ela_payload is not None:
        block_size = (
            ela_payload.get("ela", {})
            .get("blockwise", {})
            .get("block_size", 64)
        )
    paths = {}
    # 1. Original image overlay
    original_overlay_path = output_dir / f"{selected_file.stem}_shared_boundary_overlay_original.png"
    draw_cluster_overlay(
        base_image_path=selected_file,
        output_path=original_overlay_path,
        cluster_blocks=boundary_blocks,
        block_size=block_size,
        outline_color="cyan",
        outline_width=4,
        fill_color="cyan",
        fill_alpha=45,
    )
    paths["original"] = str(original_overlay_path)
    # 2. ELA differential overlay
    if ela_payload is not None:
        ela_diff_path = Path(ela_payload["ela"]["ela_image_path"])
        ela_overlay_path = output_dir / f"{selected_file.stem}_shared_boundary_overlay_ela.png"
        draw_cluster_overlay(
            base_image_path=ela_diff_path,
            output_path=ela_overlay_path,
            cluster_blocks=boundary_blocks,
            block_size=block_size,
            outline_color="cyan",
            outline_width=3,
            fill_color="cyan",
            fill_alpha=35,
        )
        paths["ela"] = str(ela_overlay_path)
    # 3. Residual overlay
    if noise_payload is not None:
        noise_diff_path = Path(noise_payload["noise"]["residual_image_path"])
        noise_overlay_path = output_dir / f"{selected_file.stem}_shared_boundary_overlay_noise.png"
        draw_cluster_overlay(
            base_image_path=noise_diff_path,
            output_path=noise_overlay_path,
            cluster_blocks=boundary_blocks,
            block_size=block_size,
            outline_color="cyan",
            outline_width=3,
            fill_color="cyan",
            fill_alpha=35,
        )
        paths["noise"] = str(noise_overlay_path)
    return paths
# ------------------------------------------------
# Normalize block coordinates
#------------------------------------------------
def _normalize_block_coords(blocks: Iterable[Any]) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    coords = []
    seen = set()
    for block in blocks:
        if isinstance(block, dict):
            row = int(block["row"])
            col = int(block["col"])
        else:
            row, col = block
        key = (row, col)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            coords.append(key)
    return coords

# ------------------------------------------------
# Ranked hotspot overlay (used for ELA / residual top blocks)
#------------------------------------------------
def create_hotspot_overlay(
    base_image_path: str | Path,
    output_path: str | Path,
    top_blocks: list[dict],
    block_size: int = 64,
    max_blocks: int = 10,
    outline_color: str = "red",
    outline_width: int = 3,
    show_labels: bool = True,
) -> Path:
    base_image_path = Path(base_image_path)
    output_path = Path(output_path)
    img = Image.open(base_image_path).convert("RGB")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    for i, block in enumerate(top_blocks[:max_blocks], start=1):
        row = int(block["row"])
        col = int(block["col"])
        x1 = col * block_size
        y1 = row * block_size
        x2 = min(x1 + block_size, img.width)
        y2 = min(y1 + block_size, img.height)
        draw.rectangle(
            [x1, y1, x2, y2],
            outline=outline_color,
            width=outline_width,
        )
        if show_labels:
            draw.text((x1 + 3, y1 + 3), str(i), fill=outline_color)
    img.save(output_path)
    return output_path

# ------------------------------------------------
# Cluster overlay with outer contour (used for boundary signals)
#---------------------
def draw_cluster_overlay(
    base_image_path: str | Path,
    output_path: str | Path,
    cluster_blocks: list[dict] | list[tuple[int, int]],
    block_size: int = 64,
    outline_color: str = "cyan",
    outline_width: int = 4,
    fill_color: str | None = None,
    fill_alpha: int = 40,
) -> Path:
    base_image_path = Path(base_image_path)
    output_path = Path(output_path)
    coords = _normalize_block_coords(cluster_blocks)
    coord_set = set(coords)
    img = Image.open(base_image_path).convert("RGBA")
    overlay = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(overlay)
    # translucent fill
    if fill_color is not None:
        fill_rgb = ImageColor.getrgb(fill_color)
        fill_rgba = (fill_rgb[0], fill_rgb[1], fill_rgb[2], fill_alpha)
        for row, col in coords:
            x0 = col * block_size
            y0 = row * block_size
            x1 = min(x0 + block_size, img.width)
            y1 = min(y0 + block_size, img.height)

            draw.rectangle([x0, y0, x1, y1], fill=fill_rgba)
    # draw only exposed edges -> continuous contour
    offset = outline_width / 2
    for row, col in coords:
        x0 = col * block_size
        y0 = row * block_size
        x1 = min(x0 + block_size, img.width)
        y1 = min(y0 + block_size, img.height)
        # top edge
        if (row - 1, col) not in coord_set:
            draw.line(
                [(x0 - offset, y0 - offset), (x1 + offset, y0 - offset)],
                fill=outline_color,
                width=outline_width,
                joint="curve",
            )
        # bottom edge
        if (row + 1, col) not in coord_set:
            draw.line(
                [(x0 - offset, y1 + offset), (x1 + offset, y1 + offset)],
                fill=outline_color,
                width=outline_width,
                joint="curve",
            )
        # left edge
        if (row, col - 1) not in coord_set:
            draw.line(
                [(x0 - offset, y0 - offset), (x0 - offset, y1 + offset)],
                fill=outline_color,
                width=outline_width,
                joint="curve",
            )
        # right edge
        if (row, col + 1) not in coord_set:
            draw.line(
                [(x1 + offset, y0 - offset), (x1 + offset, y1 + offset)],
                fill=outline_color,
                width=outline_width,
                joint="curve",
            )
    combined = Image.alpha_composite(img, overlay).convert("RGB")
    combined.save(output_path)
    return output_path
--- End of vf_overlay.py ---

--- Start of vf_patch_similarity.py ---
#vf_patch_similarity.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages
#
# This module will produce false positives on: brick walls, windows, tiles, fabrics, foliage, repetitive architecture, patterned wallpapers,
# and many other naturally occurring repeated structures. 
# that is normal, That is why: thresholds are strict, labels are cautious,  the interpretation references scene context
# especially in combination with fft analysis, detector interaction needs interpretation thresholds.
# This module behaves more like “strong repeated local structure detector” than “reliable clone-reuse detector”, thus is best treated as supporting evidence not standalone proof

from __future__ import annotations
import json
import math
import numpy as np
import cv2
from   dataclasses import dataclass
from   pathlib import Path
from   typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple
from   PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from  sklearn.neighbors import NearestNeighbors

# ============================================================
# configuration
# ============================================================
DEFAULT_MAX_DIM = 768
DEFAULT_PATCH_SIZE = 24
DEFAULT_STRIDE = 12
DEFAULT_DESCRIPTOR_SIZE = 8
DEFAULT_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD = 0.965
DEFAULT_MIN_SEPARATION_FACTOR = 1.5
DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_TO_KEEP = 300
DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_FOR_OVERLAY = 60

# ============================================================
# dataclasses
# ============================================================

@dataclass
class PatchDescriptor:
    x: int
    y: int
    vector: np.ndarray

@dataclass
class PatchPair:
    ax: int
    ay: int
    bx: int
    by: int
    similarity: float
    distance: float

# ============================================================
# public API
# ============================================================
def run_patch_similarity_analysis(
image_path: str | Path,
    output_dir: str | Path,
    max_dim: int = DEFAULT_MAX_DIM,
    patch_size: int = DEFAULT_PATCH_SIZE,
    stride: int = DEFAULT_STRIDE,
    descriptor_size: int = DEFAULT_DESCRIPTOR_SIZE,
    similarity_threshold: float = DEFAULT_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD,
    max_pairs_to_keep: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_TO_KEEP,
    max_pairs_for_overlay: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_FOR_OVERLAY,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:    
    """
    Patch similarity / repetition analysis.
    Detects strong repeated local structures and possible clone-like reuse.
    Writes:
      - patch_similarity_results.json
      - *_patch_similarity_overlay.png
      - *_patch_similarity_heatmap.png
    """
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    gray, rgb_for_overlay, scale_info = load_images(
        image_path=image_path,
        max_dim=max_dim,
    )
    h, w = gray.shape
    if h < patch_size or w < patch_size:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Image too small for patch analysis: {w}x{h}, patch_size={patch_size}"
        )
    descriptors = extract_patch_descriptors(
        gray=gray,
        patch_size=patch_size,
        stride=stride,
        descriptor_size=descriptor_size,
    )
        
    # choose between old bruteforce comparison vs bucketed comparison vs accelerated ANN search
    # Convert Cosine Similarity threshold to Euclidean Distance threshold
    # Formula: dist = sqrt(2 * (1 - similarity))
    dist_threshold = math.sqrt(2 * (1.0 - similarity_threshold))
    pairs, nn_stats = find_similar_patches_accelerated(
            patch_descriptors=descriptors,
            patch_size=patch_size,
            threshold=dist_threshold,
            max_matches=max_pairs_to_keep,
        )
    recurrence_map = build_recurrence_map(
        shape=gray.shape,
        pairs=pairs,
        patch_size=patch_size,
    )
    features = compute_similarity_features(
        pairs=pairs,
        recurrence_map=recurrence_map,
        patch_size=patch_size,
        shape=gray.shape,
    )
    similarity_pattern, similarity_note = interpret_patch_similarity(features)
    stem = image_path.stem
    overlay_path = output_dir / f"{stem}_patch_similarity_overlay.png"
    heatmap_path = output_dir / f"{stem}_patch_similarity_heatmap.png"
    json_path = output_dir / "patch_similarity_results.json"
    save_patch_similarity_overlay(
            base_image=rgb_for_overlay,
            pairs=pairs[:max_pairs_for_overlay],
            out_path=overlay_path,
            patch_size=patch_size,
        )
    save_recurrence_heatmap(
        recurrence_map=recurrence_map,
        out_path=heatmap_path,
    )
    result = {
        "patch_similarity": {
            "input_image": str(image_path),
            "patch_count": int(nn_stats["patch_count"]),
            # We map the new stats to the old keys so the Controller doesn't break
            "bucket_count": int(nn_stats["patch_count"]),  # Total patches acting as 'nodes'
            "nontrivial_bucket_count": len(pairs),         # Actual matches found
            "candidate_comparisons": int(nn_stats["candidate_comparisons"]),
            "repetition_score": round(float(features["repetition_score"]), 4),
            "clone_suspicion_score": round(float(features["clone_suspicion_score"]), 4),
            "pair_count": int(features["pair_count"]),
            "coverage_ratio": round(float(features["coverage_ratio"]), 4),
            "similarity_pattern": similarity_pattern,
            "similarity_note": similarity_note,
            # expose matched-pair geometry for spatial analysis            
            "top_pairs": [
                {
                    "ax": int(p.ax),
                    "ay": int(p.ay),
                    "bx": int(p.bx),
                    "by": int(p.by),
                    "similarity": round(float(p.similarity), 6),
                    "distance": round(float(p.distance), 3),
                }
                for p in pairs[:200]
            ],
            "patch_size": int(patch_size),
            "scale_info": {
                "scale": float(scale_info["scale"]),
                "width": int(scale_info["width"]),
                "height": int(scale_info["height"]),
            },
            "artifacts": {
                "patch_similarity_overlay": str(overlay_path),
                "patch_similarity_heatmap": str(heatmap_path),
            }
        }
    }
    save_json(result, json_path)
    return result

# ============================================================
# loading
# ============================================================

def load_images(
    image_path: str | Path,
    max_dim: int = DEFAULT_MAX_DIM,
) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Image.Image, Dict[str, float]]:
    """
    Return grayscale float32 [0,1], RGB PIL image for overlay, and scale metadata.
    """
    with Image.open(image_path) as im:
        rgb = im.convert("RGB")
        gray = im.convert("L")
        w, h = rgb.size
        scale = min(1.0, max_dim / max(w, h))
        if scale < 1.0:
            new_size = (max(1, int(round(w * scale))), max(1, int(round(h * scale))))
            rgb = rgb.resize(new_size, Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
            gray = gray.resize(new_size, Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        gray_arr = np.asarray(gray, dtype=np.float32) / 255.0
        return gray_arr, rgb, {
            "scale": scale,
            "width": rgb.width,
            "height": rgb.height,
        }


# ============================================================
# descriptor extraction
# ============================================================

def extract_patch_descriptors(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    patch_size: int,
    stride: int,
    descriptor_size: int,
) -> List[PatchDescriptor]:
    """
    Extract simple normalized descriptors from overlapping grayscale patches.
    Each descriptor is created by:
      - cropping patch
      - resizing to descriptor_size x descriptor_size
      - z-normalizing
      - flattening
    """
    h, w = gray.shape
    descriptors: List[PatchDescriptor] = []
    for y in range(0, h - patch_size + 1, stride):
        for x in range(0, w - patch_size + 1, stride):
            patch = gray[y:y + patch_size, x:x + patch_size]
            desc = patch_to_descriptor(patch, descriptor_size=descriptor_size)
            descriptors.append(PatchDescriptor(x=x, y=y, vector=desc))
    return descriptors

def patch_to_descriptor(patch: np.ndarray, descriptor_size: int) -> np.ndarray:
    small = cv2.resize(
        patch.astype(np.float32),
        (descriptor_size, descriptor_size),
        interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA,
    )
    mean = float(small.mean())
    std = float(small.std())
    if std < 1e-6:
        vec = small - mean
    else:
        vec = (small - mean) / std
    vec = vec.reshape(-1).astype(np.float32)
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vec))
    if norm > 1e-8:
        vec /= norm
    return vec

def descriptor_to_bucket_key(
    vec: np.ndarray,
    bucket_dims: int,
    bucket_quant: float,
) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    """
    Convert a normalized descriptor into a coarse quantized bucket key.
    """
    dims = min(bucket_dims, len(vec))
    coarse = np.round(vec[:dims] * bucket_quant).astype(np.int16)
    return tuple(int(x) for x in coarse)    
        
def build_descriptor_buckets(
    descriptors: List[PatchDescriptor],
    bucket_dims: int,
    bucket_quant: float,
) -> Dict[tuple[int, ...], List[PatchDescriptor]]:
    buckets: Dict[tuple[int, ...], List[PatchDescriptor]] = {}
    for desc in descriptors:
        key = descriptor_to_bucket_key(
            vec=desc.vector,
            bucket_dims=bucket_dims,
            bucket_quant=bucket_quant,
        )
        buckets.setdefault(key, []).append(desc)
    return buckets

# ============================================================
# pair search replaces bucketed comparison
# The KD-Tree reduces the search complexity from $O(N^2)$ to $O(N \log N)$
# ============================================================
def find_similar_patches_accelerated(
    patch_descriptors: List[PatchDescriptor],
    patch_size: int = 8,
    threshold: float = 0.26,
    max_matches: int = 500,
    k_neighbors: int = 8,
) -> Tuple[List[PatchPair], Dict[str, Any]]:
    pairs: List[PatchPair] = []
    n_patches = len(patch_descriptors)
    k = min(k_neighbors, n_patches)
    stats = {
        "patch_count": n_patches,
        "search_mode": "nearest_neighbors",
        "k_neighbors": int(k),
        "candidate_comparisons": int(n_patches * max(k - 1, 0)),
    }
    if n_patches < 2:
        return pairs, stats
    feature_matrix = np.array(
        [p.vector for p in patch_descriptors],
        dtype=np.float32,
    )
    nn = NearestNeighbors(
        n_neighbors=k,
        algorithm="auto",
        metric="euclidean",
    )
    nn.fit(feature_matrix)
    distances, indices = nn.kneighbors(feature_matrix)
    seen_pairs = set()
    min_separation = patch_size * 2
    for i, (dists, idxs) in enumerate(zip(distances, indices)):
        p1 = patch_descriptors[i]
        for dist, neighbor_idx in zip(dists[1:], idxs[1:]):
            dist = float(dist)
            neighbor_idx = int(neighbor_idx)
            if dist >= threshold:
                continue
            p2 = patch_descriptors[neighbor_idx]
            dxy = math.hypot(p1.x - p2.x, p1.y - p2.y)
            if dxy <= min_separation:
                continue
            pair_key = tuple(sorted((i, neighbor_idx)))
            if pair_key in seen_pairs:
                continue
            seen_pairs.add(pair_key)
            sim = 1.0 - (dist ** 2 / 2.0)
            pairs.append(
                PatchPair(
                    ax=p1.x,
                    ay=p1.y,
                    bx=p2.x,
                    by=p2.y,
                    similarity=float(sim),
                    distance=float(dxy),
                )
            )
            if len(pairs) >= max_matches:
                break
        if len(pairs) >= max_matches:
            break
    pairs.sort(key=lambda p: (p.similarity, p.distance), reverse=True)
    return pairs, stats
#------------------------------------------------------
# bucketed comparison
#------------------------------------------------------
def find_similar_patch_pairs_bucketed(
    descriptors: List[PatchDescriptor],
    patch_size: int,
    similarity_threshold: float,
    min_separation_factor: float,
    max_pairs_to_keep: int,
    bucket_dims: int,
    bucket_quant: float,
    max_bucket_size: int,
) -> Tuple[List[PatchPair], Dict[str, float]]:
    """
    Compare descriptors only within coarse feature buckets.
    This is a first-pass candidate-pruning strategy to avoid full O(N^2).
    """
    min_separation = patch_size * min_separation_factor
    pairs: List[PatchPair] = []
    buckets = build_descriptor_buckets(
        descriptors=descriptors,
        bucket_dims=bucket_dims,
        bucket_quant=bucket_quant,
    )
    candidate_comparisons = 0
    bucket_sizes = []
    for key, bucket in buckets.items():
        bucket_size = len(bucket)
        bucket_sizes.append(bucket_size)
        # ignore singleton buckets
        if bucket_size < 2:
            continue
        # optional cap to avoid pathological buckets
        if bucket_size > max_bucket_size:
            # keep most "energetic" / variable descriptors first
            # for now: deterministic truncation by position
            bucket = bucket[:max_bucket_size]
            bucket_size = len(bucket)
        for i in range(bucket_size):
            di = bucket[i]
            vi = di.vector
            for j in range(i + 1, bucket_size):
                dj = bucket[j]
                candidate_comparisons += 1
                dxy = math.hypot(dj.x - di.x, dj.y - di.y)
                if dxy < min_separation:
                    continue
                sim = cosine_similarity(vi, dj.vector)
                if sim < similarity_threshold:
                    continue
                pairs.append(
                    PatchPair(
                        ax=di.x,
                        ay=di.y,
                        bx=dj.x,
                        by=dj.y,
                        similarity=float(sim),
                        distance=float(dxy),
                    )
                )
    pairs.sort(key=lambda p: (p.similarity, p.distance), reverse=True)
    pairs = pairs[:max_pairs_to_keep]
    bucket_count = len(buckets)
    nontrivial_bucket_count = sum(1 for s in bucket_sizes if s >= 2)
    mean_bucket_size = float(np.mean(bucket_sizes)) if bucket_sizes else 0.0
    max_observed_bucket_size = int(max(bucket_sizes)) if bucket_sizes else 0
    nn_stats = {
        "bucket_count": bucket_count,
        "nontrivial_bucket_count": nontrivial_bucket_count,
        "mean_bucket_size": mean_bucket_size,
        "max_observed_bucket_size": max_observed_bucket_size,
        "candidate_comparisons": candidate_comparisons,
    }
    return pairs, nn_stats    

# ============================================================
# 'old' pair search
# ============================================================
def find_similar_patch_pairs_bruteforce(
    descriptors: List[PatchDescriptor],
    patch_size: int,
    similarity_threshold: float,
    min_separation_factor: float,
    max_pairs_to_keep: int,
) -> List[PatchPair]:
    """
    Compare patch descriptors and keep highly similar, spatially separated pairs.
    For V1, a direct O(N^2) pass is acceptable on resized images with moderate stride.
    If needed later, this can be accelerated with ANN / LSH / KD-tree strategies.
    """
    min_separation = patch_size * min_separation_factor
    pairs: List[PatchPair] = []
    n = len(descriptors)
    for i in range(n):
        di = descriptors[i]
        vi = di.vector
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            dj = descriptors[j]
            # suppress trivial local neighbors / overlapping patches
            dxy = math.hypot(dj.x - di.x, dj.y - di.y)
            if dxy < min_separation:
                continue
            sim = cosine_similarity(vi, dj.vector)
            if sim < similarity_threshold:
                continue
            pairs.append(
                PatchPair(
                    ax=di.x,
                    ay=di.y,
                    bx=dj.x,
                    by=dj.y,
                    similarity=float(sim),
                    distance=float(dxy),
                )
            )
    pairs.sort(key=lambda p: (p.similarity, p.distance), reverse=True)
    return pairs[:max_pairs_to_keep]

def cosine_similarity(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(np.dot(a, b))

# ============================================================
# recurrence map and features
# ============================================================
def build_recurrence_map(
    shape: Tuple[int, int],
    pairs: List[PatchPair],
    patch_size: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Build a heatmap where repeated-patch regions accumulate evidence.
    """
    h, w = shape
    heat = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.float32)
    for p in pairs:
        weight = max(0.0, p.similarity - 0.90) / 0.10  # similarity emphasis
        weight = float(np.clip(weight, 0.0, 1.5))
        heat[p.ay:p.ay + patch_size, p.ax:p.ax + patch_size] += weight
        heat[p.by:p.by + patch_size, p.bx:p.bx + patch_size] += weight
    return heat

def compute_similarity_features(
    pairs: List[PatchPair],
    recurrence_map: np.ndarray,
    patch_size: int,
    shape: Tuple[int, int],
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Derive conservative summary features from strong repeated pairs.
    """
    h, w = shape
    image_area = float(h * w)
    pair_count = len(pairs)
    if pair_count == 0:
        return {
            "repetition_score": 0.0,
            "clone_suspicion_score": 0.0,
            "pair_count": 0,
            "spatially_separated_pair_count": 0,
            "strong_pair_density_score": 0.0,
            "mean_similarity": 0.0,
            "max_similarity": 0.0,
            "coverage_ratio": 0.0,
        }
    sims = np.array([p.similarity for p in pairs], dtype=np.float32)
    dists = np.array([p.distance for p in pairs], dtype=np.float32)
    # stronger evidence if repeated pairs are not just barely above threshold
    mean_similarity = float(np.mean(sims))
    max_similarity = float(np.max(sims))
    # "spatially separated" means well beyond local texture continuity
    separated_threshold = patch_size * 3.0
    spatially_separated_pair_count = int(np.sum(dists >= separated_threshold))
    # how much of the image participates in repeated-patch evidence
    active_mask = recurrence_map > 0.0
    coverage_ratio = float(np.count_nonzero(active_mask) / max(image_area, 1.0))
    # recurrence concentration / density
    if np.count_nonzero(active_mask) > 0:
        mean_active = float(np.mean(recurrence_map[active_mask]))
        max_active = float(np.max(recurrence_map[active_mask]))
    else:
        mean_active = 0.0
        max_active = 0.0
    strong_pair_density_score = clamp01(
        0.60 * min(pair_count / 50.0, 1.0) +
        0.40 * min(spatially_separated_pair_count / 20.0, 1.0)
    )
    repetition_score = clamp01(
        0.40 * min(pair_count / 40.0, 1.0) +
        0.30 * min(spatially_separated_pair_count / 16.0, 1.0) +
        0.15 * clamp01((mean_similarity - 0.96) / 0.03) +
        0.15 * clamp01(coverage_ratio / 0.12)
    )
    # make clone suspicion less coverage-positive
    clone_suspicion_score = clamp01(
        0.45 * min(spatially_separated_pair_count / 12.0, 1.0) +
        0.30 * clamp01((max_similarity - 0.975) / 0.02) +
        0.15 * clamp01((mean_similarity - 0.965) / 0.03) +
        0.10 * clamp01(1.0 - abs(coverage_ratio - 0.10) / 0.10)
        # balanced peak function: peak at coverage = 0.10 and falls off both sides
        # 0.10 * clamp01(coverage_ratio / 0.10) this always rewards more coverage, this is backwards for clone detection
        # High coverage is often natural repetition, low/moderate coverage is more consistent with localized copy-move
    )
    return {
        "repetition_score": float(repetition_score),
        "clone_suspicion_score": float(clone_suspicion_score),
        "pair_count": int(pair_count),
        "spatially_separated_pair_count": int(spatially_separated_pair_count),
        "strong_pair_density_score": float(strong_pair_density_score),
        "mean_similarity": float(mean_similarity),
        "max_similarity": float(max_similarity),
        "coverage_ratio": float(coverage_ratio),
    }

# ============================================================
# interpretation
# ============================================================
def interpret_patch_similarity(features: Dict[str, float]) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    repetition_score = float(features["repetition_score"])
    clone_score = float(features["clone_suspicion_score"])
    pair_count = int(features["pair_count"])
    separated_count = int(features["spatially_separated_pair_count"])
    mean_similarity = float(features["mean_similarity"])
    max_similarity = float(features["max_similarity"])
    coverage_ratio = float(features["coverage_ratio"])
    if pair_count == 0 or repetition_score < 0.18:
        return (
            "patch_similarity_inconclusive",
            "Patch-similarity analysis did not reveal strong spatially separated repeated structures beyond what can occur naturally in textured, patterned, or repetitive scene content."
        )
    # tighten possible_clone_reuse by adding coverage_ratio <= 0.18
    if clone_score >= 0.82 and separated_count >= 10 and max_similarity >= 0.985 and mean_similarity >= 0.93 and coverage_ratio <= 0.18:
        return (
            "possible_clone_reuse",
            "Patch-similarity analysis reveals highly similar spatially separated local regions consistent with duplicated local content or copy-move style reuse."
        )
    if repetition_score >= 0.60 and separated_count >= 10 and mean_similarity >= 0.972:
        return (
            "possible_synthetic_texture_repetition",
            "Patch-similarity analysis reveals repeated high-similarity local motifs across spatially separated regions, consistent with structured texture reuse or synthetic patch recurrence."
        )
    if repetition_score >= 0.35 and separated_count >= 4:
        return (
            "structured_patch_recurrence",
            "Patch-similarity analysis reveals repeated local structures across spatially separated regions. The result is notable, though it may still reflect natural patterned content depending on scene context."
        )
    return (
        "localized_repetition_detected",
        "Patch-similarity analysis reveals some repeated local structures, though the result remains only moderately specific and may reflect natural scene repetition."
    )

# ============================================================
# artifacts
# ============================================================
def save_patch_similarity_overlay(
    base_image: Image.Image,
    pairs: List[PatchPair],
    out_path: str | Path,
    patch_size: int,
) -> None:
    """
    Draw top repeated-patch boxes and connecting lines.
    """
    im = base_image.copy().convert("RGBA")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    for idx, p in enumerate(pairs):
        # slight taper in alpha/importance by rank
        rank_scale = max(0.25, 1.0 - idx / max(len(pairs), 1))
        line_alpha = int(180 * rank_scale)
        box_alpha = int(130 * rank_scale)
        line_color = (255, 80, 80, line_alpha)
        box_a_color = (255, 220, 80, box_alpha)
        box_b_color = (80, 220, 255, box_alpha)
        # centers
        acx = p.ax + patch_size / 2
        acy = p.ay + patch_size / 2
        bcx = p.bx + patch_size / 2
        bcy = p.by + patch_size / 2
        draw.line((acx, acy, bcx, bcy), fill=line_color, width=2)
        draw.rectangle(
            (p.ax, p.ay, p.ax + patch_size, p.ay + patch_size),
            outline=box_a_color,
            width=2,
        )
        draw.rectangle(
            (p.bx, p.by, p.bx + patch_size, p.by + patch_size),
            outline=box_b_color,
            width=2,
        )
    im.save(out_path)

def save_recurrence_heatmap(
    recurrence_map: np.ndarray,
    out_path: str | Path,
) -> None:
    """
    Save recurrence heatmap as grayscale for now.
    """
    arr = recurrence_map.astype(np.float32)
    if np.max(arr) > 0:
        arr = arr / np.max(arr)
    arr = np.clip(arr * 255.0, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    Image.fromarray(arr, mode="L").save(out_path)

# ============================================================
# helpers
# ============================================================
def clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    return float(max(0.0, min(1.0, x)))

def save_json(data: Dict[str, Any], out_path: str | Path) -> None:
    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="SignalLens Patch Similarity / Clone / Repetition Analysis")

--- Start of vf_patch_spatial.py ---
# vf_patch_spatial.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np
# local imports
from vf_patch_similarity import DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_TO_KEEP

def _load_mask(path: str | Path) -> np.ndarray:
    m = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if m is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load mask: {path}")
    return (m > 127).astype(np.uint8)

def _safe_ratio(a: float, b: float) -> float:
    return float(a / (b + 1e-6))

def _point_region(x: int, y: int, subject_mask: np.ndarray, boundary_mask: np.ndarray, background_mask: np.ndarray) -> str:
    h, w = subject_mask.shape
    x = max(0, min(w - 1, int(x)))
    y = max(0, min(h - 1, int(y)))
    if boundary_mask[y, x] > 0:
        return "boundary"
    if subject_mask[y, x] > 0:
        return "subject"
    if background_mask[y, x] > 0:
        return "background"
    # fallback if masks don't perfectly partition
    return "background"

def _pair_category(r1: str, r2: str) -> str:
    if r1 == r2:
        return f"{r1}_{r2}"
    ordered = sorted([r1, r2])
    return f"{ordered[0]}_{ordered[1]}"

def _remap_point_to_mask_space(
    x: int,
    y: int,
    patch_width: int,
    patch_height: int,
    mask_width: int,
    mask_height: int,
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    if patch_width <= 0 or patch_height <= 0:
        return int(x), int(y)

    sx = mask_width / float(patch_width)
    sy = mask_height / float(patch_height)

    xr = int(round(x * sx))
    yr = int(round(y * sy))

    xr = max(0, min(mask_width - 1, xr))
    yr = max(0, min(mask_height - 1, yr))
    return xr, yr

def run_patch_spatial_analysis(patch_similarity_payload: Dict[str, Any], region_masks_payload: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str | Path,) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Load masks
    # --------------------------------------------------
    masks = region_masks_payload["region_masks"]
    subject_mask = _load_mask(masks["subject_mask_path"])
    boundary_mask = _load_mask(masks["boundary_band_path"])
    background_mask = _load_mask(masks["background_mask_path"])
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Load top matched pairs from patch_similarity payload
    # --------------------------------------------------
    patch = patch_similarity_payload.get("patch_similarity", {})
    patch_size = int(patch.get("patch_size", 24))
    patch_half = patch_size / 2.0    
    top_pairs = patch.get("top_pairs", []) 
    # on error, instead of braking the pipeline return gracefully
    if not top_pairs:
            return {
                "patch_spatial": {
                        "pattern": "spatial_patch_recurrence_inconclusive",
                        "note": "No patch similarity pairs available for spatial analysis.",
        }
    }                
    top_pairs = top_pairs[:DEFAULT_MAX_PAIRS_TO_KEEP]
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Load patch scale info for remapping coordinates to mask space
    # --------------------------------------------------    
    scale_info = patch.get("scale_info", {})
    patch_width = int(scale_info.get("width", subject_mask.shape[1]))
    patch_height = int(scale_info.get("height", subject_mask.shape[0]))
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Accumulate counts and weighted scores by region topology
    # --------------------------------------------------
    categories = {
        "subject_subject": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
        "boundary_boundary": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
        "background_background": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
        "boundary_subject": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
        "background_subject": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
        "background_boundary": {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0},
    }
    endpoint_counts = {
        "subject": 0,
        "boundary": 0,
        "background": 0,
    }
    endpoint_weights = {
        "subject": 0.0,
        "boundary": 0.0,
        "background": 0.0,
    }
    for p in top_pairs:
        ax = float(p["ax"]) + patch_half
        ay = float(p["ay"]) + patch_half
        bx = float(p["bx"]) + patch_half
        by = float(p["by"]) + patch_half
        sim = float(p.get("similarity", 0.0))
        # remap endpoints before _point_region
        axr, ayr = _remap_point_to_mask_space(
            ax, ay,
            patch_width=patch_width,
            patch_height=patch_height,
            mask_width=subject_mask.shape[1],
            mask_height=subject_mask.shape[0],
        )
        bxr, byr = _remap_point_to_mask_space(
            bx, by,
            patch_width=patch_width,
            patch_height=patch_height,
            mask_width=subject_mask.shape[1],
            mask_height=subject_mask.shape[0],
        )
        r1 = _point_region(axr, ayr, subject_mask, boundary_mask, background_mask)
        r2 = _point_region(bxr, byr, subject_mask, boundary_mask, background_mask)
                
        cat = _pair_category(r1, r2)
        if cat not in categories:
            categories[cat] = {"count": 0, "weight": 0.0}
        categories[cat]["count"] += 1
        categories[cat]["weight"] += sim
        endpoint_counts[r1] += 1
        endpoint_counts[r2] += 1
        endpoint_weights[r1] += sim
        endpoint_weights[r2] += sim
    total_pair_count = float(len(top_pairs))
    total_pair_weight = float(sum(float(p.get("similarity", 0.0)) for p in top_pairs))
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Derived region-aware features
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_subject_count = categories.get("subject_subject", {}).get("count", 0)
    boundary_boundary_count = categories.get("boundary_boundary", {}).get("count", 0)
    background_background_count = categories.get("background_background", {}).get("count", 0)
    boundary_subject_count = categories.get("boundary_subject", {}).get("count", 0)
    background_subject_count = categories.get("background_subject", {}).get("count", 0)
    background_boundary_count = categories.get("background_boundary", {}).get("count", 0)
    subject_subject_weight = categories.get("subject_subject", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    boundary_boundary_weight = categories.get("boundary_boundary", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    background_background_weight = categories.get("background_background", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    boundary_subject_weight = categories.get("boundary_subject", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    background_subject_weight = categories.get("background_subject", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    background_boundary_weight = categories.get("background_boundary", {}).get("weight", 0.0)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Subject-internal recurrence metrics (for virtual fashion model etc)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_internal_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_subject_count, total_pair_count)
    subject_internal_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_subject_weight, total_pair_weight)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Area-normalized endpoint densities
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_area = float(np.count_nonzero(subject_mask))
    boundary_area = float(np.count_nonzero(boundary_mask))
    background_area = float(np.count_nonzero(background_mask))
    subject_endpoint_density = endpoint_counts["subject"] / max(subject_area, 1.0)
    boundary_endpoint_density = endpoint_counts["boundary"] / max(boundary_area, 1.0)
    background_endpoint_density = endpoint_counts["background"] / max(background_area, 1.0)
    subject_weight_density = endpoint_weights["subject"] / max(subject_area, 1.0)
    boundary_weight_density = endpoint_weights["boundary"] / max(boundary_area, 1.0)
    background_weight_density = endpoint_weights["background"] / max(background_area, 1.0)
    subject_density_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_endpoint_density, background_endpoint_density)
    boundary_density_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_endpoint_density, background_endpoint_density)
    subject_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_weight_density, background_weight_density)
    boundary_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_weight_density, background_weight_density)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Bridge topology metrics
    # --------------------------------------------------
    bridge_count    = (boundary_subject_count + background_subject_count + background_boundary_count)
    bridge_weight   = (boundary_subject_weight + background_subject_weight + background_boundary_weight)
    internal_count  = (subject_subject_count + boundary_boundary_count + background_background_count)
    internal_weight = (subject_subject_weight + boundary_boundary_weight + background_background_weight)
    bridge_ratio = _safe_ratio(bridge_count, internal_count)
    bridge_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(bridge_weight, internal_weight)
    boundary_involvement_count  = (boundary_boundary_count + boundary_subject_count + background_boundary_count )
    boundary_involvement_weight = (boundary_boundary_weight + boundary_subject_weight + background_boundary_weight)
    boundary_involvement_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_involvement_count, total_pair_count)
    boundary_involvement_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_involvement_weight, total_pair_weight)
    boundary_correlated_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_boundary_count + boundary_subject_count, total_pair_count,)
    boundary_correlated_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(boundary_boundary_weight + boundary_subject_weight, total_pair_weight,)
    subject_localized_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_subject_count + boundary_subject_count, total_pair_count,)
    subject_localized_weight_ratio = _safe_ratio(subject_subject_weight + boundary_subject_weight, total_pair_weight,)
    cross_region_patch_reuse_ratio = bridge_ratio
    #---------------------------------------------------
    # Debug prints for key metrics
    #---------------------------------------------------
    '''
    print("[DEBUG][patch_spatial][final]", {
        "bridge_count": bridge_count,
        "internal_count": internal_count,
        "bridge_ratio": bridge_ratio,
        "boundary_involvement_count": boundary_involvement_count,
        "boundary_involvement_ratio": boundary_involvement_ratio,
        "boundary_correlated_ratio": boundary_correlated_ratio,
        "subject_localized_ratio": subject_localized_ratio,
    })
    print("[DEBUG][patch_spatial] pair_category_counts:", {
        "subject_subject": subject_subject_count,
        "boundary_boundary": boundary_boundary_count,
        "background_background": background_background_count,
        "boundary_subject": boundary_subject_count,
        "background_subject": background_subject_count,
        "background_boundary": background_boundary_count,
    })
    print("[DEBUG][patch_spatial] endpoint_counts:", endpoint_counts)
    print("[DEBUG][patch_spatial] areas:", {
        "subject": subject_area,
        "boundary": boundary_area,
        "background": background_area,
    })        
    '''
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Interpretation
    # --------------------------------------------------
    pattern = "spatial_patch_recurrence_inconclusive"
    # subject-internal synthetic texture recurrence
    # Intended for fashion / virtual-model cases where repeated synthetic fabric
    # structure is concentrated inside the subject rather than bridging regions.
    if (
        subject_subject_count >= 6
        and subject_internal_ratio >= 0.08
        and subject_density_ratio >= 0.75
    ):
        pattern = "subject_internal_synthetic_recurrence"
    # Strongest case: almost all matched pairs bridge different regions
    elif (
        bridge_ratio >= 0.85
        and bridge_weight_ratio >= 0.85
        and boundary_involvement_ratio >= 0.50
        and bridge_count >= 8
    ):
        pattern = "cross_region_patch_recurrence"
    # Contour-band correlated recurrence
    elif (
        boundary_density_ratio >= 1.10
        and boundary_correlated_ratio >= 0.10
        and boundary_involvement_ratio >= 0.20
    ):
        pattern = "boundary_correlated_patch_recurrence"
    # Subject-focused recurrence
    elif (
        subject_density_ratio >= 1.10
        and subject_localized_ratio >= 0.10
    ):
        pattern = "subject_localized_patch_recurrence"
    # Broad recurrence across multiple regions
    elif (
        subject_density_ratio >= 1.02
        and boundary_density_ratio >= 1.02
        and bridge_ratio >= 0.30
    ):
        pattern = "scene_wide_patch_recurrence"
    elif (
        bridge_ratio < 0.30
        and 0.90 <= subject_density_ratio <= 1.10
        and 0.90 <= boundary_density_ratio <= 1.10
    ):
        pattern = "patch_spatial_convergence_supported"
    #--------------------------------------------------
    # Debug print for pattern interpretation
    #--------------------------------------------------
    '''''
    print("[DEBUG][patch_spatial][final]", {
        "bridge_count": bridge_count,
        "internal_count": internal_count,
        "bridge_ratio": bridge_ratio,
        "boundary_involvement_count": boundary_involvement_count,
        "boundary_involvement_ratio": boundary_involvement_ratio,
        "boundary_correlated_ratio": boundary_correlated_ratio,
        "subject_localized_ratio": subject_localized_ratio,
        "subject_subject_count": subject_subject_count,
        "subject_internal_ratio": subject_internal_ratio,
        "subject_internal_weight_ratio": subject_internal_weight_ratio,
    })
    '''''
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Output
    # --------------------------------------------------
    return {
        "patch_spatial": {
            "pattern": pattern,
            "region_stats": {
                "areas": {
                    "subject": float(subject_area),
                    "boundary": float(boundary_area),
                    "background": float(background_area),
                },
                "endpoint_densities": {
                    "subject": float(subject_endpoint_density),
                    "boundary": float(boundary_endpoint_density),
                    "background": float(background_endpoint_density),
                },
                "weight_densities": {
                    "subject": float(subject_weight_density),
                    "boundary": float(boundary_weight_density),
                    "background": float(background_weight_density),
                },                                                                
                "endpoint_counts": {
                    "subject": int(endpoint_counts["subject"]),
                    "boundary": int(endpoint_counts["boundary"]),
                    "background": int(endpoint_counts["background"]),
                },
                "endpoint_weights": {
                    "subject": float(endpoint_weights["subject"]),
                    "boundary": float(endpoint_weights["boundary"]),
                    "background": float(endpoint_weights["background"]),
                },
                "pair_category_counts": {
                    "subject_subject": int(subject_subject_count),
                    "boundary_boundary": int(boundary_boundary_count),
                    "background_background": int(background_background_count),
                    "boundary_subject": int(boundary_subject_count),
                    "background_subject": int(background_subject_count),
                    "background_boundary": int(background_boundary_count),
                },
                "pair_category_weights": {
                    "subject_subject": float(subject_subject_weight),
                    "boundary_boundary": float(boundary_boundary_weight),
                    "background_background": float(background_background_weight),
                    "boundary_subject": float(boundary_subject_weight),
                    "background_subject": float(background_subject_weight),
                    "background_boundary": float(background_boundary_weight),
                },
                "subject_subject_count": int(subject_subject_count),
                "subject_subject_weight": float(subject_subject_weight),                
                "total_pair_count": int(total_pair_count),
                "total_pair_weight": float(total_pair_weight),
                "bridge_count": int(bridge_count),
                "bridge_weight": float(bridge_weight),
                "internal_count": int(internal_count),
                "internal_weight": float(internal_weight),
                "boundary_involvement_count": int(boundary_involvement_count),
                "boundary_involvement_weight": float(boundary_involvement_weight),                
            },
            "comparative_features": {
                "subject_similarity_ratio": float(subject_density_ratio),
                "boundary_similarity_ratio": float(boundary_density_ratio),
                "subject_weight_ratio": float(subject_weight_ratio),
                "boundary_weight_ratio": float(boundary_weight_ratio),
                "bridge_ratio": float(bridge_ratio),
                "bridge_weight_ratio": float(bridge_weight_ratio),
                "boundary_involvement_ratio": float(boundary_involvement_ratio),
                "boundary_involvement_weight_ratio": float(boundary_involvement_weight_ratio),
                "boundary_correlated_ratio": float(boundary_correlated_ratio),
                "boundary_correlated_weight_ratio": float(boundary_correlated_weight_ratio),
                "subject_localized_ratio": float(subject_localized_ratio),
                "subject_localized_weight_ratio": float(subject_localized_weight_ratio),
                "cross_region_patch_reuse_ratio": float(cross_region_patch_reuse_ratio),
                "subject_internal_ratio": float(subject_internal_ratio),
                "subject_internal_weight_ratio": float(subject_internal_weight_ratio),
            },
            "artifacts": {
                "recurrence_map": patch.get("artifacts", {}).get("patch_similarity_heatmap"),
                "patch_similarity_overlay": patch.get("artifacts", {}).get("patch_similarity_overlay"),
            },
        }
    }
--- End of vf_patch_spatial.py ---

--- Start of vf_person_subject_mask.py ---
#vf_person_subject_mask.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
# Person-aware subject segmentation for region-aware forensic analysis.
# Preferred backend: MediaPipe Tasks ImageSegmenter using selfie_segmenter.tflite.
# Fallbacks: OpenCV HOG person detector, then conservative central-subject mask.
# 
# 
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple
import cv2
import json
import numpy as np
import sys
import contextlib
import os
from importlib.metadata import version, PackageNotFoundError
# local imports
from app_paths import app_dir, resource_path
from mask_contract import normalize_region_masks, person_mask_diagnostics

@contextlib.contextmanager
def suppress_native_stderr():
    """
    Suppress native/C++ stderr output, including some MediaPipe/TFLite warnings.
    In PyInstaller --windowed mode, sys.stderr may be None. In that case,
    do nothing instead of failing before MediaPipe can run.
    """
    if sys.stderr is None or not hasattr(sys.stderr, "fileno"):
        yield
        return
    try:
        stderr_fd = sys.stderr.fileno()
    except Exception:
        yield
        return
    try:
        saved_stderr_fd = os.dup(stderr_fd)
    except OSError:
        yield
        return
    try:
        with open(os.devnull, "w") as devnull:
            os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), stderr_fd)
            yield
    finally:
        try:
            os.dup2(saved_stderr_fd, stderr_fd)
        finally:
            os.close(saved_stderr_fd)

def _write_person_mask_debug(
    output_dir: Path,
    stem: str,
    payload: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    try:
        debug_path = output_dir / f"{stem}_person_mask_debug.json"
        debug_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    except Exception:
        # Never let debugging break analysis.
        pass


def _detect_person_mask_mediapipe_tasks(
    image_bgr: np.ndarray,
    model_path: str | Path = "selfie_segmenter.tflite",
    threshold: float = 0.50,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray | None, float]:
    import mediapipe as mp
    from mediapipe.tasks import python
    from mediapipe.tasks.python import vision
    model_path = Path(model_path)
    if not model_path.is_absolute():
        model_path = resource_path(model_path)    
    if not model_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"MediaPipe segmenter model not found: {model_path}. "
        )
    image_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    mp_image = mp.Image(image_format=mp.ImageFormat.SRGB, data=image_rgb)
    base_options = python.BaseOptions(model_asset_path=str(model_path))
    options = vision.ImageSegmenterOptions(
        base_options=base_options,
    )
    with vision.ImageSegmenter.create_from_options(options) as segmenter:
        result = segmenter.segment(mp_image)
    if getattr(result, "confidence_masks", None):
        mask = result.confidence_masks[0].numpy_view()
    elif getattr(result, "category_mask", None) is not None:
        mask = result.category_mask.numpy_view().astype(np.float32)
    else:
        return None, 0.0
    mask = _ensure_2d_mask(mask).astype(np.float32)
    binary = _refine_mediapipe_mask_from_confidence(
        conf_mask=mask,
        threshold=threshold,
        core_threshold=0.70,
    )    
    confidence = float(np.mean(mask))
    return binary, confidence

def _refine_mediapipe_mask_from_confidence(
    conf_mask: np.ndarray,
    threshold: float = 0.50,
    core_threshold: float = 0.70,
) -> np.ndarray:
    conf_mask = _ensure_2d_mask(conf_mask).astype(np.float32)
    base = (conf_mask > threshold).astype(np.uint8)
    core = (conf_mask > core_threshold).astype(np.uint8)
    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(
        base,
        connectivity=8,
    )
    keep = np.zeros_like(base, dtype=np.uint8)
    core_labels = set(np.unique(labels[core > 0]).tolist())
    core_labels.discard(0)
    for label in core_labels:
        keep[labels == label] = 1
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (3, 3))
    keep = cv2.morphologyEx(keep, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)
    return keep.astype(np.uint8)

def _ensure_2d_mask(mask: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    mask = np.asarray(mask)
    if mask.ndim == 3:
        if mask.shape[2] == 1:
            mask = mask[:, :, 0]
        else:
            mask = mask[:, :, 0]
    if mask.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"Expected 2D mask, got shape {mask.shape}")
    return mask

# for non mediapipe masks
def _clean_mask(mask: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    mask_u8 = (mask > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    kernel_close = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (21, 21))
    kernel_open = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (9, 9))
    mask_u8 = cv2.morphologyEx(mask_u8, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel_close)
    mask_u8 = cv2.morphologyEx(mask_u8, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel_open)
    largest, _, _ = _largest_component_stats(mask_u8)
    return largest.astype(np.uint8)

def _safe_area_ratio(mask: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(np.count_nonzero(mask)) / max(float(mask.size), 1.0)

def _largest_component_stats(mask: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int, float]:
    mask_u8 = (mask > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(mask_u8, connectivity=8)
    if num_labels <= 1:
        return mask_u8, 0, 0.0
    best_label = 1
    best_area = 0
    component_count = 0
    for label in range(1, num_labels):
        area = int(stats[label, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA])
        if area <= 0:
            continue
        component_count += 1
        if area > best_area:
            best_area = area
            best_label = label
    largest = (labels == best_label).astype(np.uint8)
    total_area = max(int(np.count_nonzero(mask_u8)), 1)
    largest_component_ratio = float(best_area) / float(total_area)
    return largest, component_count, largest_component_ratio

def _make_bbox_mask(shape_hw: Tuple[int, int], bbox: Tuple[int, int, int, int]) -> np.ndarray:
    h, w = shape_hw
    x, y, bw, bh = bbox
    x0 = max(0, int(x))
    y0 = max(0, int(y))
    x1 = min(w, int(x + bw))
    y1 = min(h, int(y + bh))
    mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)
    if x1 > x0 and y1 > y0:
        mask[y0:y1, x0:x1] = 1
    return mask

def _detect_person_bbox_hog(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> tuple[Tuple[int, int, int, int] | None, float]:
    """
    Lightweight first-pass person detector.
    This is deliberately conservative and replaceable. It returns the largest detected
    person-like bounding box plus a detector confidence proxy.
    """
    hog = cv2.HOGDescriptor()
    hog.setSVMDetector(cv2.HOGDescriptor_getDefaultPeopleDetector())
    rects, weights = hog.detectMultiScale(
        image_bgr,
        winStride=(8, 8),
        padding=(16, 16),
        scale=1.05,
    )
    if len(rects) == 0:
        return None, 0.0
    best_idx = int(np.argmax([w * h for x, y, w, h in rects]))
    bbox = tuple(int(v) for v in rects[best_idx])
    confidence = float(weights[best_idx]) if len(weights) > best_idx else 0.5
    return bbox, confidence

def _fallback_center_subject_mask(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Fallback mask used only when person detection fails.
    It deliberately returns a conservative central foreground region, not a final
    reliable person mask. QA should mark this as low reliability.
    """
    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    x0 = int(round(w * 0.20))
    x1 = int(round(w * 0.80))
    y0 = int(round(h * 0.08))
    y1 = int(round(h * 0.95))
    mask = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)
    mask[y0:y1, x0:x1] = 1
    return mask

def _quality_from_mask(
    mask: np.ndarray,
    person_detected: bool,
    detector_confidence: float,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    area_ratio = _safe_area_ratio(mask)
    _, component_count, largest_component_ratio = _largest_component_stats(mask)
    if (
        person_detected
        and 0.08 <= area_ratio <= 0.85
        and largest_component_ratio >= 0.70
        and component_count <= 3
    ):
        reliability = "high"
        pattern = "person_subject_mask_generated"
    elif (
        person_detected
        and 0.03 <= area_ratio <= 0.90
        and largest_component_ratio >= 0.50
    ):
        reliability = "medium"
        pattern = "person_subject_mask_generated_low_confidence"
    else:
        reliability = "low"
        pattern = "person_subject_mask_unreliable"
    reasons = []
    if not person_detected:
        reasons.append("No detected person supports this mask.")
    if not 0.03 <= area_ratio <= 0.90:
        reasons.append(f"Foreground area ratio {area_ratio:.6f} is outside the existing usable range [0.03, 0.90].")
    if largest_component_ratio < 0.50:
        reasons.append(f"Largest-component coverage {largest_component_ratio:.6f} is below the existing 0.50 usable minimum.")
    if reliability == "medium":
        reasons.append("Usable with limitations: the existing high-quality area/component criteria were not all met.")
    return {
        "pattern": pattern,
        "person_detected": bool(person_detected),
        "detector_confidence": float(detector_confidence),
        "subject_area_ratio": float(area_ratio),
        "largest_component_ratio": float(largest_component_ratio),
        "component_count": int(component_count),
        "mask_reliability": reliability,
        "quality_reasons": reasons,
    }

def _save_mask(mask: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(path), (mask > 0).astype(np.uint8) * 255)

def _save_overlay(image_bgr: np.ndarray, mask: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> None:
    mask = _ensure_2d_mask(mask).astype(np.uint8)
    if mask.shape != image_bgr.shape[:2]:
        mask = cv2.resize(
            mask,
            (image_bgr.shape[1], image_bgr.shape[0]),
            interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST,
        )
    overlay = image_bgr.copy()
    color_layer = np.zeros_like(image_bgr)
    color_layer[:, :, 1] = 220
    blended = cv2.addWeighted(image_bgr, 0.55, color_layer, 0.45, 0.0)
    mask_3 = mask[:, :, None].astype(bool)
    overlay = np.where(mask_3, blended, overlay)
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(
        mask.astype(np.uint8),
        cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
        cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE,
    )
    cv2.drawContours(overlay, contours, -1, (80, 255, 120), 2)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(path), overlay)
    
def run_person_subject_mask_analysis(
    image_path: str | Path,
    output_dir: str | Path,
    backend: str = "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter",
    model_name: str = "selfie_segmenter.tflite",
    conf: float = 0.25,
    imgsz: int = 640,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    image_bgr = cv2.imread(str(image_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if image_bgr is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load image: {image_path}")
    h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    raw_mask: np.ndarray | None = None
    detector_confidence = 0.0
    person_count = 0
    person_detected = False
    detection_backend = "none"
    backend_error: str | None = None    
    backend_errors = []
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Preferred backend: MediaPipe
# ------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        with suppress_native_stderr():
            raw_mask, detector_confidence = _detect_person_mask_mediapipe_tasks(
                image_bgr=image_bgr,
                model_path=model_name,
                threshold=0.60,
            )
        if raw_mask is not None:
            person_detected = bool(np.any(raw_mask))
            person_count = int(person_detected)
            detection_backend = "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter"
    except Exception as e:
        backend_error = str(e)
        backend_errors.append({"backend": "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter", "error": str(e)})
        print(f"[PersonMask] MediaPipe Tasks failed, falling back to HOG: {e}")
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fallback backend: OpenCV HOG person detector
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    if raw_mask is None:
        try:
            bbox, hog_confidence = _detect_person_bbox_hog(image_bgr)
        except Exception as exc:
            backend_errors.append({"backend": "opencv_hog", "error": str(exc)})
            bbox, hog_confidence = None, 0.0
        if bbox is not None:
            raw_mask = _make_bbox_mask((h, w), bbox)
            detector_confidence = float(hog_confidence)
            person_count = 1
            person_detected = True
            detection_backend = "opencv_hog"
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Last-resort fallback: conservative central subject box
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    if raw_mask is None:
        raw_mask = _fallback_center_subject_mask(image_bgr)
        detector_confidence = 0.0
        person_count = 0
        person_detected = False
        detection_backend = "fallback_center_subject"
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Clean and validate mask
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    if detection_backend.startswith("mediapipe"):
        mask = raw_mask.astype(np.uint8)
    else:
        mask = _clean_mask(raw_mask)
    quality = _quality_from_mask(
        mask=mask,
        person_detected=person_detected,
        detector_confidence=detector_confidence,
    )
    person_mask_reliability = quality["mask_reliability"]
    quality["person_instance_count"] = int(person_count)
    quality["backend_error"] = backend_error
    stem = image_path.stem
    # debug 
    model_candidate = resource_path("selfie_segmenter.tflite")
    debug_info: dict[str, Any] = {
        "stage": "person_subject_mask",
        "image_path": str(image_path),
        "output_dir": str(output_dir),
        "frozen": bool(getattr(sys, "frozen", False)),
        "executable": str(sys.executable),
        "cwd": str(Path.cwd()),
        "app_dir": str(app_dir()),
        "_MEIPASS": str(getattr(sys, "_MEIPASS", "")),
        "selfie_segmenter_resource_path": str(model_candidate),
        "selfie_segmenter_resource_exists": bool(model_candidate.exists()),
    }
    
    mask_path = output_dir / f"{stem}_person_subject_mask.png"
    overlay_path = output_dir / f"{stem}_person_subject_mask_overlay.png"
    _save_mask(mask, mask_path)
    _save_overlay(image_bgr, mask, overlay_path)
    # write debug info
    debug_info.update({
        "detection_backend": detection_backend,
        "backend_error": backend_error,
        "person_detected": bool(person_detected),
        "person_count": int(person_count),
        "detector_confidence": float(detector_confidence),
        "mask_shape": list(mask.shape) if mask is not None else None,
        "mask_nonzero": int(np.count_nonzero(mask)) if mask is not None else 0,
        "mask_area_ratio": float(_safe_area_ratio(mask)) if mask is not None else 0.0,
        "quality": quality,
    })
    try:
        backend_version = version("mediapipe") if detection_backend == "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter" else cv2.__version__
    except PackageNotFoundError:
        backend_version = None
    payload = {
        "person_subject_mask": {
            "pattern": quality["pattern"],
            "status": (
                "ok"
                if quality["mask_reliability"] in {"high", "medium"}
                else "unreliable"
            ),
            "person_mask_reliability": person_mask_reliability,
            "backend": detection_backend,
            "requested_backend": backend,
            "backend_version": backend_version,
            "backend_errors": backend_errors,
            "confidence_kind": "mean foreground confidence, not a person-detection probability" if detection_backend == "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter" else "HOG score proxy or unavailable",
            "fallback_reason": backend_error or ("No MediaPipe person mask was produced." if detection_backend != "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter" else None),
            "model_name": model_name if detection_backend == "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter" else None,
            "mask_path": str(mask_path),
            "overlay_path": str(overlay_path),
            "quality": quality,
            "image_geometry": {
                "width": int(w),
                "height": int(h),
            },
        }
    }
    diagnostics = person_mask_diagnostics(payload)
    payload["person_subject_mask"].update({key: diagnostics[key] for key in (
        "mask_status", "mask_source", "mask_reliability", "person_specific", "fallback_used", "fallback_reason",
    )})
    payload["person_subject_mask"]["person_mask_reliability"] = diagnostics["person_mask_reliability"]
    if diagnostics["mask_status"] in {"approximate", "unavailable", "error"}:
        payload["person_subject_mask"]["status"] = diagnostics["mask_status"]
    debug_info.update(backend_version=backend_version, mask_status=diagnostics["mask_status"],
                      rejection_reasons=diagnostics["rejection_reasons"], backend_errors=backend_errors)
    _write_person_mask_debug(output_dir, stem, debug_info)
    return payload

def derive_region_masks_from_person_mask(person_mask_path: str | Path, image_path: str | Path, output_dir: str | Path, person_mask_reliability: str | None = None, mask_source: str = "mediapipe_tasks_image_segmenter", person_specific: bool = True,) -> dict:
    person_mask = cv2.imread(str(person_mask_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if person_mask is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load person mask: {person_mask_path}")
    subject = (person_mask > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (21, 21))
    dilated = cv2.dilate(subject, kernel, iterations=1)
    eroded = cv2.erode(subject, kernel, iterations=1)
    boundary = ((dilated > 0) & (eroded == 0)).astype(np.uint8)
    background = (subject == 0).astype(np.uint8)
    stem = Path(image_path).stem
    out = Path(output_dir)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    subject_path = out / f"{stem}_person_subject_region_mask.png"
    boundary_path = out / f"{stem}_person_boundary_band_mask.png"
    background_path = out / f"{stem}_person_background_mask.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(subject_path), subject * 255)
    cv2.imwrite(str(boundary_path), boundary * 255)
    cv2.imwrite(str(background_path), background * 255)
    image_area = max(subject.size, 1)
    h, w = subject.shape
    payload = {
        "region_masks": {
            "shape": [int(h), int(w)],
            "height": int(h),
            "width": int(w),
            "source": "person_subject_mask",
            "person_mask_reliability": person_mask_reliability,
            "subject_mask_path": str(subject_path),
            "boundary_band_path": str(boundary_path),
            "background_mask_path": str(background_path),
            "subject_ratio": float(np.count_nonzero(subject) / image_area),
            "boundary_ratio": float(np.count_nonzero(boundary) / image_area),
            "background_ratio": float(np.count_nonzero(background) / image_area),
        }
    }
    return normalize_region_masks(
        payload, mask_source=mask_source, mask_reliability=person_mask_reliability or "low",
        mask_status=("reliable" if person_mask_reliability == "high" else "limited" if person_mask_reliability == "medium" else "unreliable") if person_specific else "approximate",
        person_specific=person_specific, fallback_used=not person_specific,
        fallback_reason="Bounding-box geometry is approximate." if not person_specific else None,
    )

--- End of vf_person_subject_mask.py ---

--- Start of vf_region_fft.py ---
#vf_region_fft.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages
#

import numpy as np
import cv2
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple
# locaal import, reuse existing FFT helpers
from vf_fft import run_fft_analysis


def _load_mask(path: str | Path) -> np.ndarray:
    m = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if m is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load mask: {path}")
    return (m > 127).astype(np.uint8)

def _extract_fft_metrics(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    fft = payload["fft"]
    return {
        "fft_anomaly_score": float(fft.get("fft_anomaly_score", 0.0)),
        "peak_periodicity_score": float(fft.get("peak_periodicity_score", 0.0)),
        "peak_symmetry_score": float(fft.get("peak_symmetry_score", 0.0)),
        "anisotropy_score": float(fft.get("anisotropy_score", 0.0)),
        "axis_bias_score": float(fft.get("axis_bias_score", 0.0)),
        "grid_response_score": float(fft.get("grid_response_score", 0.0)),
    }

def _mask_bbox(mask: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[int, int, int, int] | None:
    ys, xs = np.where(mask > 0)
    if len(xs) == 0 or len(ys) == 0:
        return None
    x0, x1 = int(xs.min()), int(xs.max())
    y0, y1 = int(ys.min()), int(ys.max())
    return x0, y0, x1, y1

def _expand_bbox(
    bbox: Tuple[int, int, int, int],
    shape: Tuple[int, int],
    pad: int = 24,
) -> Tuple[int, int, int, int]:
    h, w = shape
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = bbox
    x0 = max(0, x0 - pad)
    y0 = max(0, y0 - pad)
    x1 = min(w - 1, x1 + pad)
    y1 = min(h - 1, y1 + pad)
    return x0, y0, x1, y1

def _soften_mask(mask_crop: np.ndarray, blur_sigma: float = 5.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Convert binary crop mask to soft mask in [0,1].
    """
    m = mask_crop.astype(np.float32)
    if blur_sigma > 0:
        m = cv2.GaussianBlur(m, (0, 0), sigmaX=blur_sigma, sigmaY=blur_sigma)
    m = np.clip(m, 0.0, 1.0)
    return m

def _extract_soft_region_crop(
    image_gray: np.ndarray,
    mask: np.ndarray,
    pad: int = 24,
    blur_sigma: float = 5.0,
    fill_mode: str = "mean",
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Extract a cropped softly-masked patch for FFT analysis.
    fill_mode:
        "mean"  -> fill outside region with mean intensity from inside region
        "blur"  -> fill outside region with a blurred local crop
    """
    bbox = _mask_bbox(mask)
    if bbox is None:
        raise ValueError("Mask is empty; cannot extract region crop.")
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = _expand_bbox(bbox, image_gray.shape, pad=pad)
    img_crop = image_gray[y0:y1 + 1, x0:x1 + 1].astype(np.float32)
    mask_crop = mask[y0:y1 + 1, x0:x1 + 1].astype(np.uint8)
    soft_mask = _soften_mask(mask_crop, blur_sigma=blur_sigma)
    inside_vals = img_crop[mask_crop > 0]
    region_mean = float(np.mean(inside_vals)) if inside_vals.size else float(np.mean(img_crop))
    if fill_mode == "blur":
        background_fill = cv2.GaussianBlur(img_crop, (0, 0), sigmaX=7.0, sigmaY=7.0)
    else:
        background_fill = np.full_like(img_crop, region_mean, dtype=np.float32)
    out = img_crop * soft_mask + background_fill * (1.0 - soft_mask)
    # normalize back to uint8-safe grayscale range
    out = np.clip(out, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    return out

def _align_mask_to_image(mask: np.ndarray, image_gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    if mask.shape == image_gray.shape:
        return mask.astype(np.uint8)
    return cv2.resize(
        mask.astype(np.uint8),
        (image_gray.shape[1], image_gray.shape[0]),
        interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST,
    )

def run_region_fft_analysis(image_path: str | Path, region_masks_payload: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str | Path, ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    img = cv2.imread(str(image_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if img is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load image: {image_path}")
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Load masks
    # --------------------------------------------------
    masks = region_masks_payload["region_masks"]
    subject_mask = _load_mask(masks["subject_mask_path"])
    boundary_mask = _load_mask(masks["boundary_band_path"])
    background_mask = _load_mask(masks["background_mask_path"])
    # align masks and log initial mismatch
    if subject_mask.shape != img.shape:
        print(f"[RegionFFT] Aligning mask from {subject_mask.shape} to image {img.shape}")    
    subject_mask = _align_mask_to_image(subject_mask, img)
    boundary_mask = _align_mask_to_image(boundary_mask, img)
    background_mask = _align_mask_to_image(background_mask, img)        
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Apply masks
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_img = _extract_soft_region_crop(
        image_gray=img,
        mask=subject_mask,
        pad=24,
        blur_sigma=5.0, 
        fill_mode="mean",
    )
    boundary_img = _extract_soft_region_crop(
        image_gray=img,
        mask=boundary_mask,
        pad=20,
        blur_sigma=4.0,
        fill_mode="mean",
    )
    background_img = _extract_soft_region_crop(
        image_gray=img,
        mask=background_mask,
        pad=24,
        blur_sigma=5.0,
        fill_mode="mean",
    )
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Save temporary masked images
    # (so we can reuse existing FFT pipeline)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_subject_tmp.png"
    boundary_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_boundary_tmp.png"
    background_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_background_tmp.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(subject_path), subject_img)
    cv2.imwrite(str(boundary_path), boundary_img)
    cv2.imwrite(str(background_path), background_img)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Run FFT per region
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_fft = run_fft_analysis(subject_path, output_dir, presentation_mode_override=False)
    boundary_fft = run_fft_analysis(boundary_path, output_dir, presentation_mode_override=False)
    background_fft = run_fft_analysis(background_path, output_dir, presentation_mode_override=False)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Extract comparable metrics
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_metrics = _extract_fft_metrics(subject_fft)
    boundary_metrics = _extract_fft_metrics(boundary_fft)
    background_metrics = _extract_fft_metrics(background_fft)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Comparative features
    # --------------------------------------------------
    def safe_ratio(a, b):
        return float(a / (b + 1e-6))
    features = {
        "subject_vs_background_fft_gap":
            subject_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"] - background_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"],
        "boundary_vs_subject_fft_gap":
            boundary_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"] - subject_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"],
        "boundary_vs_background_fft_gap":
            boundary_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"] - background_metrics["fft_anomaly_score"],
        "subject_grid_response_ratio":
            safe_ratio(subject_metrics["grid_response_score"], background_metrics["grid_response_score"]),
        "boundary_grid_response_ratio":
            safe_ratio(boundary_metrics["grid_response_score"], background_metrics["grid_response_score"]),
        "boundary_minus_subject_grid_response":
            boundary_metrics["grid_response_score"] - subject_metrics["grid_response_score"],            
    }
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Interpretation (very conservative)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    b_gap = features["boundary_vs_background_fft_gap"]
    s_gap = features["subject_vs_background_fft_gap"]
    bs_gap = features["boundary_vs_subject_fft_gap"]
