    b_ratio = features["boundary_grid_response_ratio"]
    s_ratio = features["subject_grid_response_ratio"]
    pattern = "region_frequency_inconclusive"
    # Strong localized mismatch at contour band
    if (
        b_ratio >= 1.35
        and bs_gap >= 0.0
    ):
        pattern = "boundary_band_frequency_mismatch"
    # Subject and boundary both elevated relative to background
    elif (
        s_ratio >= 1.40
        and b_ratio >= 1.35
    ):
        pattern = "region_frequency_elevated"
    # Subject clearly elevated relative to background
    elif s_ratio >= 1.30:
        pattern = "subject_background_frequency_mismatch"
    # Near-convergence
    elif (
        0.90 <= s_ratio <= 1.10
        and 0.90 <= b_ratio <= 1.10
        and abs(s_gap) < 0.03
        and abs(b_gap) < 0.03
    ):
        pattern = "region_frequency_convergence_supported"
    #print("[DEBUG][region_fft] subject_metrics:", subject_metrics)
    #print("[DEBUG][region_fft] boundary_metrics:", boundary_metrics)
    #print("[DEBUG][region_fft] background_metrics:", background_metrics)
    #print("[DEBUG][region_fft] features:", features)
    #print("[DEBUG][region_fft] pattern:", pattern)    
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Output
    # --------------------------------------------------
    return {
        "region_fft": {
            "pattern": pattern,
            "subject_metrics": subject_metrics,
            "boundary_metrics": boundary_metrics,
            "background_metrics": background_metrics,
            "comparative_features": features,
            "artifacts": {
                "subject_input": str(subject_path),
                "boundary_input": str(boundary_path),
                "background_input": str(background_path),
            }
        }
    }
--- End of vf_region_fft.py ---

--- Start of vf_region_masks.py ---
#vf_region_masks.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Imgages
#
import numpy as np
import cv2
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict
from mask_contract import normalize_region_masks, StageUnavailable

def _normalize_to_u8(img: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    img = img.astype(np.float32)
    mn, mx = img.min(), img.max()
    if mx - mn < 1e-8:
        return np.zeros_like(img, dtype=np.uint8)
    return ((img - mn) / (mx - mn) * 255).astype(np.uint8)

def _largest_connected_component(mask: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(mask, connectivity=8)
    if num_labels <= 1:
        return mask

    largest = 1 + np.argmax(stats[1:, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA])
    return (labels == largest).astype(np.uint8)

def _create_boundary_band(subject_mask: np.ndarray, width: int) -> np.ndarray:
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (width, width))
    dilated = cv2.dilate(subject_mask, kernel)
    eroded = cv2.erode(subject_mask, kernel)
    band = dilated - eroded
    band = np.clip(band, 0, 1)
    return band.astype(np.uint8)

def _create_preview(image: np.ndarray, subject, boundary, background) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    RGB overlay:
    - subject → green
    - boundary → red
    - background → blue (very light)
    """
    preview = image.copy().astype(np.float32)
    # normalize image if needed
    if preview.max() <= 1.0:
        preview *= 255.0
    # overlays
    preview[subject == 1] *= [0.6, 1.0, 0.6]
    preview[boundary == 1] *= [1.0, 0.6, 0.6]
    preview[background == 1] *= [0.9, 0.9, 1.0]
    return np.clip(preview, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)

def run_region_mask_analysis(image_path: str | Path, output_dir: str | Path, boundary_width_px: int = 18, ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    img_bgr = cv2.imread(str(image_path))
    if img_bgr is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load image: {image_path}")
    img_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1: Edge + saliency proxy
    # --------------------------------------------------
    edges = cv2.Canny(gray, 60, 120)
    # blur to create coarse saliency
    saliency = cv2.GaussianBlur(edges.astype(np.float32), (0, 0), sigmaX=5)
    saliency_u8 = _normalize_to_u8(saliency)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2: Threshold → foreground
    # --------------------------------------------------
    _, mask = cv2.threshold(saliency_u8, 0, 1, cv2.THRESH_BINARY + cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3: Keep largest component
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_mask = _largest_connected_component(mask)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4: Clean mask
    # --------------------------------------------------
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (9, 9))
    subject_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(subject_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)
    subject_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(subject_mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)
    subject_mask = (subject_mask > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5: Boundary band
    # --------------------------------------------------
    boundary_band = _create_boundary_band(subject_mask, boundary_width_px)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6: Background
    # --------------------------------------------------
    background_mask = (1 - np.clip(subject_mask + boundary_band, 0, 1)).astype(np.uint8)
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 7: Save masks
    # --------------------------------------------------
    subject_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_subject_mask.png"
    boundary_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_boundary_band.png"
    background_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_background_mask.png"
    preview_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_region_preview.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(subject_path), subject_mask * 255)
    cv2.imwrite(str(boundary_path), boundary_band * 255)
    cv2.imwrite(str(background_path), background_mask * 255)
    preview = _create_preview(img_rgb, subject_mask, boundary_band, background_mask)
    cv2.imwrite(str(preview_path), cv2.cvtColor(preview, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
    # --------------------------------------------------
    # Step 8: stats
    # --------------------------------------------------
    total = float(h * w)
    ratios = {
        "subject": float(np.sum(subject_mask) / total),
        "boundary": float(np.sum(boundary_band) / total),
        "background": float(np.sum(background_mask) / total),
    }
    payload = {
        "region_masks": {
            "subject_mask_path": str(subject_path),
            "boundary_band_path": str(boundary_path),
            "background_mask_path": str(background_path),
            "preview_path": str(preview_path),
            "shape": [h, w],
            "ratios": ratios,
        }
    }
    if any(ratios[name] <= 0 for name in ("subject", "boundary", "background")):
        raise StageUnavailable("Legacy saliency did not produce meaningful foreground/background/boundary regions.")
    return normalize_region_masks(
        payload, mask_source="legacy_edge_saliency", mask_reliability="low",
        mask_status="approximate", person_specific=False, fallback_used=True,
        fallback_reason="Edge-saliency regions are approximate and do not identify a person contour.",
        background_excludes_boundary=True,
    )

--- End of vf_region_masks.py ---

--- Start of vf_region_segmentation.py ---
#vf_region_segmentation.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

import cv2
# local imports
from vf_subject_process_asymmetry import _load_mask

def run_region_segmentation_analysis(region_masks_payload) -> dict:
    masks = region_masks_payload.get("region_masks", {})

    subject_mask = _load_mask(masks["subject_mask_path"])
    boundary_mask = _load_mask(masks["boundary_band_path"])
    background_mask = _load_mask(masks["background_mask_path"])

    total_pixels = subject_mask.size

    subject_ratio = float(subject_mask.sum()) / total_pixels
    boundary_ratio = float(boundary_mask.sum()) / total_pixels
    background_ratio = float(background_mask.sum()) / total_pixels

    # --- Fragmentation ---
    num_labels, labels = cv2.connectedComponents(subject_mask)
    component_count = num_labels - 1

    component_sizes = [
        (labels == i).sum() for i in range(1, num_labels)
    ]
    largest_component_ratio = (
        max(component_sizes) / subject_mask.sum()
        if component_sizes else 0.0
    )

    # --- Boundary density ---
    boundary_density = boundary_ratio / (subject_ratio + 1e-6)

    # --- Heuristic scoring ---
    pattern = "segmentation_convergence_supported"
    score = 0.0

    if component_count > 5 and largest_component_ratio < 0.6:
        pattern = "fragmented_subject_mask"
        score = 0.7

    elif boundary_density < 0.05:
        pattern = "unnaturally_clean_segmentation"
        score = 0.6

    elif subject_ratio > 0.85 or subject_ratio < 0.05:
        pattern = "dominant_subject_ratio_anomaly"
        score = 0.5

    return {
        "region_segmentation": {
            "pattern": pattern,
            "score": score,
            "ratios": {
                "subject": subject_ratio,
                "boundary": boundary_ratio,
                "background": background_ratio,
            },
            "metrics": {
                "component_count": component_count,
                "largest_component_ratio": largest_component_ratio,
                "boundary_density": boundary_density,
            },
        }
    }
--- End of vf_region_segmentation.py ---

--- Start of vf_segmentation_alignment.py ---
#vf_segmentation_alignment.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#

import numpy as np
import cv2

def _overlap_ratio(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    # both binary masks (0/1 or 0/255)
    a_bin = (a > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    b_bin = (b > 0).astype(np.uint8)
    inter = (a_bin & b_bin).sum()
    total = a_bin.sum()
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(inter) / float(total)

def _load_bin_mask(path) -> np.ndarray:
    m = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if m is None:
        return None
    return (m > 0).astype(np.uint8)

def compute_segmentation_boundary_alignment(cross_payload, region_masks_payload):
    if cross_payload is None or region_masks_payload is None:
        return None
    hotspot = cross_payload.get("hotspot_mask")  # or load from file if you save it
    if hotspot is None:
        return None

    masks = region_masks_payload.get("region_masks", {})
    boundary_path = masks.get("boundary_band_path")
    boundary = _load_bin_mask(boundary_path)
    if boundary is None:
        return None
    # Hotspots use the ELA/residual image grid. Person masks may use a resized
    # gatekeeper image; align coordinates instead of cropping the hotspot grid.
    original_hotspot_shape = list(hotspot.shape)
    if hotspot.shape != boundary.shape:
        hotspot = cv2.resize(hotspot.astype(np.uint8), (boundary.shape[1], boundary.shape[0]), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)

    # Raw overlap: how much of hotspots sit on boundary
    raw_align = _overlap_ratio(hotspot, boundary)

    # Normalize by boundary size to avoid trivial hits
    boundary_coverage = boundary.sum() / (hotspot.size + 1e-6)

    # Final score (penalize huge boundaries)
    score = raw_align * (1.0 - np.clip(boundary_coverage, 0.0, 0.7))

    return {
        "alignment_score": float(score),
        "raw_overlap": float(raw_align),
        "boundary_coverage": float(boundary_coverage),
        "hotspot_input_shape": original_hotspot_shape,
        "boundary_shape": list(boundary.shape),
    }
    

--- End of vf_segmentation_alignment.py ---

--- Start of vf_sensor_origin.py ---
#vf_sensor_origin.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#
# CFA colour filter array - demosaicing consistency module
# We use a high-pass residual + 2×2 periodicity consistency approach, real camera images often retain weak CFA / demosaicing / acquisition periodicity
# synthetic images, screenshots, and some heavily manipulated regions may show weaker periodic acquisition traces, spatial inconsistency in those traces
# output patterns: camera_like_acquisition_supported, sensor_origin_inconclusive, weak_or_absent_sensor_origin_traces, localized_sensor_trace_inconsistency
#

from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import math
from typing import Any
import cv2
import numpy as np

#----------------------------------------
# Helpers
#----------------------------------------

def clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, float(x)))

def ensure_uint8_gray(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    if gray.dtype != np.uint8:
        gray = np.clip(gray, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    return gray

def highpass_residual(gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    gray_f = gray.astype(np.float32)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_f, (0, 0), sigmaX=1.2, sigmaY=1.2)
    resid = gray_f - blur
    return resid

def block_view_shape(h: int, w: int, block_size: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    return math.ceil(h / block_size), math.ceil(w / block_size)

def compute_block_maps(residual: np.ndarray, block_size: int = 32, ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Returns:
      periodicity_map: blockwise 2x2 acquisition-like periodicity strength
      energy_map: blockwise residual energy
    """
    h, w = residual.shape
    rows, cols = block_view_shape(h, w, block_size)
    periodicity_map = np.zeros((rows, cols), dtype=np.float32)
    energy_map = np.zeros((rows, cols), dtype=np.float32)
    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            y0 = r * block_size
            x0 = c * block_size
            block = residual[y0:y0 + block_size, x0:x0 + block_size]
            if block.size < 16:
                continue
            energy = float(np.mean(np.abs(block)))
            energy_map[r, c] = energy
            # 2x2 phase means
            p00 = float(np.mean(np.abs(block[0::2, 0::2]))) if block[0::2, 0::2].size else 0.0
            p01 = float(np.mean(np.abs(block[0::2, 1::2]))) if block[0::2, 1::2].size else 0.0
            p10 = float(np.mean(np.abs(block[1::2, 0::2]))) if block[1::2, 0::2].size else 0.0
            p11 = float(np.mean(np.abs(block[1::2, 1::2]))) if block[1::2, 1::2].size else 0.0
            phases = np.array([p00, p01, p10, p11], dtype=np.float32)
            mu = float(np.mean(phases)) + 1e-6
            sigma = float(np.std(phases))
            # Relative phase structure: higher can indicate acquisition periodicity
            periodicity = sigma / mu
            periodicity_map[r, c] = periodicity
    return periodicity_map, energy_map

def normalize_map(m: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    if m.size == 0:
        return m
    lo = float(np.min(m))
    hi = float(np.max(m))
    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
        return np.zeros_like(m, dtype=np.float32)
    return (m - lo) / (hi - lo)

def build_top_blocks(score_map: np.ndarray, top_k: int = 12) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows, cols = score_map.shape
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            items.append({
                "row": int(r),
                "col": int(c),
                "score": float(score_map[r, c]),
            })
    items.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
    return items[:top_k]

def create_sensor_overlay(image_bgr: np.ndarray, score_map: np.ndarray, out_path: Path, block_size: int = 32,) -> str:
    vis = image_bgr.copy()
    rows, cols = score_map.shape
    score_map_n = normalize_map(score_map)
    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            score = float(score_map_n[r, c])
            if score < 0.55:
                continue
            y0 = r * block_size
            x0 = c * block_size
            y1 = min(y0 + block_size, vis.shape[0])
            x1 = min(x0 + block_size, vis.shape[1])
            color = (0, int(255 * (1.0 - score)), int(255 * score))
            cv2.rectangle(vis, (x0, y0), (x1, y1), color, 1)
    cv2.imwrite(str(out_path), vis)
    return str(out_path)

def interpret_sensor_origin(global_trace_strength: float, block_consistency_score: float, localized_inconsistency_score: float,) -> tuple[str, str]:
    # ✅ Camera-like support (phone + DSLR)
    if (
        global_trace_strength >= 0.035
        and block_consistency_score >= 0.65
        and localized_inconsistency_score < 0.35
    ):
        return (
            "camera_like_acquisition_supported",
            "Sensor-origin analysis detects spatially coherent microstructure compatible with a camera capture pipeline."
        )
    # 🚨 Suspicious ONLY if clearly extreme
    if (
        global_trace_strength >= 0.14
        and block_consistency_score < 0.60
        and localized_inconsistency_score >= 0.40
    ):
        return (
            "localized_sensor_trace_inconsistency",
            "Microstructure is unusually strong and spatially inconsistent, which may indicate a disrupted or non-standard acquisition pattern."
        )
    # Very weak → inconclusive (not suspicious!)
    if global_trace_strength < 0.03:
        return (
            "weak_camera_pipeline_supported",
            "Sensor-origin microstructure is weak but not abnormal, which remains compatible with a modern camera processing pipeline."
        )
    return (
        "sensor_origin_inconclusive",
        "Sensor-origin analysis did not produce a strong camera-like or strongly inconsistent reading."
    )
    
#-----------------------------------------------
# Detector Analysis
#-----------------------------------------------
def run_sensor_origin_analysis(image_path: str | Path, output_dir: str | Path, block_size: int = 32,) -> dict[str, Any]:
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    image_bgr = cv2.imread(str(image_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if image_bgr is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Could not read image: {image_path}")

    gray = ensure_uint8_gray(image_bgr)
    residual = highpass_residual(gray)
    periodicity_map, energy_map = compute_block_maps(residual, block_size=block_size)
    # Normalize
    periodicity_n = normalize_map(periodicity_map)
    energy_n = normalize_map(energy_map)
    # We care most about periodic residual structure, but only where some residual energy exists
    support_map = periodicity_n * (0.35 + 0.65 * energy_n)
    global_trace_strength = float(np.mean(support_map)) if support_map.size else 0.0
    # Consistency: if support varies too wildly across blocks, consistency drops
    block_std = float(np.std(support_map)) if support_map.size else 0.0
    block_consistency_score = clamp01(1.0 - (block_std / 0.30))
    # Inconsistency: strong if some blocks differ strongly from overall support
    if support_map.size:
        mu = float(np.mean(support_map))
        localized_inconsistency_score = clamp01(float(np.mean(np.abs(support_map - mu))) / 0.22)
    else:
        localized_inconsistency_score = 0.0
    pattern, note = interpret_sensor_origin(
        global_trace_strength=global_trace_strength,
        block_consistency_score=block_consistency_score,
        localized_inconsistency_score=localized_inconsistency_score,
    )
    top_blocks = build_top_blocks(np.abs(support_map - np.mean(support_map)), top_k=12)
    overlay_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_sensor_origin_overlay.jpg"
    overlay_path_str = create_sensor_overlay(
        image_bgr=image_bgr,
        score_map=np.abs(support_map - np.mean(support_map)),
        out_path=overlay_path,
        block_size=block_size,
    )
    # Debug prints
    #print("global_trace_strength:", global_trace_strength)
    #print("global_periodicity_strength:", float(np.mean(periodicity_n)) if periodicity_n.size else 0.0)
    #print("block_consistency_score:", block_consistency_score)
    #print("localized_inconsistency_score:", localized_inconsistency_score)
    #print("periodicity_map min/max/mean:", float(np.min(periodicity_n)), float(np.max(periodicity_n)), float(np.mean(periodicity_n)))
    #print("energy_map min/max/mean:", float(np.min(energy_n)), float(np.max(energy_n)), float(np.mean(energy_n)))
    #print("support_map min/max/mean:", float(np.min(support_map)), float(np.max(support_map)), float(np.mean(support_map)))   
    return {
        "sensor_origin": {
            "pattern": {
                "sensor_origin_pattern": pattern,
                "sensor_origin_note": note,
            },
            "scores": {
                "global_trace_strength": global_trace_strength,
                "global_periodicity_strength": float(np.mean(periodicity_n)) if periodicity_n.size else 0.0,
                "block_consistency_score": block_consistency_score,
                "localized_inconsistency_score": localized_inconsistency_score,
            },
            "blockwise": {
                "grid_shape": [int(support_map.shape[0]), int(support_map.shape[1])],
                "score_map": support_map.tolist(),
                "top_blocks": top_blocks,
            },
            "artifacts": {
                "sensor_overlay_path": overlay_path_str,
            },
        }
    }

--- End of vf_sensor_origin.py ---

--- Start of vf_subject_boundary.py ---
# vf_subject_boundary.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
#Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images


from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
import cv2
import numpy as np


def clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, float(x)))

def ensure_bgr(image_path: str | Path) -> np.ndarray:
    img = cv2.imread(str(image_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if img is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Could not read image: {image_path}")
    return img

def ensure_gray(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    return gray.astype(np.uint8)

def normalize_map(m: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    if m.size == 0:
        return m.astype(np.float32)
    m = m.astype(np.float32)
    lo = float(np.min(m))
    hi = float(np.max(m))
    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
        return np.zeros_like(m, dtype=np.float32)
    return (m - lo) / (hi - lo)

def local_std_map(gray: np.ndarray, ksize: int = 9) -> np.ndarray:
    gray_f = gray.astype(np.float32)
    mean = cv2.blur(gray_f, (ksize, ksize))
    mean_sq = cv2.blur(gray_f * gray_f, (ksize, ksize))
    var = np.maximum(mean_sq - mean * mean, 0.0)
    return np.sqrt(var)

def gradient_map(gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    gx = cv2.Sobel(gray.astype(np.float32), cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gy = cv2.Sobel(gray.astype(np.float32), cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    mag = cv2.magnitude(gx, gy)
    return mag

def estimate_sensor_like_map(gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Lightweight local microstructure proxy.
    Not full CFA/PRNU; just a local residual-energy style map.
    """
    gray_f = gray.astype(np.float32)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_f, (0, 0), sigmaX=1.2, sigmaY=1.2)
    resid = gray_f - blur
    sensor_like = cv2.blur(np.abs(resid), (7, 7))
    return sensor_like

def make_boundary_mask(gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    grad = gradient_map(gray)
    grad_n = normalize_map(grad)
    # Strong-edge threshold; tune later if needed
    mask = (grad_n >= 0.35).astype(np.uint8) * 255
    # Clean a bit
    kernel = np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8)
    mask = cv2.morphologyEx(mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel, iterations=1)
    mask = cv2.dilate(mask, kernel, iterations=1)
    return mask

def ring_masks(boundary_mask: np.ndarray, inner_radius: int = 3, outer_radius: int = 9) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    kernel_inner = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (inner_radius * 2 + 1, inner_radius * 2 + 1))
    kernel_outer = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (outer_radius * 2 + 1, outer_radius * 2 + 1))
    dil_inner = cv2.dilate(boundary_mask, kernel_inner)
    dil_outer = cv2.dilate(boundary_mask, kernel_outer)
    inner_ring = cv2.subtract(dil_inner, boundary_mask)
    outer_ring = cv2.subtract(dil_outer, dil_inner)
    return inner_ring, outer_ring

def masked_mean(m: np.ndarray, mask: np.ndarray) -> float:
    vals = m[mask > 0]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.mean(vals))

def color_diff_across_rings(image_bgr: np.ndarray, inner_ring: np.ndarray, outer_ring: np.ndarray) -> float:
    image_f = image_bgr.astype(np.float32)
    inner_vals = image_f[inner_ring > 0]
    outer_vals = image_f[outer_ring > 0]
    if inner_vals.size == 0 or outer_vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    inner_mean = np.mean(inner_vals, axis=0)
    outer_mean = np.mean(outer_vals, axis=0)
    diff = np.linalg.norm(inner_mean - outer_mean)
    # Normalize roughly to 0..1
    return clamp01(diff / 80.0)

def build_top_blocks(score_map: np.ndarray, top_k: int = 12) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows, cols = score_map.shape
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            items.append({
                "row": int(r),
                "col": int(c),
                "score": float(score_map[r, c]),
            })
    items.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
    return items[:top_k]

def block_score_map(gray: np.ndarray, boundary_anomaly_map: np.ndarray, block_size: int = 32) -> np.ndarray:
    h, w = gray.shape
    rows = (h + block_size - 1) // block_size
    cols = (w + block_size - 1) // block_size
    out = np.zeros((rows, cols), dtype=np.float32)
    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            y0 = r * block_size
            x0 = c * block_size
            block = boundary_anomaly_map[y0:y0 + block_size, x0:x0 + block_size]
            if block.size == 0:
                continue
            out[r, c] = float(np.mean(block))
    return out

def create_boundary_overlay(image_bgr: np.ndarray, anomaly_map: np.ndarray, out_path: Path, threshold: float = 0.55,) -> str:
    vis = image_bgr.copy()
    an_n = normalize_map(anomaly_map)
    hot = (an_n >= threshold).astype(np.uint8) * 255
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(hot, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    cv2.drawContours(vis, contours, -1, (0, 0, 255), 2)
    cv2.imwrite(str(out_path), vis)
    return str(out_path)

def interpret_subject_boundary(
    boundary_anomaly_score: float,
    texture_mismatch: float,
    edge_softness_mismatch: float,
    sensor_mismatch: float,
    color_discontinuity: float,
    texture_region_mismatch: float,
    sharpness_region_mismatch: float,
    sensor_region_mismatch: float,
    contrast_region_mismatch: float,
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    strong_local_pair = (
        (texture_mismatch >= 0.40 and sensor_mismatch >= 0.35) or
        (edge_softness_mismatch >= 0.40 and color_discontinuity >= 0.30)
    )
    strong_regime_pair = (
        (texture_region_mismatch >= 0.42 and sharpness_region_mismatch >= 0.35) or
        (sensor_region_mismatch >= 0.35 and contrast_region_mismatch >= 0.30)
    )
    if boundary_anomaly_score >= 0.56 and (strong_local_pair or strong_regime_pair):
        return (
            "possible_subject_background_segmentation_artifact",
            "Boundary-transition analysis indicates a likely subject/background segmentation or replacement artifact, with multiple cues suggesting an implausible cutout-style or background-regime transition."
        )
    if boundary_anomaly_score >= 0.38:
        return (
            "possible_boundary_transition_inconsistency",
            "Boundary-transition analysis detects moderate inconsistency across the subject/background transition, though the evidence is not strong enough for a more specific segmentation-artifact conclusion."
        )
    return (
        "boundary_semantic_inconclusive",
        "Boundary-transition analysis did not reveal a strong subject/background segmentation inconsistency."
    )

def region_masks(boundary_mask: np.ndarray, inner_radius: int = 6, outer_radius: int = 24) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Build broader subject-adjacent inside/outside regions around the boundary.
    inner_region: close to the boundary, intended to sample subject-side structure
    outer_region: broader outside band, intended to sample background-side structure
    """
    kernel_inner = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (inner_radius * 2 + 1, inner_radius * 2 + 1))
    kernel_outer = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (outer_radius * 2 + 1, outer_radius * 2 + 1))
    dil_inner = cv2.dilate(boundary_mask, kernel_inner)
    dil_outer = cv2.dilate(boundary_mask, kernel_outer)
    near_region = cv2.subtract(dil_inner, boundary_mask)
    wide_outer_region = cv2.subtract(dil_outer, dil_inner)
    return near_region, wide_outer_region

def local_contrast_map(gray: np.ndarray, ksize: int = 9) -> np.ndarray:
    gray_f = gray.astype(np.float32)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_f, (0, 0), sigmaX=max(1.0, ksize / 6.0), sigmaY=max(1.0, ksize / 6.0))
    contrast = np.abs(gray_f - blur)
    return contrast

def run_subject_boundary_analysis(image_path: str | Path, output_dir: str | Path, block_size: int = 32,) -> dict[str, Any]:
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    image_bgr = ensure_bgr(image_path)
    gray = ensure_gray(image_bgr)
    
    texture = local_std_map(gray, ksize=9)
    grad = gradient_map(gray)
    sensor_like = estimate_sensor_like_map(gray)
    contrast = local_contrast_map(gray, ksize=9)
    texture_n = normalize_map(texture)
    grad_n = normalize_map(grad)
    sensor_n = normalize_map(sensor_like)
    contrast_n = normalize_map(contrast)
    boundary_mask = make_boundary_mask(gray)
    inner_ring, outer_ring = ring_masks(boundary_mask, inner_radius=3, outer_radius=9)
    near_region, wide_outer_region = region_masks(boundary_mask, inner_radius=6, outer_radius=24)
    texture_in = masked_mean(texture_n, inner_ring)
    texture_out = masked_mean(texture_n, outer_ring)
    texture_mismatch = clamp01(abs(texture_in - texture_out) / 0.20)
    grad_in = masked_mean(grad_n, inner_ring)
    grad_out = masked_mean(grad_n, outer_ring)
    edge_softness_mismatch = clamp01(abs(grad_in - grad_out) / 0.20)
    sensor_in = masked_mean(sensor_n, inner_ring)
    sensor_out = masked_mean(sensor_n, outer_ring)
    sensor_mismatch = clamp01(abs(sensor_in - sensor_out) / 0.18)
    color_discontinuity = color_diff_across_rings(image_bgr, inner_ring, outer_ring)
    texture_near = masked_mean(texture_n, near_region)
    texture_wide = masked_mean(texture_n, wide_outer_region)
    texture_region_mismatch = clamp01(abs(texture_near - texture_wide) / 0.18)
    grad_near = masked_mean(grad_n, near_region)
    grad_wide = masked_mean(grad_n, wide_outer_region)
    sharpness_region_mismatch = clamp01(abs(grad_near - grad_wide) / 0.18)
    sensor_near = masked_mean(sensor_n, near_region)
    sensor_wide = masked_mean(sensor_n, wide_outer_region)
    sensor_region_mismatch = clamp01(abs(sensor_near - sensor_wide) / 0.16)
    contrast_near = masked_mean(contrast_n, near_region)
    contrast_wide = masked_mean(contrast_n, wide_outer_region)
    contrast_region_mismatch = clamp01(abs(contrast_near - contrast_wide) / 0.18)
    boundary_anomaly_score = clamp01(
        0.16 * texture_mismatch +
        0.14 * edge_softness_mismatch +
        0.14 * sensor_mismatch +
        0.12 * color_discontinuity +
        0.16 * texture_region_mismatch +
        0.12 * sharpness_region_mismatch +
        0.10 * sensor_region_mismatch +
        0.06 * contrast_region_mismatch
    )
    # Pixelwise anomaly emphasis near boundaries only
    boundary_zone = cv2.dilate(boundary_mask, cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (11, 11)))
    local_map = (
        0.30 * np.abs(texture_n - cv2.blur(texture_n, (15, 15))) +
        0.25 * np.abs(grad_n - cv2.blur(grad_n, (15, 15))) +
        0.25 * np.abs(sensor_n - cv2.blur(sensor_n, (15, 15)))
    ).astype(np.float32)
    local_map *= (boundary_zone > 0).astype(np.float32)
    local_map_n = normalize_map(local_map)
    block_map = block_score_map(gray, local_map_n, block_size=block_size)
    top_blocks = build_top_blocks(block_map, top_k=12)
    overlay_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_subject_boundary_overlay.jpg"
    overlay_path_str = create_boundary_overlay(image_bgr, local_map_n, overlay_path)
    pattern, note = interpret_subject_boundary(
        boundary_anomaly_score=boundary_anomaly_score,
        texture_mismatch=texture_mismatch,
        edge_softness_mismatch=edge_softness_mismatch,
        sensor_mismatch=sensor_mismatch,
        color_discontinuity=color_discontinuity,
        texture_region_mismatch=texture_region_mismatch,
        sharpness_region_mismatch=sharpness_region_mismatch,
        sensor_region_mismatch=sensor_region_mismatch,
        contrast_region_mismatch=contrast_region_mismatch,
    )
    return {
        "subject_boundary": {
            "pattern": {
                "subject_boundary_pattern": pattern,
                "subject_boundary_note": note,
            },
        "scores": {
            "boundary_anomaly_score": boundary_anomaly_score,
            "texture_mismatch": texture_mismatch,
            "edge_softness_mismatch": edge_softness_mismatch,
            "sensor_mismatch": sensor_mismatch,
            "color_discontinuity": color_discontinuity,
            "texture_region_mismatch": texture_region_mismatch,
            "sharpness_region_mismatch": sharpness_region_mismatch,
            "sensor_region_mismatch": sensor_region_mismatch,
            "contrast_region_mismatch": contrast_region_mismatch,
        },
        "blockwise": {
                "grid_shape": [int(block_map.shape[0]), int(block_map.shape[1])],
                "score_map": block_map.tolist(),
                "top_blocks": top_blocks,
            },
            "artifacts": {
                "subject_boundary_overlay_path": overlay_path_str,
            },
        }
    }
--- End of vf_subject_boundary.py ---

--- Start of vf_subject_material_realism.py ---
#vf_subject_material_realism.py
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any
import cv2
import numpy as np
# local imports
from vf_subject_process_asymmetry import (
    _load_mask,
    _resize_mask,
    _load_bgr_image_exact,
    _validate_region_geometry,
    _sample_region_patches,
    _aggregate_region_metrics,
    _write_overlay,
    _sample_single_region_with_retries
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# helpers 
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _sigmoid(x: float, center: float = 0.5, slope: float = 10.0) -> float:
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-slope * (x - center)))

def _clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    return float(max(0.0, min(1.0, x)))

def _build_subject_variance_heatmap(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    subject_mask: np.ndarray,
    patch_size: int = 32,
    stride: int = 16,
    min_coverage: float = 0.85,
) -> np.ndarray:
    h, w = gray.shape
    heat = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.float32)
    weight = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.float32)
    for y in range(0, h - patch_size + 1, stride):
        for x in range(0, w - patch_size + 1, stride):
            patch_mask = subject_mask[y:y+patch_size, x:x+patch_size]
            coverage = patch_mask.mean()
            if coverage < min_coverage:
                continue
            patch = gray[y:y+patch_size, x:x+patch_size]
            # variance as proxy for microtexture
            val = float(np.var(patch))
            heat[y:y+patch_size, x:x+patch_size] += val
            weight[y:y+patch_size, x:x+patch_size] += 1.0
    weight = np.maximum(weight, 1e-6)
    heat = heat / weight
    # normalize to 0–1
    heat_min = float(heat.min())
    heat_max = float(heat.max())
    if heat_max > heat_min:
        heat = (heat - heat_min) / (heat_max - heat_min)
    else:
        heat[:] = 0.0
    return heat

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# main entry
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def run_subject_material_realism_analysis(
    profile,
    image_path: str | Path,
    subject_mask_path: str | Path,
    output_dir: str | Path,
    analysis_shape_hw: tuple[int, int] | None = None,
    analysis_scale: float | None = None,
    patch_size: int = 32,
    stride: int = 16,
    min_coverage: float = 0.85,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    profile = profile
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if analysis_shape_hw is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "analysis_shape_hw must be provided by the gatekeeper so that "
            "image and subject mask share one explicit analysis geometry."
        )
    image_bgr = _load_bgr_image_exact(image_path, analysis_shape_hw)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    subject_mask_full = _load_mask(subject_mask_path)
    subject_mask = _resize_mask(subject_mask_full, analysis_shape_hw)
    _validate_region_geometry(
        gray=gray,
        subject_mask=subject_mask,
        boundary_mask=None,
        background_mask=None,
        expected_shape_hw=analysis_shape_hw,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # patch sampling
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    sampling_result = _sample_single_region_with_retries(
        gray=gray,
        region_mask=subject_mask,
        max_patches=None,
        sampling_schedule=[
            (patch_size, stride, min_coverage),
            (24, 12, 0.75),
            (16, 8, 0.65),
        ],
        stratify_bins=6,
        seed=42,
        min_required_patches=12,
        debug_label="SMR",
    )
    subject_patches = sampling_result["patches"]
    effective_patch_size = sampling_result["patch_size"]
    effective_stride = sampling_result["stride"]
    effective_min_coverage = sampling_result["min_coverage"]
    sampling_attempt_index = sampling_result["attempt_index"]
    sampling_rescued = bool(sampling_result["sampling_rescued"])
    subject_metrics = _aggregate_region_metrics(subject_patches)
    patch_count = int(subject_metrics["patch_count"])
    if patch_count < 12:
        print(
            "[DEBUG][SMR][LOW_PATCH_FINAL] "
            f"subject={patch_count} | "
            f"patch_size={effective_patch_size} | "
            f"stride={effective_stride} | "
            f"min_coverage={effective_min_coverage:.2f} | "
            f"attempt={sampling_attempt_index} | "
            f"image_shape={gray.shape} | "
            f"mask_subject_sum={int(subject_mask.sum())}"
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # metric extraction (subject-only)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    var_mean = float(subject_metrics["variance_mean"])
    res_mean = float(subject_metrics["residual_energy_mean"])
    ent_mean = float(subject_metrics["entropy_mean"])
    var_std = subject_metrics["variance_std"]
    res_std = subject_metrics["residual_energy_std"]
    ent_std = subject_metrics["entropy_std"]  
    grad_std = float(subject_metrics["gradient_std_mean"])
    grad_axis_bias = float(subject_metrics["grad_axis_bias_mean"])
    # grad_mean = float(subject_metrics["gradient_mean"])
    dct_sim = float(subject_metrics["dct_similarity_mean"])
    dct_sim_std = float(subject_metrics["dct_similarity_std"])

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # score families
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. microtexture suppression
    microtexture_suppression = _clamp01(
        0.4 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (res_mean + 1e-6), center=0.35, slope=6.0) +
        0.3 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (ent_mean + 1e-6), center=0.22, slope=10.0) +
        0.3 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (grad_std + 1e-6), center=0.08, slope=40.0)
    )
    # 2. material homogenization (too stable across patches)
    material_homogenization = _clamp01(
        0.5 * _sigmoid(dct_sim, center=0.85, slope=10.0) +
        0.25 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (var_std + 1e-6), center=0.015, slope=120.0) +
        0.25 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (res_std + 1e-6), center=0.03, slope=80.0)
    )
    # 3. structure without stochasticity
    # Favor regions that still contain organized structure, but where that structure
    # is too directionally stable and too low-noise to read as natural material.
    structure_coherence = _clamp01(
        0.40 * _sigmoid(dct_sim, center=0.84, slope=10.0) +
        0.40 * _sigmoid(grad_axis_bias, center=0.14, slope=14.0) +
        0.20 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (dct_sim_std + 1e-6), center=10.0, slope=0.35)
    )
    stochasticity_suppression = _clamp01(
        0.45 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (res_mean + 1e-6), center=0.35, slope=6.0) +
        0.35 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (ent_mean + 1e-6), center=0.22, slope=10.0) +
        0.20 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (grad_std + 1e-6), center=0.08, slope=40.0)
    )
    structure_ratio = grad_std / (res_mean + 1e-6)
    structure_without_stochasticity = _clamp01(
        0.5 * structure_coherence +
        0.5 * stochasticity_suppression
    )
    # 4. distribution compression (lack of variability)
    # Real subject:      variance_std = high, residual_std = high, entropy_std = high   → distribution_compression ≈ low
    # Synthetic subject: variance_std = low,  residual_std = low,  entropy_std = low    → distribution_compression ≈ high    
    # distribution_compression ≈ global uniformity detector
    # averaging or max-driven logic?
    # consensus logic: “The subject is suspicious if most signals agree” OR anomaly logic “The subject is suspicious if any strong signal exists”    
    averaging = (profile == "normal")
    # print(f"Debug: profile used is {profile}")
    if averaging:      # "all must be compressed"
        distribution_compression = _clamp01(
            0.34 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (subject_metrics["variance_std"] + 1e-6), center=0.015, slope=120.0) +
            0.33 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (subject_metrics["residual_energy_std"] + 1e-6), center=0.03, slope=80.0) +
            0.33 * _sigmoid(1.0 / (subject_metrics["entropy_std"] + 1e-6), center=0.02, slope=100.0)
        )
    else:              # "any strong compression channel"                             
        var_c = _sigmoid(1.0 / (var_std + 1e-6), center=0.002, slope=200.0)
        res_c = _sigmoid(1.0 / (res_std + 1e-6), center=0.15, slope=10.0)
        ent_c = _sigmoid(1.0 / (ent_std + 1e-6), center=1.5, slope=3.0)
        avg_score = 0.34 * var_c + 0.33 * res_c + 0.33 * ent_c
        max_score = max(var_c, res_c, ent_c)
        distribution_compression = _clamp01(0.6 * avg_score + 0.4 * max_score)
    # print(f"Debug: variance_std: {subject_metrics['variance_std']}, residual_energy_std: {subject_metrics['residual_energy_std']}, entropy_std: {subject_metrics['entropy_std']}")
    # print(f"Debug: resulting distribution_compression: {distribution_compression}")
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # final realism score
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # strongest signals for synthetic subject: structure + distribution, when underweighted we mesure 'smoothing' instead of 'material realism'
    subject_material_realism_score = _clamp01(
        0.20 * microtexture_suppression +
        0.20 * material_homogenization +
        0.30 * structure_without_stochasticity +
        0.30 * distribution_compression
    )
    sampling_reliability_factor = 1.0
    if sampling_attempt_index == 2:
        sampling_reliability_factor = 0.85
    elif sampling_attempt_index >= 3:
        sampling_reliability_factor = 0.70
    subject_material_realism_score *= sampling_reliability_factor    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # pattern interpretation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    if sampling_attempt_index >= 3 and patch_count < 24:
        if subject_material_realism_score >= 0.44:
            pattern = "subject_material_realism_inconclusive"
            note = (
                "Subject material analysis became measurable only under aggressive rescue sampling. "
                "The resulting material-regularity signal is treated as contextual and not stable enough "
                "for a stronger realism-failure conclusion."
            )        
    elif subject_material_realism_score >= 0.58:
        pattern = "subject_material_realism_failure_strong"
        note = "Subject exhibits strong material realism failure: textures appear over-regularized, physically implausible, or statistically compressed."
    elif subject_material_realism_score >= 0.44:
        pattern = "subject_material_regularization_dominant"
        note = "Subject shows moderate signs of material regularization or homogenization, consistent with synthetic or heavily processed rendering."
    elif subject_material_realism_score >= 0.30:
        pattern = "subject_structure_without_stochasticity"
        note = "Subject retains structural features but lacks natural stochastic microtexture, suggesting possible synthetic surface modeling."
    elif subject_material_realism_score <= 0.18:
        pattern = "subject_material_realism_supported"
        note = "Subject material statistics are broadly consistent with natural photographic surfaces."
    else:
        pattern = "subject_material_realism_inconclusive"
        note = "Subject material realism remains inconclusive; no strong evidence of either natural or synthetic rendering."

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # overlay (simple variance heatmap proxy)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    heat = _build_subject_variance_heatmap(
        gray=gray,
        subject_mask=subject_mask,
        patch_size=patch_size,
        stride=stride,
        min_coverage=min_coverage,
    )
    overlay_path = output_dir / f"{image_path.stem}_subject_material_realism_overlay.jpg"
    overlay_path_str = _write_overlay(image_bgr, heat, overlay_path)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # debug logging
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    """
    print(
        "[DEBUG][subject_material_realism]",
        {
            "pattern": pattern,
            "score": subject_material_realism_score,
            "microtexture_suppression": microtexture_suppression,
            "material_homogenization": material_homogenization,
            "structure_without_stochasticity": structure_without_stochasticity,
            "distribution_compression": distribution_compression,
            "patch_count": patch_count,
            "variance_mean": var_mean,
            "variance_std": var_std,
            "residual_mean": res_mean,
            "residual_std": res_std,
            "entropy_mean": ent_mean,
            "entropy_std": ent_std,
            "gradient_std": grad_std,
            "grad_axis_bias": grad_axis_bias,
            "dct_similarity": dct_sim,
            "dct_similarity_std": dct_sim_std,
            "structure_coherence": structure_coherence,
            "stochasticity_suppression": stochasticity_suppression,
        }
    )
    """

    def clean_region_metrics(m: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {k: v for k, v in m.items() if not str(k).startswith("_")}

    return {
        "subject_material_realism": {
            "analysis_geometry": {
                "height": int(gray.shape[0]),
                "width": int(gray.shape[1]),
                "analysis_scale": float(analysis_scale) if analysis_scale is not None else None,
                "image_path_used": str(image_path),
            },            
            "pattern": pattern,
            "note": note,
            "scores": {
                "subject_material_realism_score": subject_material_realism_score,
                "microtexture_suppression": microtexture_suppression,
                "material_homogenization": material_homogenization,
                "structure_without_stochasticity": structure_without_stochasticity,
                "distribution_compression": distribution_compression,
            },
            "subject_metrics": clean_region_metrics(subject_metrics),
            "controls": {
                "patch_count": patch_count,
                "effective_patch_size": int(effective_patch_size),
                "effective_stride": int(effective_stride),
                "effective_min_coverage": float(effective_min_coverage),
                "sampling_attempt_index": int(sampling_attempt_index),
                "sampling_rescued": bool(sampling_rescued),
                "sampling_reliability_factor": float(sampling_reliability_factor),
            },
            "artifacts": {
                "subject_input": str(subject_mask_path),
                "subject_material_realism_overlay": overlay_path_str,
            },
        }
    }

--- End of vf_subject_material_realism.py ---

--- Start of vf_subject_process_asymmetry.py ---
#vf_subject_process_asymmetry.py
# Copyright (c) 2025, 2026 Hans De Weme
# Licensed under the MIT License (https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT).
# Part of the SignalLens project evaluating signals of AI-generated and/or AI-enhanced Photo Images
#
# geometry utility owner for the subject-region detector family
#
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple
import math
import cv2
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# shared helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_bgr_image_exact(image_path: str | Path, shape_hw: Tuple[int, int]) -> np.ndarray:
    img = cv2.imread(str(image_path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if img is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load image: {image_path}")
    h, w = shape_hw
    if img.shape[:2] != (h, w):
        img = cv2.resize(img, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    return img

def _resize_mask(mask: np.ndarray, shape_hw: Tuple[int, int]) -> np.ndarray:
    h, w = shape_hw
    if mask.shape == (h, w):
        return mask.astype(np.uint8)
    out = cv2.resize(mask.astype(np.uint8), (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    return (out > 0).astype(np.uint8)

def _validate_region_geometry(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    subject_mask: np.ndarray,
    boundary_mask: np.ndarray | None,
    background_mask: np.ndarray | None,
    expected_shape_hw: Tuple[int, int],
) -> None:
    h, w = expected_shape_hw
    if gray.shape != (h, w):
        raise ValueError(f"Gray shape mismatch: got {gray.shape}, expected {(h, w)}")
    if subject_mask.shape != (h, w):
        raise ValueError(f"Subject mask mismatch: got {subject_mask.shape}, expected {(h, w)}")
    if boundary_mask is not None and boundary_mask.shape != (h, w):
        raise ValueError(f"Boundary mask mismatch: got {boundary_mask.shape}, expected {(h, w)}")
    if background_mask is not None and background_mask.shape != (h, w):
        raise ValueError(f"Background mask mismatch: got {background_mask.shape}, expected {(h, w)}")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# basic helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_mask(path: str | Path) -> np.ndarray:
    m = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if m is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load mask: {path}")
    return (m > 127).astype(np.uint8)

def _safe_float(x: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
    try:
        return float(x)
    except Exception:
        return default

def _normalize_map(m: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    m = m.astype(np.float32)
    mn = float(m.min()) if m.size else 0.0
    mx = float(m.max()) if m.size else 0.0
    if mx - mn < 1e-8:
        return np.zeros_like(m, dtype=np.float32)
    return (m - mn) / (mx - mn)

def _sigmoid(x: float, center: float, slope: float) -> float:
    z = (x - center) * slope
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-z))

def _clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, float(x)))

def _ratio(a: float, b: float, eps: float = 1e-6) -> float:
    return float(a) / float(b + eps)

def _hist_distance(values_a: np.ndarray, values_b: np.ndarray, bins: int = 24) -> float:
    """
    Simple normalized L1 histogram distance in [0, 1].
    """
    if values_a.size < 8 or values_b.size < 8:
        return 0.0
    lo = float(min(values_a.min(), values_b.min()))
    hi = float(max(values_a.max(), values_b.max()))
    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
        return 0.0
    hist_a, _ = np.histogram(values_a, bins=bins, range=(lo, hi), density=True)
    hist_b, _ = np.histogram(values_b, bins=bins, range=(lo, hi), density=True)
    dist = 0.5 * np.sum(np.abs(hist_a - hist_b))
    return _clamp01(dist / max(np.sum(hist_a), 1e-6))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# patch feature extraction
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _patch_entropy_u8(patch_u8: np.ndarray) -> float:
    hist = cv2.calcHist([patch_u8], [0], None, [32], [0, 256]).ravel().astype(np.float32)
    s = hist.sum()
    if s <= 1e-6:
        return 0.0
    p = hist / s
    p = p[p > 1e-9]
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())

def _patch_dct_vector(gray_patch: np.ndarray, keep: int = 4) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Compact low/mid-band DCT vector.
    We intentionally ignore the DC component.
    """
    patch_f = gray_patch.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    dct = cv2.dct(patch_f)
    block = dct[:keep, :keep].copy()
    block[0, 0] = 0.0
    vec = block.flatten()
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vec) + 1e-8
    return (vec / norm).astype(np.float32)

def _extract_patch_features(gray_patch: np.ndarray) -> Dict[str, float | np.ndarray]:
    patch_u8 = gray_patch.astype(np.uint8)
    lap = cv2.Laplacian(gray_patch, cv2.CV_32F, ksize=3)
    gx = cv2.Sobel(gray_patch, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gy = cv2.Sobel(gray_patch, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    grad_mag = np.sqrt(gx * gx + gy * gy)
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_patch, (0, 0), 1.2)
    residual = gray_patch.astype(np.float32) - blur.astype(np.float32)
    variance = float(np.var(gray_patch))
    lap_energy = float(np.mean(np.abs(lap)))
    grad_mean = float(np.mean(grad_mag))
    grad_std = float(np.std(grad_mag))
    residual_energy = float(np.mean(np.abs(residual)))
    entropy = _patch_entropy_u8(patch_u8)
    # directional gradient bias: high value means orientation is overly stable
    gx_abs = float(np.mean(np.abs(gx)))
    gy_abs = float(np.mean(np.abs(gy)))
    grad_axis_bias = abs(gx_abs - gy_abs) / (gx_abs + gy_abs + 1e-6)
    dct_vec = _patch_dct_vector(gray_patch, keep=4)
    return {
        "variance": variance,
        "laplacian_energy": lap_energy,
        "gradient_mean": grad_mean,
        "gradient_std": grad_std,
        "residual_energy": residual_energy,
        "entropy": entropy,
        "grad_axis_bias": float(grad_axis_bias),
        "dct_vec": dct_vec,
    }


@dataclass
class PatchCandidate:
    x: int
    y: int
    coverage: float
    bin_id: tuple[int, int]


def _collect_region_patch_candidates(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    region_mask: np.ndarray,
    patch_size: int = 32,
    stride: int = 16,
    min_coverage: float = 0.85,
    stratify_bins: int = 6,
) -> List[PatchCandidate]:
    h, w = gray.shape[:2]
    candidates: List[PatchCandidate] = []

    max_y = max(1, h - patch_size + 1)
    max_x = max(1, w - patch_size + 1)

    for y in range(0, h - patch_size + 1, stride):
        for x in range(0, w - patch_size + 1, stride):
            m = region_mask[y:y + patch_size, x:x + patch_size]
            coverage = float(m.mean())
            if coverage < min_coverage:
                continue

            # spatial bin for stratified sampling
            by = min(stratify_bins - 1, int((y / max_y) * stratify_bins))
            bx = min(stratify_bins - 1, int((x / max_x) * stratify_bins))

            candidates.append(
                PatchCandidate(
                    x=x,
                    y=y,
                    coverage=coverage,
                    bin_id=(by, bx),
                )
            )
    return candidates


def _select_stratified_candidates(
    candidates: List[PatchCandidate],
    max_patches: int | None = None,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> List[PatchCandidate]:
    if max_patches is None or len(candidates) <= max_patches:
        return candidates

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # group by bin
    bins: Dict[tuple[int, int], List[PatchCandidate]] = {}
    for c in candidates:
        bins.setdefault(c.bin_id, []).append(c)

    # sort each bin by coverage descending so very good patches are preferred
    for bin_list in bins.values():
        bin_list.sort(key=lambda c: c.coverage, reverse=True)

    bin_keys = list(bins.keys())
    selected: List[PatchCandidate] = []

    # first pass: guarantee spatial spread
    for bk in bin_keys:
        if len(selected) >= max_patches:
            break
        if bins[bk]:
            selected.append(bins[bk].pop(0))

    if len(selected) >= max_patches:
        return selected[:max_patches]

    # second pass: weighted fill from remaining candidates
    remaining: List[PatchCandidate] = []
    for bin_list in bins.values():
        remaining.extend(bin_list)

    if not remaining:
        return selected

    # slightly favor high-coverage patches but still allow diversity
    weights = np.array([0.25 + c.coverage for c in remaining], dtype=np.float64)
    weights = weights / weights.sum()

    need = max_patches - len(selected)
    if need >= len(remaining):
        selected.extend(remaining)
        return selected

    idx = rng.choice(len(remaining), size=need, replace=False, p=weights)
    selected.extend([remaining[i] for i in idx])
    return selected


def _sample_region_patches(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    region_mask: np.ndarray,
    patch_size: int = 32,
    stride: int = 16,
    min_coverage: float = 0.85,
    max_patches: int | None = None,
    stratify_bins: int = 6,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> List[Dict[str, float | np.ndarray]]:
    candidates = _collect_region_patch_candidates(
        gray=gray,
        region_mask=region_mask,
        patch_size=patch_size,
        stride=stride,
        min_coverage=min_coverage,
        stratify_bins=stratify_bins,
    )

    selected = _select_stratified_candidates(
        candidates=candidates,
        max_patches=max_patches,
        seed=seed,
    )
    features: List[Dict[str, float | np.ndarray]] = []
    for c in selected:
        patch = gray[c.y:c.y + patch_size, c.x:c.x + patch_size]
        f = _extract_patch_features(patch)
        f["x"] = float(c.x)
        f["y"] = float(c.y)
        f["coverage"] = float(c.coverage)
        features.append(f)
    return features

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# aggregation
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _aggregate_region_metrics(patches: List[Dict[str, float | np.ndarray]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not patches:
        empty_arr = np.zeros((0,), dtype=np.float32)
        return {
            "patch_count": 0,
            "variance_mean": 0.0,
            "variance_std": 0.0,
            "laplacian_energy_mean": 0.0,
            "gradient_mean_mean": 0.0,
            "gradient_std_mean": 0.0,
            "residual_energy_mean": 0.0,
            "residual_energy_std": 0.0,
            "entropy_mean": 0.0,
            "entropy_std": 0.0,
            "grad_axis_bias_mean": 0.0,
            "dct_similarity_mean": 0.0,
            "dct_similarity_p90": 0.0,
            "dct_similarity_std": 0.0,
            "_variance_values": empty_arr,
            "_lap_values": empty_arr,
            "_grad_values": empty_arr,
            "_residual_values": empty_arr,
            "_entropy_values": empty_arr,
            "_dct_sim_values": empty_arr,
        }

    def arr(key: str) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([_safe_float(p.get(key, 0.0)) for p in patches], dtype=np.float32)
    
    variance = arr("variance")
    lap_energy = arr("laplacian_energy")
    grad_mean = arr("gradient_mean")
    grad_std = arr("gradient_std")
    residual_energy = arr("residual_energy")
    entropy = arr("entropy")
    grad_axis_bias = arr("grad_axis_bias")
    dct_vecs = np.stack([p["dct_vec"] for p in patches], axis=0).astype(np.float32)
    dct_sims: List[float] = []
    if len(dct_vecs) >= 2:
        ref = dct_vecs.mean(axis=0)
        ref_norm = np.linalg.norm(ref) + 1e-8
        ref = ref / ref_norm
        for v in dct_vecs:
            sim = float(np.clip(np.dot(v, ref), -1.0, 1.0))
            dct_sims.append(sim)
    dct_sims_a = np.array(dct_sims, dtype=np.float32) if dct_sims else np.zeros((0,), dtype=np.float32)
    return {
        "patch_count": int(len(patches)),
        "variance_mean": float(variance.mean()),
        "variance_std": float(variance.std()),
        "laplacian_energy_mean": float(lap_energy.mean()),
        "gradient_mean_mean": float(grad_mean.mean()),
        "gradient_std_mean": float(grad_std.mean()),
        "residual_energy_mean": float(residual_energy.mean()),
        "residual_energy_std": float(residual_energy.std()),
        "entropy_mean": float(entropy.mean()),
        "entropy_std": float(entropy.std()),
        "grad_axis_bias_mean": float(grad_axis_bias.mean()),
        "dct_similarity_mean": float(dct_sims_a.mean()) if dct_sims_a.size else 0.0,
        "dct_similarity_p90": float(np.percentile(dct_sims_a, 90)) if dct_sims_a.size else 0.0,
        "dct_similarity_std": float(dct_sims_a.std()) if dct_sims_a.size else 0.0,
        # raw arrays for comparative features
        "_variance_values": variance,
        "_lap_values": lap_energy,
        "_grad_values": grad_mean,
        "_residual_values": residual_energy,
        "_entropy_values": entropy,
        "_dct_sim_values": dct_sims_a,
    }

#==================================================================
# flexible version with retries
#===================================================================
def _sample_regions_with_retries(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    subject_mask: np.ndarray,
    boundary_mask: np.ndarray,
    background_mask: np.ndarray,
    subject_budget: int,
    boundary_budget: int,
    background_budget: int,
    sampling_schedule: list[tuple[int, int, float]] | None = None,
    min_subject_patches: int = 12,
    min_boundary_patches: int = 4,
    min_background_patches: int = 12,
    debug: bool = False,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Progressive retry sampler for subject/boundary/background regions.
    Tries progressively smaller patches and lower coverage thresholds until
    enough support is available for the key regions.
    """
    
    if sampling_schedule is None:
        sampling_schedule = [
            (32, 16, 0.85),
            (24, 12, 0.75),
            (16, 8, 0.65),
        ]

    best = None
    for attempt_idx, (patch_size, stride, min_coverage) in enumerate(sampling_schedule, start=1):
        subject_patches = _sample_region_patches(
            gray,
            subject_mask,
            patch_size=patch_size,
            stride=stride,
            min_coverage=min_coverage,
            max_patches=subject_budget,
            stratify_bins=6,
            seed=42,
        )
        boundary_patches = _sample_region_patches(
            gray,
            boundary_mask,
            patch_size=patch_size,
            stride=stride,
            min_coverage=min_coverage,
            max_patches=boundary_budget,
            stratify_bins=6,
            seed=43,
        )
        background_patches = _sample_region_patches(
            gray,
            background_mask,
            patch_size=patch_size,
            stride=stride,
            min_coverage=min_coverage,
            max_patches=background_budget,
            stratify_bins=8,
            seed=44,
        )
        subject_count = len(subject_patches)
        boundary_count = len(boundary_patches)
        background_count = len(background_patches)
        if debug and (attempt_idx > 1):
            print(
                "[DEBUG][SPA][SAMPLING_ATTEMPT] "
                f"attempt={attempt_idx} | "
                f"patch_size={patch_size} | stride={stride} | min_coverage={min_coverage:.2f} | "
                f"subject={subject_count} | boundary={boundary_count} | background={background_count}"
            )
        attempt_result = {
            "subject_patches": subject_patches,
            "boundary_patches": boundary_patches,
            "background_patches": background_patches,
            "patch_size": patch_size,
            "stride": stride,
            "min_coverage": min_coverage,
            "attempt_index": attempt_idx,
            "subject_count": subject_count,
            "boundary_count": boundary_count,
            "background_count": background_count,
        }
        # Keep the best attempt seen so far, even if it does not fully satisfy thresholds.
        if best is None:
            best = attempt_result
        else:
            prev_score = (
                best["subject_count"] >= min_subject_patches,
                best["background_count"] >= min_background_patches,
                best["boundary_count"] >= min_boundary_patches,
                best["subject_count"] + best["boundary_count"] + best["background_count"],
            )
            new_score = (
                subject_count >= min_subject_patches,
                background_count >= min_background_patches,
                boundary_count >= min_boundary_patches,
                subject_count + boundary_count + background_count,
            )
            if new_score > prev_score:
                best = attempt_result
        # Success condition: subject + background are usable; boundary is allowed to be thinner.
        if (
            subject_count >= min_subject_patches
            and background_count >= min_background_patches
            and boundary_count >= min_boundary_patches
        ):
            return attempt_result
    return best

#=============================================================
# try resue resampling for subject region only 
#=============================================================
def _sample_single_region_with_retries(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    region_mask: np.ndarray,
    max_patches: int | None = None,
    sampling_schedule: list[tuple[int, int, float]] | None = None,
    stratify_bins: int = 6,
    seed: int = 42,
    min_required_patches: int = 12,
    debug_label: str | None = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Progressive retry sampler for a single region.
    Tries progressively smaller patches and lower coverage thresholds until
    enough support is available. Returns the best attempt even if thresholds
    are never fully met.
    """
    if sampling_schedule is None:
        sampling_schedule = [
            (32, 16, 0.85),
            (24, 12, 0.75),
            (16, 8, 0.65),
        ]

    best_attempt: Dict[str, Any] | None = None
    for attempt_index, (patch_size, stride, min_coverage) in enumerate(sampling_schedule, start=1):
        patches = _sample_region_patches(
            gray=gray,
            region_mask=region_mask,
            patch_size=patch_size,
            stride=stride,
            min_coverage=min_coverage,
            max_patches=max_patches,
            stratify_bins=stratify_bins,
            seed=seed,
        )
        patch_count = len(patches)
        if debug_label and (attempt_index > 1):
            print(
                f"[DEBUG][{debug_label}][SAMPLING_ATTEMPT] "
                f"attempt={attempt_index} | "
                f"patch_size={patch_size} | "
                f"stride={stride} | "
                f"min_coverage={min_coverage:.2f} | "
                f"patches={patch_count}"
            )
        attempt = {
            "patches": patches,
            "patch_count": patch_count,
            "patch_size": patch_size,
            "stride": stride,
            "min_coverage": min_coverage,
            "attempt_index": attempt_index,
            "sampling_rescued": attempt_index > 1,
        }
        if best_attempt is None or patch_count > best_attempt["patch_count"]:
            best_attempt = attempt
        if patch_count >= min_required_patches:
            return attempt
    return best_attempt if best_attempt is not None else {
        "patches": [],
        "patch_count": 0,
        "patch_size": sampling_schedule[-1][0],
        "stride": sampling_schedule[-1][1],
        "min_coverage": sampling_schedule[-1][2],
        "attempt_index": len(sampling_schedule),
        "sampling_rescued": len(sampling_schedule) > 1,
    }


def _comparative_features(
    subject_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    boundary_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    background_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    sv = subject_metrics
    bv = boundary_metrics
    bg = background_metrics
    # Lower subject values vs background often imply stronger subject regularization.
    variance_ratio = _ratio(sv["variance_mean"], bg["variance_mean"])
    lap_ratio = _ratio(sv["laplacian_energy_mean"], bg["laplacian_energy_mean"])
    residual_ratio = _ratio(sv["residual_energy_mean"], bg["residual_energy_mean"])
    entropy_ratio = _ratio(sv["entropy_mean"], bg["entropy_mean"])
    grad_std_ratio = _ratio(sv["gradient_std_mean"], bg["gradient_std_mean"])
    dct_similarity_gap = float(sv["dct_similarity_mean"] - bg["dct_similarity_mean"])
    boundary_subject_entropy_gap = float(bv["entropy_mean"] - sv["entropy_mean"])
    boundary_background_entropy_gap = float(bv["entropy_mean"] - bg["entropy_mean"])
    boundary_subject_residual_gap = float(bv["residual_energy_mean"] - sv["residual_energy_mean"])
    boundary_background_residual_gap = float(bv["residual_energy_mean"] - bg["residual_energy_mean"])
    variance_hist_distance = _hist_distance(sv["_variance_values"], bg["_variance_values"])
    residual_hist_distance = _hist_distance(sv["_residual_values"], bg["_residual_values"])
    entropy_hist_distance = _hist_distance(sv["_entropy_values"], bg["_entropy_values"])
    grad_hist_distance = _hist_distance(sv["_grad_values"], bg["_grad_values"])
    dct_hist_distance = _hist_distance(sv["_dct_sim_values"], bg["_dct_sim_values"]) if (
        sv["_dct_sim_values"].size >= 4 and bg["_dct_sim_values"].size >= 4
    ) else 0.0
    return {
        "subject_bg_variance_ratio": float(variance_ratio),
        "subject_bg_laplacian_ratio": float(lap_ratio),
        "subject_bg_residual_ratio": float(residual_ratio),
        "subject_bg_entropy_ratio": float(entropy_ratio),
        "subject_bg_gradient_std_ratio": float(grad_std_ratio),
        "subject_bg_dct_similarity_gap": float(dct_similarity_gap),
        "variance_hist_distance": float(variance_hist_distance),
        "residual_hist_distance": float(residual_hist_distance),
        "entropy_hist_distance": float(entropy_hist_distance),
        "gradient_hist_distance": float(grad_hist_distance),
        "dct_hist_distance": float(dct_hist_distance),
        "boundary_subject_entropy_gap": float(boundary_subject_entropy_gap),
        "boundary_background_entropy_gap": float(boundary_background_entropy_gap),
        "boundary_subject_residual_gap": float(boundary_subject_residual_gap),
        "boundary_background_residual_gap": float(boundary_background_residual_gap),
    }

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# scoring
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def _compute_baseline_reliability(
    subject_count: int,
    boundary_count: int,
    background_count: int,
    subject_texture_payload: Dict[str, Any] | None,
) -> Tuple[float, Dict[str, Any]]:
    penalties = 0.0
    controls: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "background_is_highly_repetitive": False,
        "valid_subject_patch_count": int(subject_count),
        "valid_boundary_patch_count": int(boundary_count),
        "valid_background_patch_count": int(background_count),
    }
    if subject_count < 20:
        penalties += 0.25
    if background_count < 20:
        penalties += 0.30
    if boundary_count < 8:
        penalties += 0.10
    if subject_texture_payload:
        block = subject_texture_payload.get("subject_texture_consistency", {})
        scores = block.get("scores", {})
        bg_high = bool(scores.get("background_is_highly_repetitive", False))
        controls["background_is_highly_repetitive"] = bg_high
        if bg_high:
            penalties += 0.25
        bg_soft = _safe_float(scores.get("bg_soft_recurrence_score", 0.0))
        if bg_soft >= 0.90:
            penalties += 0.15
    return _clamp01(1.0 - penalties), controls

def _compute_scores(
    subject_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    boundary_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    background_metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    features: Dict[str, float],
    baseline_reliability_score: float,
    subject_texture_payload: Dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    region_fft_payload: Dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    # regularization dominance:
    # lower subject microtexture / entropy / residual than background,
    # plus stronger subject DCT stability.
    variance_dom = _sigmoid(1.0 - features["subject_bg_variance_ratio"], center=0.10, slope=8.0)
    residual_dom = _sigmoid(1.0 - features["subject_bg_residual_ratio"], center=0.08, slope=8.0)
    entropy_dom = _sigmoid(1.0 - features["subject_bg_entropy_ratio"], center=0.05, slope=8.0)
    grad_dom = _sigmoid(1.0 - features["subject_bg_gradient_std_ratio"], center=0.08, slope=8.0)
    dct_dom = _sigmoid(features["subject_bg_dct_similarity_gap"], center=0.03, slope=10.0)
    regularization_dominance_score = _clamp01(
        0.24 * variance_dom +
        0.22 * residual_dom +
        0.20 * entropy_dom +
        0.14 * grad_dom +
        0.20 * dct_dom
    )
    # distribution divergence:
    process_divergence_score = _clamp01(
        0.24 * features["variance_hist_distance"] +
        0.24 * features["residual_hist_distance"] +
        0.20 * features["entropy_hist_distance"] +
        0.14 * features["gradient_hist_distance"] +
        0.18 * features["dct_hist_distance"]
    )
    # boundary paradox:
    # boundary roughly tracks background more than subject interior.
    entropy_paradox = _sigmoid(
        abs(features["boundary_subject_entropy_gap"]) - abs(features["boundary_background_entropy_gap"]),
        center=0.05,
        slope=8.0,
    )
    residual_paradox = _sigmoid(
        abs(features["boundary_subject_residual_gap"]) - abs(features["boundary_background_residual_gap"]),
        center=0.75,
        slope=0.8,
    )
    boundary_paradox_score = _clamp01(0.55 * entropy_paradox + 0.45 * residual_paradox)
    # small carry-forward stabilizers
    carry_forward = 0.0
    if subject_texture_payload:
        block = subject_texture_payload.get("subject_texture_consistency", {})
        scores = block.get("scores", {})
        subject_bg_soft_gap = _safe_float(scores.get("subject_background_soft_gap", 0.0))
        carry_forward += 0.08 * _sigmoid(subject_bg_soft_gap, center=0.05, slope=8.0)
    if region_fft_payload:
        block = region_fft_payload.get("region_fft", {})
        comp = block.get("comparative_features", {})
        fft_gap = _safe_float(comp.get("subject_vs_background_fft_gap", 0.0))
        carry_forward += 0.06 * _sigmoid(fft_gap, center=0.03, slope=10.0)
    asymmetry_score = _clamp01(
        (
            0.45 * regularization_dominance_score +
            0.40 * process_divergence_score +
            0.15 * boundary_paradox_score +
            carry_forward
        ) * baseline_reliability_score
    )
    return {
        "regularization_dominance_score": float(regularization_dominance_score),
        "process_divergence_score": float(process_divergence_score),
        "boundary_paradox_score": float(boundary_paradox_score),
        "baseline_reliability_score": float(baseline_reliability_score),
        "asymmetry_score": float(asymmetry_score),
    }

def _interpret_pattern(scores: Dict[str, float], controls: Dict[str, Any], ) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    asym = scores["asymmetry_score"]
    reg = scores["regularization_dominance_score"]
    div = scores["process_divergence_score"]
    par = scores["boundary_paradox_score"]
    rel = scores["baseline_reliability_score"]
    rev = float(scores.get("reverse_asymmetry_score", 0.0) or 0.0)
    bg_high = bool(controls.get("background_is_highly_repetitive", False))
    subj_n = int(controls.get("valid_subject_patch_count", 0))
    bg_n = int(controls.get("valid_background_patch_count", 0))
    # damping if recue resampling was needed
    rescued = bool(scores.get("sampling_rescued", False))
    attempt = int(scores.get("sampling_attempt_index", 1))
    if controls.get("region_geometry_unreliable"):
        return (
            "subject_process_asymmetry_support_unreliable",
            "Subject/background process asymmetry analysis was not considered stable because the subject-region support is too small or unreliable. "
            "The result should not be interpreted as strong subject-level asymmetry.",
        )        
    if rescued and attempt >= 3 and asym >= 0.52:
        return (
            "subject_process_asymmetry_inconclusive",
            "Subject/background asymmetry became measurable only under aggressive rescue sampling. "
            "The result is therefore treated as contextual and not stable enough for a stronger asymmetry interpretation.",
        )        
    if subj_n < 12 or bg_n < 12:
        return (
            "subject_process_asymmetry_inconclusive",
            "Subject/background asymmetry analysis completed, but the available region support is too limited for a stable reading.",
        )
    if rel < 0.45:
        return (
            "subject_process_asymmetry_inconclusive",
            "Some subject/background process asymmetry is present, but the comparison baseline is too unreliable for a stable interpretation.",
        )
    # Strong asymmetry can arise from either: 1. subject smoother (reg) or  2. subject more detailed (reverse)
    if asym >= 0.68 and ( (reg >= 0.50 and div >= 0.50) or (rev >= 0.65 and div >= 0.50) ):
        return (
            "subject_process_asymmetry_strong",
            "Strong subject-vs-background process asymmetry detected. The subject interior appears more regularized and distributionally distinct than the surrounding scene.",
        )
